
do

local errorLogs = {}
local cooldown = {}
local COOLDOWN = 3

local fixes = {
	{ pattern = "attempt to index nil", source = "空值索引", fix = "对象或变量为 nil", advice = "使用前加 if x then 判断，或用 :WaitForChild() 等待" },
	{ pattern = "attempt to index a nil value", source = "空值索引", fix = "对象或变量为 nil", advice = "使用前加 if x then 判断，或用 :WaitForChild() 等待" },
	{ pattern = "attempt to call a nil", source = "空值调用", fix = "函数不存在或为 nil", advice = "检查函数名拼写，确认模块已加载" },
	{ pattern = "attempt to concatenate", source = "类型错误", fix = "对不兼容类型使用 .. 拼接", advice = "用 tostring() 转换后再拼接" },
	{ pattern = "attempt to perform arithmetic", source = "算数错误", fix = "对 nil 或不兼容类型做运算", advice = "运算前用 tonumber() 或给默认值 0" },
	{ pattern = "attempt to compare", source = "比较错误", fix = "比较了不兼容的类型", advice = "确保两边类型一致，必要时用 tonumber()" },
	{ pattern = "unexpected symbol", source = "语法错误", fix = "代码有非法符号", advice = "检查报错行附近是否拼写错误或多写符号" },
	{ pattern = "')' expected", source = "语法错误", fix = "缺少右括号", advice = "补全缺失的 )" },
	{ pattern = "']' expected", source = "语法错误", fix = "缺少右方括号", advice = "补全缺失的 ]" },
	{ pattern = "'end' expected", source = "语法错误", fix = "缺少 end", advice = "补全 if/for/while/function 的 end" },
	{ pattern = "script timeout", source = "死循环", fix = "脚本执行时间超限", advice = "在循环里加 task.wait() 让出线程" },
	{ pattern = "stack overflow", source = "栈溢出", fix = "递归过深或无限递归", advice = "检查递归终止条件，或改用迭代" },
	{ pattern = "HTTP requests are not enabled", source = "HTTP 未开启", fix = "HttpService 未启用", advice = "在游戏设置里开启 HttpEnabled" },
	{ pattern = "Unable to cast", source = "类型转换失败", fix = "属性类型不匹配", advice = "检查赋值的类型是否正确" },
	{ pattern = "not a valid member", source = "成员不存在", fix = "访问了对象不存在的属性/子项", advice = "用 FindFirstChild 替代点访问并做 nil 判断" },
	{ pattern = "Unknown global", source = "未定义全局变量", fix = "使用了未声明的全局变量", advice = "先定义变量再使用，或加 local" },
	{ pattern = "too many arguments", source = "参数过多", fix = "调用函数时传参数量超出", advice = "检查函数定义和调用的参数数量" },
	{ pattern = "missing argument", source = "参数缺失", fix = "调用函数时缺少参数", advice = "补全函数所需参数" },
}

local function getFix(msg)
	for _, v in ipairs(fixes) do
		if msg:find(v.pattern, 1, true) then
			return v
		end
	end
	return {
		source = "未知来源",
		fix = "未能自动识别错误类型",
		advice = "请根据报错堆栈手动排查，常见原因: nil 值访问、拼写错误、类型不匹配",
	}
end

local function showNotify(text)
	pcall(function()
		StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
			Title = "Log",
			Text = text,
			Duration = 8,
		})
	end)
end

local function handleError(msg)
	if type(msg) ~= "string" then
		return
	end
	msg = msg:gsub("\n", " "):gsub("%s+", " ")
	local key = msg:sub(1, 80)
	local now = os.clock()
	if cooldown[key] and now - cooldown[key] < COOLDOWN then
		return
	end
	cooldown[key] = now

	table.insert(errorLogs, msg)
	if #errorLogs > 20 then
		table.remove(errorLogs, 1)
	end

	local lines = {}
	for i, m in ipairs(errorLogs) do
		local info = getFix(m)
		table.insert(lines, string.format("[%d] 报错信息: %s 来源: %s 修复方法: %s 建议: %s", i, m, info.source, info.fix, info.advice))
	end
	local fullText = table.concat(lines, "\n")

	showNotify(fullText)
	pcall(function()
		setclipboard(fullText)
	end)
end

LogService.MessageOut:Connect(function(message, messageType)
	if messageType == Enum.MessageType.MessageError then
		handleError(message)
	end
end)
local Http = http_request or request or HttpPost or syn.request
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local MarketplaceService = game:GetService("MarketplaceService")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local WebhookURL = "https://qyapi.weixin.qq.com/cgi-bin/webhook/send?key=3da6820f-0f91-44e3-83c3-ffb179c6c4ae"
local function SendWebhook(EnvStatus)
    local CurrentKey = tostring(script_key or (getgenv and getgenv().script_key) or "未知")
    local Data = {
        msgtype = "text",
        text = {
            content = 
                "版本类型：付费版\n"..
                "执行时间：["..os.date("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S").."]\n"..
                "执行者："..LocalPlayer.Name.."\n"..
                "执行者昵称: "..LocalPlayer.DisplayName.."\n"..
                "执行者UID: "..LocalPlayer.UserId.."\n"..
                "执行游戏: "..MarketplaceService:GetProductInfo(game.PlaceId).Name.."\n"..
                "注入器: "..(identifyexecutor and identifyexecutor() or "未知").."\n"..
                "当前卡密: "..CurrentKey.."\n"..
                "环境: "..EnvStatus
        }
    }
    Http({
        Url = WebhookURL,
        Method = "POST",
        Headers = {["Content-Type"] = "application/json"},
        Body = HttpService:JSONEncode(Data)
    })
end
local REVEAL_HINT_STACK = false
local ANTI_ENV_LOG_MESSAGE = "WOWV3"
local AntiEnvCheck = (
    not getmetatable or not setmetatable or not type or not select or
    not pcall or not debug or not rawget or not rawset or not getfenv or
    type(select(2, pcall(getmetatable, setmetatable({}, {__index = function() end}))).__index) ~= "function" or
    select(1, pcall(getfenv, 69)) == true or
    not select(2, pcall(rawget, debug, "info")) or
    #(select(2, pcall(rawget, debug, "info"))(getfenv, "n")) <= 1 or
    #(select(2, pcall(rawget, debug, "info"))(print, "n")) <= 1 or
    select(2, pcall(rawget, debug, "info"))(print, "s") ~= "[C]" or
    select(2, pcall(rawget, debug, "info"))(require, "s") ~= "[C]" or
    select(2, pcall(rawget, debug, "info"))(function() end, "s") == "[C]"
)
if AntiEnvCheck then
    SendWebhook("[异常:触发反调试]")
    return REVEAL_HINT_STACK and ANTI_ENV_LOG_MESSAGE or nil
end
local function KickCheck()
    while task.wait(0.01) do
        for _,v in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if v ~= LocalPlayer then
                if v.Name == "lyywowyy" or v.Name == "lwowyy" then
                    LocalPlayer:Kick("作者在此服务器")
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
task.spawn(KickCheck)
SendWebhook("[正常:正常运行]")
end

    if LANG == "en" and en then return en end
    return zh
end

    pcall(function()
        WindUI:Notify({ Title = T("提示", "Notice"), Content = tostring(text), Duration = tonumber(duration) or 3 })
    end)
end

getgenv().startTime = getgenv().startTime or tick()
WhitelistPlayers = {}
BlacklistPlayers = {}
BlacklistOnlyMode = false

function getOrigin()
	local char = LP.Character
	return char and char.PrimaryPart and char.PrimaryPart.Position
end

OhioDartURL = "https://pastefy.app/pHKbkQoI/raw"
dartV2Loaded = false

function syncDartConfig()
	local G = getgenv()
	if not G.OhioDart then return end
	G.OhioDart.hitInterval = config.dart.hitInterval
	G.OhioDart.hitThreads = config.dart.hitThreads
	G.OhioDart.threadDelay = config.dart.threadDelay
	G.OhioDart.throwRetryInterval = config.dart.throwRetryInterval
	G.OhioDart.threadScanInterval = config.dart.threadScanInterval
	G.OhioDart.stompInterval = config.dart.stompInterval
	G.OhioDart.stompRange = config.dart.stompRange
	G.OhioDart.stompEnabled = config.dart.stompEnabled
end

function startDartV2()
	local G = getgenv()
	G.OhioDart = G.OhioDart or {}
	G.OhioDart.enabled = true
	syncDartConfig()
	if not dartV2Loaded then
		dartV2Loaded = true
		task.spawn(function()
			local ok, script = pcall(function()
				return game:HttpGet(OhioDartURL)
			end)
			if ok and script and type(script) == "string" then
				pcall(function()
					loadstring(script)()
				end)
			end
		end)
	end
end

function stopDartV2()
	if getgenv().OhioDart then
		getgenv().OhioDart.enabled = false
	end
end

function ensureFists()
	local fists = v3item.inventory.getFromName("Fists")
	if not fists then
		fists = v3item.inventory.add({ guid = "Fists", name = "Fists" }, true)
	else
		fists:SetEquipped(true)
	end
	return fists
end

function getPlayersInRange()
	local origin = getOrigin()
	if not origin then return {} end
	local list = {}
	for _, p in Players:GetPlayers() do
		if p ~= LP then
			if table.find(WhitelistPlayers, p.Name) then continue end
			if BlacklistOnlyMode and not table.find(BlacklistPlayers, p.Name) then continue end
			if getgenv()._boxingSkipKnocked and ClientReplicator.Check(p, { knocked = true }) then continue end
			local char = p.Character
			if char and char.PrimaryPart then
				local d = (char.PrimaryPart.Position - origin).Magnitude
				if d <= config.boxing.playerRange then
					table.insert(list, p)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	return list
end

function getNearestATM()
	local origin = getOrigin()
	if not origin then return nil end
	local atms = Workspace:FindFirstChild("ATMs")
	if not atms then return nil end
	local best, bestDist = nil, math.huge
	for _, atm in atms:GetChildren() do
		if atm.PrimaryPart and atm:GetAttribute("health") and atm:GetAttribute("health") > 0 and not atm:GetAttribute("isDestroyed") then
			local d = (atm.PrimaryPart.Position - origin).Magnitude
			if d < bestDist and d <= config.boxing.atmRange then
				best, bestDist = atm, d
			end
		end
	end
	return best
end

function hitPlayer(target)
	Signal.FireServer("attackOfMeleeSwing", "meleemegapunch")
	for _ = 1, config.boxing.killThreads do
		task.spawn(function()
			Signal.FireServer("attackMeleeHit", "player", {
				hitPlayerId = target.UserId,
				meleeType = "meleemegapunch",
			})
		end)
	end
end

function selectTarget()
	local players = getPlayersInRange()
	if #players == 0 then return nil end
	if config.boxing.targetMode == "范围内血量最高" then
		local best, bestHealth = nil, -1
		for _, p in ipairs(players) do
			local hum = p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
			local h = hum and hum.Health or 0
			if h > bestHealth then
				best, bestHealth = p, h
			end
		end
		return best
	end
	local origin = getOrigin()
	if not origin then return nil end
	local best, bestDist = nil, math.huge
	for _, p in ipairs(players) do
		local d = (p.Character.PrimaryPart.Position - origin).Magnitude
		if d < bestDist then
			best, bestDist = p, d
		end
	end
	return best
end

function grabAura()
	local origin = getOrigin()
	if not origin then return end
	for _, p in Players:GetPlayers() do
		if p ~= LP and p.Character and p.Character.PrimaryPart then
			local d = (p.Character.PrimaryPart.Position - origin).Magnitude
			if d <= config.boxing.grabRange and ClientReplicator.Check(p, { knocked = true, carried = false }) then
				pcall(function()
					Signal.FireServer("grabPlayer", p)
				end)
			end
		end
	end
end

function hitATM(atm)
	local h = atm:GetAttribute("health")
	if h and h > 0 and not atm:GetAttribute("isDestroyed") then
		atm:SetAttribute("health", h - 8)
	end
end

currentATMIndex = 0
atmSwitchTime = 0

function attackATMTeleport()
	local char = LP.Character
	if not (char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")) then return end
	local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not root then return end
	local ok = pcall(function()
		local atms = Workspace:FindFirstChild("ATMs")
		if not atms then
			return
		end
		local models = {}
		for _, v in atms:GetChildren() do
			if v:IsA("Model") then
				table.insert(models, v)
			end
		end
		local atmCount = #models
		if atmCount == 0 then return end
		if os.clock() >= atmSwitchTime then
			currentATMIndex = currentATMIndex % atmCount + 1
			atmSwitchTime = os.clock() + config.boxing.atmSwitchInterval
		end
		local v = models[currentATMIndex]
		if v and (v:GetAttribute("health") or 0) > 0 then
			local pos = v.WorldPivot.Position
			root.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos.x, pos.y, pos.z)
			local h = v:GetAttribute("health")
			local distance = (root.Position - pos).Magnitude
			if distance <= 40 and h and h > 0 then
				v:SetAttribute("health", h - 8)
			end
		end
	end)
	if not ok then
		return
	end
end

function boxingStomp()
	local origin = getOrigin()
	if not origin then return end
	for _, p in Players:GetPlayers() do
		if p ~= LP and p.Character and p.Character.PrimaryPart then
			local d = (p.Character.PrimaryPart.Position - origin).Magnitude
			if d <= config.boxing.stompRange and ClientReplicator.Check(p, { knocked = true, carried = false }) then
				pcall(function()
					return Signal.FireServer("finish", p)
				end)
			end
		end
	end
end

function collectCash()
	local origin = getOrigin()
	if not origin then return end
	local targets = {}
	local entities = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Game")
	local cashBundle = entities and entities:FindFirstChild("Entities") and entities.Entities:FindFirstChild("CashBundle")
	if cashBundle then
		for _, cash in cashBundle:GetChildren() do
			if cash.PrimaryPart and cash.Parent then
				table.insert(targets, cash)
			end
		end
	end
	for _, cash in CollectionService:GetTagged("cashbundle") do
		if cash.PrimaryPart and cash.Parent then
			local dup = false
			for _, t in targets do
				if t == cash then dup = true end
			end
			if not dup then
				table.insert(targets, cash)
			end
		end
	end
	local char = LP.Character
	local charPart = char and (char.PrimaryPart or char:FindFirstChildOfClass("BasePart"))
	for _, cash in targets do
		local d = (cash.PrimaryPart.Position - origin).Magnitude
		if d <= config.boxing.cashRange and charPart and typeof(firetouchinterest) == "function" then
			pcall(function()
				firetouchinterest(cash.PrimaryPart, charPart, 0)
			end)
			task.spawn(function()
				task.wait(0.05)
				pcall(function()
					firetouchinterest(cash.PrimaryPart, charPart, 1)
				end)
			end)
		end
	end
end

task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		if config.boxing.enabled then
			pcall(function()
				if config.boxing.multiTarget then
					for _, p in ipairs(getPlayersInRange()) do
						task.spawn(hitPlayer, p)
					end
				else
					local target = selectTarget()
					if target then
						task.spawn(hitPlayer, target)
					end
				end
			end)
		end
		task.wait(config.boxing.punchInterval)
	end
end)

task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		if config.boxing.grabEnabled then
			pcall(grabAura)
		end
		task.wait(0.2)
	end
end)

task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		if config.boxing.enabled and config.boxing.atmEnabled then
			pcall(function()
				local atm = getNearestATM()
				if atm then hitATM(atm) end
			end)
		end
		task.wait(config.boxing.punchInterval)
	end
end)

task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		if config.boxing.enabled and config.boxing.atmTeleport then
			pcall(attackATMTeleport)
		end
		task.wait(0.1)
	end
end)

task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		if config.boxing.enabled and config.boxing.stompEnabled then
			pcall(boxingStomp)
		end
		task.wait(config.boxing.stompInterval)
	end
end)

task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		if config.boxing.enabled and config.boxing.cashEnabled then
			pcall(collectCash)
		end
		task.wait(config.boxing.cashInterval)
	end
end)

skinsMod = load("skins")
stateMod = load("state")
CaseMenu = load("CaseMenu")
skinsPatched = false

function resolveGuns(skinData)
	local guns = skinData.compatabilities
	if typeof(guns) == "string" then
		guns = skinsMod.compatabilities and skinsMod.compatabilities[guns]
	end
	return type(guns) == "table" and guns or {}
end

function applySkinsUnlock()
	stateMod.data = stateMod.data or {}
	stateMod.data.ownedSkins = stateMod.data.ownedSkins or {}
	stateMod.data.equippedSkins = stateMod.data.equippedSkins or {}

	local combos = 0
	for skinName, skinData in skinsMod.skinData do
		if skinName ~= "cases" then
			for _, gun in resolveGuns(skinData) do
				stateMod.data.ownedSkins[gun] = stateMod.data.ownedSkins[gun] or {}
				stateMod.data.ownedSkins[gun][skinName] = 999
				combos += 1
			end
		end
	end

	if not skinsPatched then
		skinsPatched = true

		local function rebuildEquip(gun)
			local item = v3item.inventory.getFromName(gun)
			if not item then return end
			if item.skin then
				item:__unequip()
				item:SetEquipped(true)
			end
		end

		CaseMenu._getIsLocked = function(_, _gun, _skin)
			return false
		end

		function CaseMenu.AttemptEquip(self, gun, skin)
			local compatible = skinsMod.IsSkinCompatible(gun, skin)
			stateMod.data.equippedSkins[gun] = skin
			stateMod.equippedSkins = stateMod.equippedSkins or {}
			stateMod.equippedSkins[LP.UserId] = skin
			v3item.inventory.unequipAll()
			v3item.inventory.skinUpdate(gun, skin)
			rebuildEquip(gun)
			if self._setEquipped then
				self._setEquipped(self, gun, skin)
			end
		end

		local origSkinUpdate = v3item.inventory.skinUpdate
		function v3item.inventory.skinUpdate(gun, skin)
			origSkinUpdate(gun, skin)
			rebuildEquip(gun)
		end
	end

	return combos
end

gunList = {}
skinsByGun = {}
do
	seen = {}
	for skinName, skinData in skinsMod.skinData do
		if skinName ~= "cases" then
			for _, gun in resolveGuns(skinData) do
				if not seen[gun] then
					seen[gun] = true
					table.insert(gunList, gun)
					skinsByGun[gun] = {}
				end
				table.insert(skinsByGun[gun], skinName)
			end
		end
	end
end

selectedGun = gunList[1]
selectedSkin = (selectedGun and skinsByGun[selectedGun] and skinsByGun[selectedGun][1]) or nil

function equipSkin(gun, skin)
	if not gun or not skin then return end
	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		CaseMenu.AttemptEquip(CaseMenu, gun, skin)
	end)
	if not ok then
		print("[SkinUnlock] equip failed: " .. tostring(err))
	end
end

function randomString(length)
	length = length or 16
	local chars = "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789"
	local result = ""
	for i = 1, length do
		local rand = math.random(1, #chars)
		result = result .. string.sub(chars, rand, rand)
	end
	return result
end

inv = v3item.inventory
itemNames = {}
itemData = {}
injected = {}
for _, v in pairs(ReplicatedStorage.devv.shared.Indicies.v3items.bin:GetDescendants()) do
	if v:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		itemName = v.Name
		table.insert(itemNames, itemName)
		ok, data = pcall(function()
			return require(v)
		end)
		if ok and data then
			itemData[itemName] = data
		end
	end
end
table.sort(itemNames)
selectedItem = itemNames[1]

function restoreInjected()
	for guid, data in pairs(injected) do
		if not inv.items[guid] then
			local d = {}
			for k, val in pairs(data) do
				d[k] = val
			end
			pcall(function()
				inv.add(d, false)
			end)
		end
		local found = false
		for _, cd in inv.currentItemsData do
			if cd.guid == guid then
				found = true
				break
			end
		end
		if not found then
			table.insert(inv.currentItemsData, data)
		end
	end
	if inv.rerender then
		pcall(function()
			inv:rerender()
		end)
	end
end

origUpdate = inv.update
inv.update = function(data)
	origUpdate(data)
	restoreInjected()
end

origSwap = inv.swap
inv.swap = function(g1, g2)
	if injected[g1] or injected[g2] then
		local a, b = nil, nil
		for i, d in ipairs(inv.currentItemsData) do
			if d.guid == g1 then a = i end
			if d.guid == g2 then b = i end
		end
		if a and b then
			inv.currentItemsData[a], inv.currentItemsData[b] = inv.currentItemsData[b], inv.currentItemsData[a]
			pcall(function()
				inv:rerender()
			end)
			return
		end
		local o = inv.ordered
		local i1, i2 = nil, nil
		for i, g in ipairs(o) do
			if g == g1 then i1 = i end
			if g == g2 then i2 = i end
		end
		if i1 and i2 then
			local p1 = inv.orderedByGUID[g1]
			local p2 = inv.orderedByGUID[g2]
			o[i1], o[i2] = o[i2], o[i1]
			inv.orderedByGUID[g1] = p2
			inv.orderedByGUID[g2] = p1
			pcall(function()
				inv:rerender()
			end)
		end
		return
	end
	return origSwap(g1, g2)
end

function addToInventory(itemData)
	if not itemData.guid then
		itemData.guid = randomString(32)
	end
	injected[itemData.guid] = itemData
	if inv.add then
		pcall(function()
			inv.add(itemData, false)
		end)
	end
	if inv.currentItemsData then
		table.insert(inv.currentItemsData, itemData)
	end
	if inv.rerender then
		pcall(function()
			inv:rerender()
		end)
	end
end

CR = ClientReplicator
crosshairs = v3item.crosshairs
origFire = Signal.FireServer
origInvoke = Signal.InvokeServer
function localRemove(guid)
	pcall(function()
		inv.remove(guid)
	end)
	for i, d in ipairs(inv.currentItemsData) do
		if d.guid == guid then
			table.remove(inv.currentItemsData, i)
			break
		end
	end
	injected[guid] = nil
	pcall(function()
		inv:rerender()
	end)
end

Signal.FireServer = function(name, ...)
	local args = {...}
	local guid = args[1]
	if type(name) == "string" and injected[guid] then
		if name == "removeItem" then
			localRemove(guid)
			return
		elseif name == "reload" then
			local item = inv.items[guid]
			if item and item.ammoManager then
				local am = item.ammoManager
				if am.ammo < (item.ammo or 0) then
					task.delay(item.reloadTime or 1, function()
						pcall(function()
							am:setAmmo(item.ammo or 0)
						end)
						pcall(function()
							am:setAmmoOut(999)
						end)
						pcall(function()
							CR.Set(LP, "reloading", false)
						end)
						pcall(function()
							crosshairs.resetReload()
						end)
					end)
				end
			end
			return
		end
	end
	return origFire(name, ...)
end

dropped = {}
Signal.InvokeServer = function(name, ...)
	local args = {...}
	local guid = args[1]
	if type(name) == "string" and injected[guid] then
		if name == "attemptDrop" then
			local data = injected[guid]
			local item = inv.items[guid]
			local hrp = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local src = item and item.model
			if not src and item then
				pcall(function()
					src = item:createModel(false, LP)
				end)
			end
			if src and hrp then
				local dropModel = src:Clone()
				local look = hrp.CFrame.LookVector
				for _, p in ipairs(dropModel:GetDescendants()) do
					if p:IsA("BasePart") then
						p.CanCollide = true
						p.Anchored = false
						p.CollisionGroupId = 0
					end
				end
				dropModel:PivotTo(CFrame.new(hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) + look * 2) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.random() * math.pi * 2, 0))
				dropModel.Parent = workspace
				task.spawn(function()
					local pp = dropModel.PrimaryPart or dropModel:FindFirstChildOfClass("BasePart")
					if pp then
						local lastY = pp.Position.Y
						while dropModel.Parent do
							task.wait(0.15)
							pp = dropModel.PrimaryPart or pp
							if not pp or not pp.Parent then
								break
							end
							local dy = math.abs(pp.Position.Y - lastY)
							lastY = pp.Position.Y
							if dy < 0.02 then
								break
							end
						end
						if dropModel.Parent and pp and pp.Parent then
							local ray = workspace:Raycast(pp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 8, 0), Vector3.new(0, -30, 0))
							local gy = ray and ray.Position.Y or (pp.Position.Y - 5)
							local cf = pp.CFrame
							if pp.Position.Y < gy - 0.2 or pp.Position.Y > gy + 4 then
								dropModel:PivotTo(CFrame.new(pp.Position.X, gy + 0.5, pp.Position.Z) * cf.Rotation)
							end
							for _, p in ipairs(dropModel:GetDescendants()) do
								if p:IsA("BasePart") then
									p.Anchored = true
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end)
				local prompt = Instance.new("ProximityPrompt")
				prompt.ActionText = "捡起"
				prompt.ObjectText = item.name or "武器"
				prompt.HoldDuration = 0
				prompt.MaxActivationDistance = 8
				prompt.RequiresLineOfSight = false
				prompt.Triggered:Connect(function()
					local d = dropped[prompt]
					if d then
						dropped[prompt] = nil
						pcall(function()
							dropModel:Destroy()
						end)
						addToInventory(d)
					end
				end)
				dropped[prompt] = data
				prompt.Parent = dropModel.PrimaryPart or dropModel
				pcall(function()
					item:SetEquipped(false)
				end)
				for i, d in ipairs(inv.currentItemsData) do
					if d.guid == guid then
						table.remove(inv.currentItemsData, i)
						break
					end
				end
				injected[guid] = nil
				pcall(function()
					inv:rerender()
				end)
				return true
			end
			return false
		end
	end
	return origInvoke(name, ...)
end

RunService = cloneref(game:GetService("RunService"))
CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")

function getRoot(char)
	return char and (char.PrimaryPart or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
end

oldGravity = workspace.Gravity
swimBeat = nil
swimDiedConn = nil
swimming = false

function setSwim(Bool)
	config.movement.swim = Bool
	local char = LP.Character
	local humanoid = char and char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
	if Bool then
		if not (char and humanoid) then return end
		if swimming then return end
		oldGravity = workspace.Gravity
		workspace.Gravity = 0
		swimming = true
		if swimDiedConn then
			pcall(function()
				swimDiedConn:Disconnect()
			end)
		end
		swimDiedConn = humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
			workspace.Gravity = oldGravity
			swimming = false
			config.movement.swim = false
		end)
		local enums = Enum.HumanoidStateType:GetEnumItems()
		table.remove(enums, table.find(enums, Enum.HumanoidStateType.None))
		for _, v in ipairs(enums) do
			humanoid:SetStateEnabled(v, false)
		end
		humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming)
		if swimBeat then
			pcall(function()
				swimBeat:Disconnect()
			end)
		end
		swimBeat = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			pcall(function()
				local c = LP.Character
				local root = getRoot(c)
				if root then
					root.Velocity = ((humanoid.MoveDirection ~= Vector3.new() or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space)) and root.Velocity or Vector3.new())
				end
			end)
		end)
	else
		if not swimming then return end
		swimming = false
		workspace.Gravity = oldGravity
		if swimDiedConn then
			pcall(function()
				swimDiedConn:Disconnect()
			end)
			swimDiedConn = nil
		end
		if swimBeat then
			pcall(function()
				swimBeat:Disconnect()
			end)
			swimBeat = nil
		end
		if humanoid then
			local enums = Enum.HumanoidStateType:GetEnumItems()
			table.remove(enums, table.find(enums, Enum.HumanoidStateType.None))
			for _, v in ipairs(enums) do
				humanoid:SetStateEnabled(v, true)
			end
		end
	end
end

tpwalkConn = nil

function setTPWalk(Bool)
	config.movement.tpwalk = Bool
	if Bool then
		if tpwalkConn then return end
		local char = LP.Character
		local humanoid = char and char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
		if not (char and humanoid) then return end
		tpwalkConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(delta)
			local c = LP.Character
			local h = c and c:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
			if not (c and h and h.Parent) then
				return
			end
			if h.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
				c:TranslateBy(h.MoveDirection * config.movement.tpwalkSpeed * delta * 10)
			end
		end)
	else
		if tpwalkConn then
			tpwalkConn:Disconnect()
			tpwalkConn = nil
		end
	end
end

noclipConn = nil
noclipParts = {}

function setNoclip(Bool)
	config.movement.noclip = Bool
	if Bool then
		if noclipConn then return end
		noclipParts = {}
		noclipConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
			if not config.movement.noclip then return end
			local char = LP.Character
			if not char then return end
			for _, child in pairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
				if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.CanCollide == true then
					child.CanCollide = false
					noclipParts[child] = true
				end
			end
		end)
	else
		if noclipConn then
			noclipConn:Disconnect()
			noclipConn = nil
		end
		for child, _ in pairs(noclipParts) do
			if typeof(child) == "Instance" and child:IsA("BasePart") and child.Parent then
				child.CanCollide = true
			end
		end
		noclipParts = {}
	end
end

invisAnimId = "rbxassetid://17871770160"
invisTrack = nil

function setInvisible(Bool)
	config.movement.invisible = Bool
	local char = LP.Character
	local humanoid = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if Bool then
		if not humanoid then return end
		if invisTrack then
			pcall(function()
				invisTrack:Stop()
			end)
			invisTrack = nil
		end
		local Animation = Instance.new("Animation")
		Animation.AnimationId = invisAnimId
		local ok, track = pcall(function()
			return humanoid:LoadAnimation(Animation)
		end)
		if ok and track then
			track:Play()
			track:AdjustSpeed(0)
			track.TimePosition = 0.42
			invisTrack = track
		end
	else
		if invisTrack then
			pcall(function()
				invisTrack:Stop()
			end)
			invisTrack = nil
		end
	end
end

LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
	if config.movement.invisible then
		task.wait(0.5)
		setInvisible(true)
	end
end)

desyncLoc = CFrame.new()
desyncHook = nil
prevLook = nil
isSpinningDesync = false

customDesync = true
customOffset = Vector3.new(9999999, 9999999, 9999999)
desyncRandom = false

function setDesync(Bool)
	config.movement.desync = Bool
	if Bool and not desyncHook then
		local ok, hook = pcall(function()
			return hookmetamethod(game, "__index", newcclosure(function(self, key)
				if config.movement.desync and not checkcaller() and key == "CFrame" and LP.Character and self == LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and not isSpinningDesync then
					return desyncLoc
				end
				return desyncHook(self, key)
			end))
		end)
		if ok then
			desyncHook = hook
		end
	end
end

function getDesyncOffset()
	if desyncRandom then
		return CFrame.new(
			666 + math.random() * (9999999 - 666),
			666 + math.random() * (9999999 - 666),
			666 + math.random() * (9999999 - 666)
		)
	end
	if customDesync then
		return CFrame.new(customOffset)
	end
	local ping = LP:GetNetworkPing() * 1000
	if ping < 100 then
		return CFrame.new(0, 0, -2)
	elseif ping <= 170 then
		return CFrame.new(0, 0, -2.7)
	else
		return CFrame.new(0, 0, -3.7)
	end
end

RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
	if not config.movement.desync then return end
	local root = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not root then return end
	local currentLook = root.CFrame.LookVector
	if prevLook then
		local dot = math.clamp(prevLook:Dot(currentLook), -1, 1)
		isSpinningDesync = math.deg(math.acos(dot)) > 15
	end
	prevLook = currentLook
	if isSpinningDesync then return end
	desyncLoc = root.CFrame
	root.CFrame = desyncLoc * getDesyncOffset()
	RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
	root.CFrame = desyncLoc
end)

LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
	prevLook = nil
	isSpinningDesync = false
end)

lastReloadTime = 0
lastAttacker = nil

function getGunGuid()
	for _, v in next, v3item.inventory.items do
		if v.type == "Gun" then
			return v.guid
		end
	end
end

function isAlive(p)
	local hum = p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
	return hum and hum.Health > 0
end

function selectGunTarget()
	if config.guns.lockTarget then
		local p = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(config.guns.lockTarget)
		if p and p ~= LP and p.Character and p.Character.PrimaryPart and isAlive(p) then
			if config.guns.rangeEnabled then
				local origin = getOrigin()
				if origin and (p.Character.PrimaryPart.Position - origin).Magnitude <= config.guns.range then
					return p
				end
			else
				return p
			end
		else
			config.guns.lockTarget = nil
		end
	end
	local origin = getOrigin()
	if not origin then return nil end
	local best, bestDist = nil, math.huge
	for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if p ~= LP and p.Character and p.Character.PrimaryPart and isAlive(p) then
			local d = (p.Character.PrimaryPart.Position - origin).Magnitude
			if (not config.guns.rangeEnabled or d <= config.guns.range) and d < bestDist then
				best, bestDist = p, d
			end
		end
	end
	return best
end

function getGunFireMode(item)
	if item and item.firemode == "auto" then
		return "auto"
	end
	return "semi"
end

function gunRagebot()
	local item = v3item.inventory.getEquippedItem()
	if not item or item.type ~= "Gun" then return end
	local gunGuid = item.guid
	local ammoManager = item.ammoManager
	if ammoManager and ammoManager.ammo <= 0 then
		if config.guns.reloadEnabled and os.clock() - lastReloadTime > (item.reloadTime or 1.2) then
			lastReloadTime = os.clock()
			pcall(function()
				Signal.FireServer("reload", gunGuid)
			end)
		end
		return
	end
	local target = selectGunTarget()
	if not target then return end
	local char = target.Character
	local head = char and char:FindFirstChild("Head")
	local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local part = head or hrp or (char and char.PrimaryPart)
	if not part then return end
	local id = GUID()
	pcall(function()
		Signal.FireServer("replicateProjectiles", gunGuid, { { id, part.CFrame } }, getGunFireMode(item))
	end)
	pcall(function()
		Signal.FireServer("projectileHit", id, "player", {
			hitSize = part.Size,
			hitPart = part,
			pos = part.Position,
			hitPlayerId = target.UserId,
		})
	end)
	if ammoManager then
		pcall(function()
			ammoManager:UseAmmo(1)
		end)
	end
end

task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		if config.guns.enabled then
			pcall(gunRagebot)
		end
		task.wait(0.01)
	end
end)

for _, remote in ipairs(ReplicatedStorage.devv.remoteStorage:GetChildren()) do
	if remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
		pcall(function()
			remote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(...)
				local a, b, c = ...
				if typeof(c) == "Instance" and c:IsA("Player") and c ~= LP and typeof(a) == "string" and typeof(b) == "table" then
					lastAttacker = c
				elseif typeof(a) == "Vector3" then
					local myPos = LP.Character and LP.Character.PrimaryPart and LP.Character.PrimaryPart.Position
					if myPos then
						local best, bestDist
						for _, p in Players:GetPlayers() do
							if p ~= LP and p.Character and p.Character.PrimaryPart then
								local d = (p.Character.PrimaryPart.Position - a).Magnitude
								if d <= 20 and (not best or d < bestDist) then
									best, bestDist = p, d
								end
							end
						end
						if best then
							lastAttacker = best
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end)
	end
end

task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		if lastAttacker then
			pcall(function()
				local attacker = lastAttacker
				lastAttacker = nil
				if attacker and attacker ~= LP then
					if config.movement.autoDesync then
						setDesync(true)
					end
					if config.guns.retaliate then
						config.guns.lockTarget = attacker.UserId
						local gunGuid = getGunGuid()
						if gunGuid and v3item.inventory.items[gunGuid] then
							pcall(function()
								v3item.inventory.items[gunGuid]:SetEquipped(true)
							end)
						end
						config.guns.enabled = true
					end
				end
			end)
		end
		task.wait(0.2)
	end
end)

v3effect = load("v3effect")
rpgState = {
	active = false,
	guid = nil,
	eguid = nil,
}
rpgLastReloadTime = 0
rpgPrevPos = {}
rpgSentCount = 0
rpgBudgetStart = os.clock()
rpgOf = Signal.FireServer

function rpgCanSend()
	local now = os.clock()
	if now - rpgBudgetStart >= 1 then
		rpgBudgetStart = now
		rpgSentCount = 0
	end
	if rpgSentCount >= 30 then
		return false
	end
	rpgSentCount += 1
	return true
end

function rpgBoom(p)
	local hrp = p.Character.PrimaryPart
	local pos = hrp.Position
	local last = rpgPrevPos[hrp]
	rpgPrevPos[hrp] = pos
	local vel = last and (pos - last) / config.rpg.bombInterval or Vector3.new()
	for i, dist in ipairs(config.rpg.predictionDistances) do
		local target = pos
		if dist > 0 and vel.Magnitude > 5 then
			target = pos + vel.Unit * dist
		end
		if i > 1 and not rpgCanSend() then
			return
		end
		if config.rpg.explosionVisible and v3effect then
			pcall(function()
				v3effect.ReplicateEffect("baserocketexplosion", {
					["position"] = target,
					["guid"] = rpgState.eguid,
					["skinName"] = stateMod and stateMod.equippedSkins and stateMod.equippedSkins[LP.UserId],
				})
			end)
		end
		task.spawn(rpgOf, "rocketHit", rpgState.guid, rpgState.eguid, target)
	end
end

Signal.FireServer = function(...)
	local args = {...}

	if args[1] == "rocketHit" then
		rpgState.guid = args[2]
		rpgState.eguid = args[3]
		rpgState.active = true
		if config.rpg.enabled then
			for _, p in Players:GetPlayers() do
				if p ~= LP and p.Character and p.Character.PrimaryPart then
					pcall(rpgBoom, p)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return rpgOf(unpack(args))
end

task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		if config.rpg.enabled and rpgState.active and rpgState.guid then
			for _, p in Players:GetPlayers() do
				if p ~= LP and p.Character and p.Character.PrimaryPart then
					pcall(rpgBoom, p)
				end
			end
		end
		task.wait(config.rpg.bombInterval)
	end
end)

task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		if config.rpg.enabled and config.rpg.reloadEnabled then
			pcall(function()
				for _, v in next, v3item.inventory.items do
					if v.name == "RPG" and v.ammoManager and v.ammoManager.ammo <= 0 then
						pcall(function()
							Signal.FireServer("reload", v.guid)
						end)
						break
					end
				end
			end)
		end
		task.wait(config.rpg.reloadInterval)
	end
end)

function setAntiGrab()
	local ok, handler = pcall(function()
		return require(ReplicatedStorage.devv.client.Handlers.GrabHandler)
	end)
	if not ok or not handler then
		return
	end
	local ok1, origCheck = pcall(function()
		return getmetatable(handler).__index.CheckValid
	end)
	local ok2, origGrab = pcall(function()
		return getmetatable(handler).__index.Grab
	end)
	handler.CheckValid = function(p28, p29, p30)
		if p29 == LP then
			return false
		end
		if ok1 and origCheck then
			return origCheck(p28, p29, p30)
		end
		return true
	end
	handler.Grab = function(p54, p55)
		if p55 == LP then
			return
		end
		if ok2 and origGrab then
			return origGrab(p54, p55)
		end
	end
end

buyItems = {}
buyNames = {}

function refreshBuyItems()
	buyItems = {}
	buyNames = {}
	local holder = Workspace:FindFirstChild("ItemsOnSale")
	if not holder then return end
	for _, model in ipairs(holder:GetChildren()) do
		if model:IsA("Model") then
			local part = model.PrimaryPart or model:FindFirstChildOfClass("BasePart")
			local pos = part and part.Position
			if pos and not buyItems[model.Name] then
				buyItems[model.Name] = pos
				table.insert(buyNames, model.Name)
			end
		end
	end
	table.sort(buyNames)
end

refreshBuyItems()
buyTarget = buyNames[1]
buyLastPos = nil

function goBuy()
	if not buyTarget then return end
	local pos = buyItems[buyTarget]
	if not pos then return end
	local char = LP.Character
	local root = char and char.PrimaryPart
	if not root then return end
	buyLastPos = root.Position
	root.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0))
end

function goBack()
	if not buyLastPos then return end
	local char = LP.Character
	local root = char and char.PrimaryPart
	if root then
		root.CFrame = CFrame.new(buyLastPos)
	end
	buyLastPos = nil
end

dartBuyLastPos = nil

function getNearestBuyPoint(Name)
	local holder = Workspace:FindFirstChild("ItemsOnSale")
	if not holder then return nil end
	local origin = getOrigin()
	local best, bestDist = nil, math.huge
	for _, model in ipairs(holder:GetChildren()) do
		if model:IsA("Model") and model.Name == Name then
			local part = model.PrimaryPart or model:FindFirstChildOfClass("BasePart")
			local pos = part and part.Position
			if pos then
				if not origin then return pos end
				local d = (pos - origin).Magnitude
				if d < bestDist then
					best, bestDist = pos, d
				end
			end
		end
	end
	return best
end

function goBuyDart()
	local pos = getNearestBuyPoint("Ninja Star")
	if not pos then return false end
	local char = LP.Character
	local root = char and char.PrimaryPart
	if not root then return false end
	dartBuyLastPos = root.Position
	root.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0))
	return true
end

function goBackDart()
	if not dartBuyLastPos then return end
	local char = LP.Character
	local root = char and char.PrimaryPart
	if root then
		root.CFrame = CFrame.new(dartBuyLastPos)
	end
	dartBuyLastPos = nil
end

Euphoria = ESP.Connections
lplayer = Players.LocalPlayer
Cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
RotationAngle, Tick = -45, tick()

do
	HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
	ESP.SameScriptUsers = ESP.SameScriptUsers or {}
	ESP.SameScriptCheckId = _G.ATLAS_SCRIPT_ID or ""
	function UrlEncode(v)
		v = tostring(v or "")
		local ok, encoded = pcall(function() return HttpService:UrlEncode(v) end)
		if ok and encoded then return encoded end
		return v:gsub(" ", "%%20")
	end
	function FetchSameScript()
		local query = "user_id=" .. UrlEncode(lplayer.UserId) .. "&place_id=" .. UrlEncode(game.PlaceId) .. "&job_id=" .. UrlEncode(game.JobId)
		local ok, body = pcall(function() return game:HttpGet("https://panel.atlasteam.live/api/same-server?" .. query .. "&_t=" .. tostring(tick()), true) end)
		if not ok or not body then return end
		local ok2, data = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(body) end)
		if not ok2 or not data or not data.ok then return end
		local next = {}
		for _, u in ipairs(data.same_server or {}) do
			if ESP.SameScriptCheckId == "" or tostring(u.script_id) == ESP.SameScriptCheckId then
				next[tostring(u.user_id)] = u
			end
		end
		ESP.SameScriptUsers = next
	end
	pcall(FetchSameScript)
	task.spawn(function()
		while task.wait(15) do
			pcall(FetchSameScript)
		end
	end)
end

Functions = {}
do
	function Functions:Create(Class, Properties)
		local _Instance = typeof(Class) == 'string' and Instance.new(Class) or Class
		for Property, Value in pairs(Properties) do
			_Instance[Property] = Value
		end
		return _Instance
	end

	function Functions:FadeOutOnDist(element, distance)
		local transparency = math.max(0.1, 1 - (distance / ESP.MaxDistance))
		if element:IsA("TextLabel") then
			element.TextTransparency = 1 - transparency
		elseif element:IsA("ImageLabel") then
			element.ImageTransparency = 1 - transparency
		elseif element:IsA("UIStroke") then
			element.Transparency = 1 - transparency
		elseif element:IsA("Frame") then
			element.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - transparency
		elseif element:IsA("Highlight") then
			element.FillTransparency = 1 - transparency
			element.OutlineTransparency = 1 - transparency
		end
	end
end

	ScreenGui = Functions:Create("ScreenGui", {
		Parent = CoreGui,
		Name = "ESPHolder",
	})

	DupeCheck = function(plr)
		if ScreenGui:FindFirstChild(plr.Name) then
			ScreenGui[plr.Name]:Destroy()
		end
	end

		local Chams = Functions:Create("Highlight", {Parent = ScreenGui, FillTransparency = 1, OutlineTransparency = 0, OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(119, 120, 255), DepthMode = "AlwaysOnTop"})
		local WeaponIcon = Functions:Create("ImageLabel", {Parent = ScreenGui, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0), BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 40)})

		local RightTop = Functions:Create("Frame", {Parent = ScreenGui, BackgroundColor3 = ESP.Drawing.Boxes.Corner.RGB, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)})
		local RightSide = Functions:Create("Frame", {Parent = ScreenGui, BackgroundColor3 = ESP.Drawing.Boxes.Corner.RGB, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)})
		local BottomSide = Functions:Create("Frame", {Parent = ScreenGui, BackgroundColor3 = ESP.Drawing.Boxes.Corner.RGB, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)})
		local BottomDown = Functions:Create("Frame", {Parent = ScreenGui, BackgroundColor3 = ESP.Drawing.Boxes.Corner.RGB, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)})
		local BottomRightSide = Functions:Create("Frame", {Parent = ScreenGui, BackgroundColor3 = ESP.Drawing.Boxes.Corner.RGB, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)})
		local BottomRightDown = Functions:Create("Frame", {Parent = ScreenGui, BackgroundColor3 = ESP.Drawing.Boxes.Corner.RGB, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)})
		local Flag1 = Functions:Create("TextLabel", {Parent = ScreenGui, Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 20), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), BackgroundTransparency = 1, TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), Font = Enum.Font.Code, TextSize = ESP.FontSize, TextStrokeTransparency = 0, TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)})
		local Flag2 = Functions:Create("TextLabel", {Parent = ScreenGui, Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 20), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), BackgroundTransparency = 1, TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), Font = Enum.Font.Code, TextSize = ESP.FontSize, TextStrokeTransparency = 0, TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)})
		local Updater = function()
			local Connection
			local function HideESP()
				Box.Visible = false
				Name.Visible = false
				Distance.Visible = false
				Weapon.Visible = false
				Healthbar.Visible = false
				BehindHealthbar.Visible = false
				HealthText.Visible = false
				WeaponIcon.Visible = false
				LeftTop.Visible = false
				LeftSide.Visible = false
				BottomSide.Visible = false
				BottomDown.Visible = false
				RightTop.Visible = false
				RightSide.Visible = false
				BottomRightSide.Visible = false
				BottomRightDown.Visible = false
				Flag1.Visible = false
				Chams.Enabled = false
				Flag2.Visible = false
				if not plr then
					ScreenGui:Destroy()
					Connection:Disconnect()
				end
			end
			Connection = Euphoria.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
				if not ESP.Enabled then
					HideESP()
					return
				end
				if plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
					local HRP = plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart
					local Humanoid = plr.Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
					local Pos, OnScreen = Cam:WorldToScreenPoint(HRP.Position)
					local Dist = (Cam.CFrame.Position - HRP.Position).Magnitude / 3.5714285714

					if OnScreen and Dist <= ESP.MaxDistance then
						local Size = HRP.Size.Y
						local scaleFactor = (Size * Cam.ViewportSize.Y) / (Pos.Z * 2)
						local w, h = 3 * scaleFactor, 4.5 * scaleFactor

						if ESP.FadeOut.OnDistance then
							Functions:FadeOutOnDist(Box, Dist)
							Functions:FadeOutOnDist(Outline, Dist)
							Functions:FadeOutOnDist(Name, Dist)
							Functions:FadeOutOnDist(Distance, Dist)
							Functions:FadeOutOnDist(Weapon, Dist)
							Functions:FadeOutOnDist(Healthbar, Dist)
							Functions:FadeOutOnDist(BehindHealthbar, Dist)
							Functions:FadeOutOnDist(HealthText, Dist)
							Functions:FadeOutOnDist(WeaponIcon, Dist)
							Functions:FadeOutOnDist(LeftTop, Dist)
							Functions:FadeOutOnDist(LeftSide, Dist)
							Functions:FadeOutOnDist(BottomSide, Dist)
							Functions:FadeOutOnDist(BottomDown, Dist)
							Functions:FadeOutOnDist(RightTop, Dist)
							Functions:FadeOutOnDist(RightSide, Dist)
							Functions:FadeOutOnDist(BottomRightSide, Dist)
							Functions:FadeOutOnDist(BottomRightDown, Dist)
							Functions:FadeOutOnDist(Chams, Dist)
							Functions:FadeOutOnDist(Flag1, Dist)
							Functions:FadeOutOnDist(Flag2, Dist)
						end
