						if (not ESP.TeamCheck or enemy) and plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then

							do
								Chams.Adornee = plr.Character
								Chams.Enabled = ESP.Drawing.Chams.Enabled
								Chams.FillColor = ESP.Drawing.Chams.FillRGB
								Chams.OutlineColor = ESP.Drawing.Chams.OutlineRGB
								do
									if ESP.Drawing.Chams.Thermal then
										local breathe_effect = math.atan(math.sin(tick() * 2)) * 2 / math.pi
										Chams.FillTransparency = ESP.Drawing.Chams.Fill_Transparency * breathe_effect * 0.01
										Chams.OutlineTransparency = ESP.Drawing.Chams.Outline_Transparency * breathe_effect * 0.01
									end
								end
								if ESP.Drawing.Chams.VisibleCheck then
									Chams.DepthMode = "Occluded"
								else
									Chams.DepthMode = "AlwaysOnTop"
								end
							end

							do
								LeftTop.Visible = ESP.Drawing.Boxes.Corner.Enabled
								LeftTop.Position = UDim2.new(0, Pos.X - w / 2, 0, Pos.Y - h / 2)
								LeftTop.Size = UDim2.new(0, w / 5, 0, 1)

								LeftSide.Visible = ESP.Drawing.Boxes.Corner.Enabled
								LeftSide.Position = UDim2.new(0, Pos.X - w / 2, 0, Pos.Y - h / 2)
								LeftSide.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, h / 5)

								BottomSide.Visible = ESP.Drawing.Boxes.Corner.Enabled
								BottomSide.Position = UDim2.new(0, Pos.X - w / 2, 0, Pos.Y + h / 2)
								BottomSide.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, h / 5)
								BottomSide.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 5)

								BottomDown.Visible = ESP.Drawing.Boxes.Corner.Enabled
								BottomDown.Position = UDim2.new(0, Pos.X - w / 2, 0, Pos.Y + h / 2)
								BottomDown.Size = UDim2.new(0, w / 5, 0, 1)
								BottomDown.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)

								RightTop.Visible = ESP.Drawing.Boxes.Corner.Enabled
								RightTop.Position = UDim2.new(0, Pos.X + w / 2, 0, Pos.Y - h / 2)
								RightTop.Size = UDim2.new(0, w / 5, 0, 1)
								RightTop.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)

								RightSide.Visible = ESP.Drawing.Boxes.Corner.Enabled
								RightSide.Position = UDim2.new(0, Pos.X + w / 2 - 1, 0, Pos.Y - h / 2)
								RightSide.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, h / 5)
								RightSide.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)

								BottomRightSide.Visible = ESP.Drawing.Boxes.Corner.Enabled
								BottomRightSide.Position = UDim2.new(0, Pos.X + w / 2, 0, Pos.Y + h / 2)
								BottomRightSide.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, h / 5)
								BottomRightSide.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1)

								BottomRightDown.Visible = ESP.Drawing.Boxes.Corner.Enabled
								BottomRightDown.Position = UDim2.new(0, Pos.X + w / 2, 0, Pos.Y + h / 2)
								BottomRightDown.Size = UDim2.new(0, w / 5, 0, 1)
								BottomRightDown.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1)
							end

							do
								Box.Position = UDim2.new(0, Pos.X - w / 2, 0, Pos.Y - h / 2)
								Box.Size = UDim2.new(0, w, 0, h)
								Box.Visible = ESP.Drawing.Boxes.Full.Enabled

								if ESP.Drawing.Boxes.Filled.Enabled then
									Box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
									if ESP.Drawing.Boxes.GradientFill then
										Box.BackgroundTransparency = ESP.Drawing.Boxes.Filled.Transparency
									else
										Box.BackgroundTransparency = 1
									end
									Box.BorderSizePixel = 1
								else
									Box.BackgroundTransparency = 1
								end
								RotationAngle = RotationAngle + (tick() - Tick) * ESP.Drawing.Boxes.RotationSpeed * math.cos(math.pi / 4 * tick() - math.pi / 2)
								if ESP.Drawing.Boxes.Animate then
									Gradient1.Rotation = RotationAngle
									Gradient2.Rotation = RotationAngle
								else
									Gradient1.Rotation = -45
									Gradient2.Rotation = -45
								end
								Tick = tick()
							end

							do
								local health = Humanoid.Health / Humanoid.MaxHealth
								Healthbar.Visible = ESP.Drawing.Healthbar.Enabled
								Healthbar.Position = UDim2.new(0, Pos.X - w / 2 - 6, 0, Pos.Y - h / 2 + h * (1 - health))
								Healthbar.Size = UDim2.new(0, ESP.Drawing.Healthbar.Width, 0, h * health)

								BehindHealthbar.Visible = ESP.Drawing.Healthbar.Enabled
								BehindHealthbar.Position = UDim2.new(0, Pos.X - w / 2 - 6, 0, Pos.Y - h / 2)
								BehindHealthbar.Size = UDim2.new(0, ESP.Drawing.Healthbar.Width, 0, h)

								do
									if ESP.Drawing.Healthbar.HealthText then
										local healthPercentage = math.floor(Humanoid.Health / Humanoid.MaxHealth * 100)
										HealthText.Position = UDim2.new(0, Pos.X - w / 2 - 6, 0, Pos.Y - h / 2 + h * (1 - healthPercentage / 100) + 3)
										HealthText.Text = tostring(healthPercentage)
										HealthText.Visible = Humanoid.Health < Humanoid.MaxHealth
										if ESP.Drawing.Healthbar.Lerp then
											local color = health >= 0.75 and Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0) or health >= 0.5 and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0) or health >= 0.25 and Color3.fromRGB(255, 170, 0) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
											HealthText.TextColor3 = color
										else
											HealthText.TextColor3 = ESP.Drawing.Healthbar.HealthTextRGB
										end
									end
								end
							end

							do
								Name.Visible = ESP.Drawing.Names.Enabled
								local wowTag = ESP.SameScriptUsers and ESP.SameScriptUsers[tostring(plr.UserId)] and ' <font color="#FFFF00">[WOW]</font>' or ""
								if ESP.Options.Friendcheck and lplayer:IsFriendsWith(plr.UserId) then
									Name.Text = string.format('(<font color="rgb(%d, %d, %d)">F</font>) %s%s', ESP.Options.FriendcheckRGB.R * 255, ESP.Options.FriendcheckRGB.G * 255, ESP.Options.FriendcheckRGB.B * 255, plr.Name, wowTag)
								else
									Name.Text = string.format('(<font color="rgb(%d, %d, %d)">E</font>) %s%s', 255, 0, 0, plr.Name, wowTag)
								end
								Name.Position = UDim2.new(0, Pos.X, 0, Pos.Y - h / 2 - 9)
							end

							do
								if ESP.Drawing.Distances.Enabled then
									local armor = plr:GetAttribute("armor") or 0
									local armorText = ESP.Drawing.Armor.Enabled and string.format(' <font color="rgb(135, 206, 235)">[%d]</font>', armor) or ""
									if ESP.Drawing.Distances.Position == "Bottom" then
										Weapon.Position = UDim2.new(0, Pos.X, 0, Pos.Y + h / 2 + 18)
										WeaponIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0, Pos.X - 21, 0, Pos.Y + h / 2 + 15)
										Distance.Position = UDim2.new(0, Pos.X, 0, Pos.Y + h / 2 + 7)
										Distance.Text = string.format("%d meters", math.floor(Dist)) .. armorText
										Distance.Visible = true
									elseif ESP.Drawing.Distances.Position == "Text" then
										Weapon.Position = UDim2.new(0, Pos.X, 0, Pos.Y + h / 2 + 8)
										WeaponIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0, Pos.X - 21, 0, Pos.Y + h / 2 + 5)
										Distance.Visible = false
										local wowTag = ESP.SameScriptUsers and ESP.SameScriptUsers[tostring(plr.UserId)] and ' <font color="#FFFF00">[WOW]</font>' or ""
										if ESP.Options.Friendcheck and lplayer:IsFriendsWith(plr.UserId) then
											Name.Text = string.format('(<font color="rgb(%d, %d, %d)">F</font>) %s [%d]%s%s', ESP.Options.FriendcheckRGB.R * 255, ESP.Options.FriendcheckRGB.G * 255, ESP.Options.FriendcheckRGB.B * 255, plr.Name, math.floor(Dist), armorText, wowTag)
										else
											Name.Text = string.format('(<font color="rgb(%d, %d, %d)">E</font>) %s [%d]%s%s', 255, 0, 0, plr.Name, math.floor(Dist), armorText, wowTag)
										end
										Name.Visible = ESP.Drawing.Names.Enabled
									end
								end
							end

							do
								Weapon.Text = "none"
								Weapon.Visible = ESP.Drawing.Weapons.Enabled
							end
						else
							HideESP()
						end
					else
						HideESP()
					end
				else
					HideESP()
				end
			end)
		end
		coroutine.wrap(Updater)()
	end

	for _, v in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if v.Name ~= lplayer.Name then
			coroutine.wrap(CreateESP)(v)
		end
	end

	Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(v)
		coroutine.wrap(CreateESP)(v)
	end)
end

do
	getgenv().EspDrone = getgenv().EspDrone or false
	getgenv().EspVehicle = getgenv().EspVehicle or false
	getgenv().EspMineral = getgenv().EspMineral or false

	CoreGui2 = CoreGui
	function getRoot2(char)
		return char and (char.PrimaryPart or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChildOfClass("BasePart"))
	end

	function makeEntityESP()
		local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
		sg.Name = "OhioEntityESP"
		sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
		sg.IgnoreGuiInset = true
		sg.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
		sg.Parent = CoreGui2
		local holder = Instance.new("Folder")
		holder.Name = "Holder"
		holder.Parent = sg
		local espMap = {}
		return sg, holder, espMap
	end
	sg, holder, espMap = makeEntityESP()

	function createEntityMark(target, text, color)
		if espMap[target] then
			espMap[target].Label.Text = text
			espMap[target].Label.TextColor3 = color or espMap[target].Color
			espMap[target].Color = color or espMap[target].Color
			return espMap[target]
		end
		local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
		bb.Name = "EntityESP"
		bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
		bb.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(140, 24)
		bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)
		bb.Adornee = target
		bb.Parent = holder
		local label = Instance.new("TextLabel", bb)
		label.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
		label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		label.Text = text
		label.TextColor3 = color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
		label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
		label.Font = Enum.Font.Code
		label.TextSize = ESP.FontSize
		label.RichText = true
		local hi = Instance.new("Highlight")
		hi.Name = "EntityESP"
		hi.Adornee = target
		hi.FillTransparency = 0.6
		hi.OutlineTransparency = 0
		hi.OutlineColor = color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)
		hi.FillColor = color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)
		hi.Parent = holder
		espMap[target] = { Billboard = bb, Label = label, Highlight = hi, Color = color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), Text = text }
		return espMap[target]
	end

	function clearEntityMark(target)
		local e = espMap[target]
		if e then
			pcall(function() e.Billboard:Destroy() end)
			pcall(function() e.Highlight:Destroy() end)
			espMap[target] = nil
		end
	end

	function visible(t)
		if not t then return false end
		if t:IsA("BasePart") then
			return t.Parent ~= nil
		end
		return t.Parent ~= nil and t.PrimaryPart ~= nil
	end

	function getMineralColor(rock)
		if not rock then return Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0) end
		local color
		pcall(function()
			local parent = rock.Parent
			local attr = parent and (parent:GetAttribute("oreColor") or parent:GetAttribute("color"))
			if attr then color = attr end
		end)
		if not color then
			pcall(function()
				if rock:IsA("BasePart") then
					color = rock.Color
				elseif rock.PrimaryPart then
					color = rock.PrimaryPart.Color
				end
			end)
		end
		return color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		while true do
			task.wait(0.1)
			local any = getgenv().EspDrone or getgenv().EspVehicle or getgenv().EspMineral
			if not any then
				for t, _ in pairs(espMap) do
					clearEntityMark(t)
				end
				continue
			end
			local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
			if not cam then continue end
			local viewport = cam.ViewportSize

			local function updateMark(adornee, txt, color)
				if not espMap[adornee] then
					createEntityMark(adornee, txt, color)
				else
					espMap[adornee].Label.Text = txt
				end
				local e = espMap[adornee]
				local pos = adornee:IsA("BasePart") and adornee.Position
					or (adornee.PrimaryPart and adornee.PrimaryPart.Position)
					or (adornee:IsA("Model") and adornee:GetPivot().Position)
				if pos then
					local rawDist = (cam.CFrame.Position - pos).Magnitude
					local dist = rawDist / 3.5714285714
					local maxD = ESP.MaxDistance or 200
					local alpha = math.clamp(1 - (dist / maxD), 0.35, 1)
					local textSize = math.max(8, math.floor(ESP.FontSize * (0.5 + 0.5 * alpha)))
					local fade = math.clamp(1 - alpha, 0, 0.6)
					e.Label.TextSize = textSize
					e.Label.TextTransparency = fade
					e.Label.Visible = true
					e.Highlight.OutlineTransparency = fade
				end
			end

			if getgenv().EspDrone then
				local drones = workspace.Game and workspace.Game.Drones
				if drones then
					for _, d in pairs(drones:GetChildren()) do
						if d:IsA("Model") and visible(d) then
							local txt = d.Name .. " [无人机]"
							local root = getRoot2(d)
							local adornee = root or d
							updateMark(adornee, txt, Color3.fromRGB(135, 206, 235))
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if getgenv().EspVehicle then
				local vehFolder = workspace.Game and workspace.Game.Entities and workspace.Game.Entities:FindFirstChild("vehicles")
				if vehFolder then
					for _, v in pairs(vehFolder:GetChildren()) do
						if v:IsA("Model") and visible(v) then
							local txt = v.Name .. " [载具]"
							local root = getRoot2(v)
							local adornee = root or v
							updateMark(adornee, txt, Color3.fromRGB(255, 170, 0))
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if getgenv().EspMineral then
				for _, prompt in CollectionService:GetTagged("MinePrompt") do
					if not prompt:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then continue end
					local rock = prompt.Parent
					if not rock then continue end
					local oreName = rock.Parent and rock.Parent:GetAttribute("oreName") or "矿物"
					local txt = tostring(oreName) .. " [矿]"
					local color = getMineralColor(rock)
					updateMark(rock, txt, color)
				end
			end

			for t, _ in pairs(espMap) do
				if not visible(t) then
					clearEntityMark(t)
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end

do
	getgenv().vflyEnabled = getgenv().vflyEnabled or false
	getgenv().vehicleflyspeed = getgenv().vehicleflyspeed or 1
	getgenv().QEfly = getgenv().QEfly or true
	vflyKeyDown = nil
	vflyKeyUp = nil

	function getRoot(char)
		if char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
			return char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid").RootPart
		end
		return nil
	end

	function vflyStop()
		getgenv().vflyEnabled = false
		if vflyKeyDown then pcall(function() vflyKeyDown:Disconnect() end) vflyKeyDown = nil end
		if vflyKeyUp then pcall(function() vflyKeyUp:Disconnect() end) vflyKeyUp = nil end
		local char = LP.Character
		if char then
			local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if humanoid then
				humanoid.PlatformStand = false
			end
		end
		pcall(function()
			workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
		end)
	end

	function vflyStart()
		local char = LP.Character or LP.CharacterAdded:Wait()
		local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if not humanoid then
			repeat task.wait() until char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		end

		if vflyKeyDown or vflyKeyUp then
			pcall(function() vflyKeyDown:Disconnect() end)
			pcall(function() vflyKeyUp:Disconnect() end)
			vflyKeyDown = nil
			vflyKeyUp = nil
		end

		local T = getRoot(char)
		if not T then return end
		local CONTROL = { F = 0, B = 0, L = 0, R = 0, Q = 0, E = 0 }
		local lCONTROL = { F = 0, B = 0, L = 0, R = 0, Q = 0, E = 0 }
		local SPEED = 0
		local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

		local BG = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
		local BV = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
		BG.P = 9e4
		BG.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
		BG.CFrame = T.CFrame
		BG.Parent = T
		BV.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
		BV.MaxForce = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
		BV.Parent = T

		getgenv().vflyEnabled = true

		task.spawn(function()
			repeat
				task.wait()
				if not getgenv().vflyEnabled then break end
				camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
				if CONTROL.L + CONTROL.R ~= 0 or CONTROL.F + CONTROL.B ~= 0 or CONTROL.Q + CONTROL.E ~= 0 then
					SPEED = 50
				elseif SPEED ~= 0 then
					SPEED = 0
				end
				if (CONTROL.L + CONTROL.R) ~= 0 or (CONTROL.F + CONTROL.B) ~= 0 or (CONTROL.Q + CONTROL.E) ~= 0 then
					BV.Velocity = ((camera.CFrame.LookVector * (CONTROL.F + CONTROL.B)) + ((camera.CFrame * CFrame.new(CONTROL.L + CONTROL.R, (CONTROL.F + CONTROL.B + CONTROL.Q + CONTROL.E) * 0.2, 0).p) - camera.CFrame.p)) * SPEED
					lCONTROL = { F = CONTROL.F, B = CONTROL.B, L = CONTROL.L, R = CONTROL.R }
				elseif SPEED ~= 0 then
					BV.Velocity = ((camera.CFrame.LookVector * (lCONTROL.F + lCONTROL.B)) + ((camera.CFrame * CFrame.new(lCONTROL.L + lCONTROL.R, (lCONTROL.F + lCONTROL.B + CONTROL.Q + CONTROL.E) * 0.2, 0).p) - camera.CFrame.p)) * SPEED
				else
					BV.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
				end
				BG.CFrame = camera.CFrame
			until not getgenv().vflyEnabled
			CONTROL = { F = 0, B = 0, L = 0, R = 0, Q = 0, E = 0 }
			lCONTROL = { F = 0, B = 0, L = 0, R = 0, Q = 0, E = 0 }
			SPEED = 0
			BG:Destroy()
			BV:Destroy()
		end)

		vflyKeyDown = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
			if processed then return end
			local speed = getgenv().vehicleflyspeed or 1
			if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.W then
				CONTROL.F = speed
			elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.S then
				CONTROL.B = -speed
			elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.A then
				CONTROL.L = -speed
			elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.D then
				CONTROL.R = speed
			elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.E and getgenv().QEfly then
				CONTROL.Q = speed * 2
			elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Q and getgenv().QEfly then
				CONTROL.E = -speed * 2
			end
			pcall(function() camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Track end)
		end)

		vflyKeyUp = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input, processed)
			if processed then return end
			if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.W then
				CONTROL.F = 0
			elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.S then
				CONTROL.B = 0
			elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.A then
				CONTROL.L = 0
			elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.D then
				CONTROL.R = 0
			elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.E then
				CONTROL.Q = 0
			elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Q then
				CONTROL.E = 0
			end
		end)
	end

end

gunPlayers = {}

function refreshGunPlayers()
	gunPlayers = {}
	for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if p ~= LP then
			table.insert(gunPlayers, p.Name)
		end
	end
	table.sort(gunPlayers)
end

refreshGunPlayers()

if config.skins.autoUnlock then
	pcall(function()
		local n = applySkinsUnlock()
		print("[SkinUnlock] auto unlocked " .. tostring(n) .. " combos")
	end)
end

getgenv().OhioExtra = getgenv().OhioExtra or {}

UE = getgenv().OhioExtra
UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
TS = game:GetService("TweenService")
DB = game:GetService("Debris")
HTP = game:GetService("HttpService")
StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")
CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

function OhioNotify(text)
	pcall(function()
		Notify(text, 3)
	end)
end

do
	Request = request or http_request or (syn and syn.request)
	TrackConfig = getgenv().AtomicTrackConfig or { userName = "", userId = nil }
	getgenv().AtomicTrackConfig = TrackConfig
	TarData = UE.TrackData or { status = nil, placeId = nil, gameId = nil, lastLocation = nil, userName = nil }
	UE.TrackData = TarData
	STATUS_NAMES = { [0] = "离线", [1] = "在线", [2] = "游戏中", [3] = "Studio 中" }
	trackBusy = false

	function TrackNotify(t, s, d)
		pcall(function()
			WindUI:Notify({ Title = tostring(t or "提示"), Content = tostring(s or ""), Duration = d or 3 })
		end)
	end

	function getStatusText()
		local statusName = STATUS_NAMES[TarData.status] or "未知"
		return string.format("状态: %s (%s)\n位置: %s\nPlaceId: %s\nJobId: %s", tostring(TarData.status or "nil"), statusName, TarData.lastLocation or "无", tostring(TarData.placeId or "nil"), tostring(TarData.gameId or "nil"))
	end

	function resetTargetData()
		TarData.status = nil
		TarData.placeId = nil
		TarData.gameId = nil
		TarData.lastLocation = nil
		TarData.userName = nil
		TrackConfig.userId = nil
	end

	function resolveUserId(userName)
		if not userName or userName == "" then
			TrackNotify("追踪服务器", "请填写目标用户名", 3)
			return nil
		end
		userName = userName:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
		local ok, userId = pcall(function()
			return Players:GetUserIdFromNameAsync(userName)
		end)
		if ok and userId and userId > 0 then
			TarData.userName = userName
			return userId
		end
		TrackNotify("追踪服务器", "未找到用户名: " .. userName, 4)
		return nil
	end

	function findUserPresence()
		if not Request then
			TrackNotify("追踪服务器", "当前环境不支持 HTTP 请求", 4)
			return false
		end
		if not TrackConfig.userId then return false end
		if getgenv().AtomicCookie == "" then
			TrackNotify("追踪服务器", "Cookie 未配置,无法查询在线状态", 4)
			return false
		end
		local headers = {
			["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
			["Cookie"] = ".ROBLOSECURITY=" .. getgenv().AtomicCookie,
		}
		local okResp, resp = pcall(function()
			return Request({
				Url = "https://presence.roblox.com/v1/presence/users",
				Method = "POST",
				Headers = headers,
				Body = HTP:JSONEncode({ userIds = { tonumber(TrackConfig.userId) } }),
			})
		end)
		if okResp and resp and resp.StatusCode == 200 then
			local okDec, data = pcall(function()
				return HTP:JSONDecode(resp.Body)
			end)
			if okDec and data and data.userPresences and #data.userPresences > 0 then
				for _, presence in ipairs(data.userPresences) do
					TarData.status = presence.userPresenceType
					TarData.placeId = presence.placeId
					TarData.gameId = presence.gameId
					TarData.lastLocation = presence.lastLocation
				end
				TrackNotify("追踪服务器", getStatusText(), 6)
				return true
			end
			TrackNotify("追踪服务器", "未找到玩家在线信息", 4)
		else
			TrackNotify("追踪服务器", "查询失败,错误码: " .. tostring(resp and resp.StatusCode or "nil") .. "(Cookie 可能过期)", 5)
		end
		return false
	end

	function findTrackedPlayer(userId, userName)
		local target = userId and Players:GetPlayerByUserId(tonumber(userId))
		if not target and userName and userName ~= "" then
			target = Players:FindFirstChild(userName)
		end
		return target
	end

	function teleportNearTrackedPlayer(userId, userName)
		local targetPlayer = nil
		for _ = 1, 60 do
			targetPlayer = findTrackedPlayer(userId, userName)
			if targetPlayer then break end
			task.wait(0.5)
		end
		if not targetPlayer then
			TrackNotify("追踪服务器", "已进入服务器,但未找到目标玩家", 4)
			return false
		end
		local char = LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
		local root = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 15)
		local targetChar = targetPlayer.Character or targetPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
		local targetRoot = targetChar:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 15)
		if not root or not targetRoot then
			TrackNotify("追踪服务器", "角色未加载完成,无法传送", 4)
			return false
		end
		root.CFrame = targetRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(3, 3, 0)
		TrackNotify("追踪服务器", "已传送到 " .. targetPlayer.Name .. " 旁边", 4)
		return true
	end

	function tryCompletePendingTrack()
		local pending = getgenv().AtomicPendingTrack
		if not pending then return end
		local matchPlace = not pending.placeId or pending.placeId == game.PlaceId
		local matchJob = not pending.gameId or pending.gameId == game.JobId
		if not (matchPlace and matchJob) then return end
		task.spawn(function()
			task.wait(2)
			if teleportNearTrackedPlayer(pending.userId, pending.userName) then
				getgenv().AtomicPendingTrack = nil
			end
		end)
	end

	function teleportToTarget()
		local placeId = TarData.placeId
		local gameJobId = TarData.gameId
		local status = TarData.status
		local targetUserId = TrackConfig.userId
		local targetUserName = TarData.userName or TrackConfig.userName
		if placeId and gameJobId then
			if game.PlaceId == placeId and game.JobId == gameJobId then
				TrackNotify("追踪服务器", "已在目标服务器,正在传送到玩家旁边...", 3)
				task.spawn(function()
					teleportNearTrackedPlayer(targetUserId, targetUserName)
				end)
				return
			end
			getgenv().AtomicPendingTrack = {
				userId = targetUserId,
				userName = targetUserName,
				placeId = placeId,
				gameId = gameJobId,
			}
			TrackNotify("追踪服务器", "正在传送... PlaceId: " .. tostring(placeId), 4)
			pcall(function()
				game:GetService("TeleportService"):TeleportToPlaceInstance(placeId, gameJobId, LocalPlayer)
			end)
			return
		end
		if status == 2 and not placeId then
			TrackNotify("追踪服务器", "玩家在游戏中,但 placeId/gameId 被隐私隐藏", 6)
		elseif status == 1 then
			TrackNotify("追踪服务器", "玩家在线但不在游戏里", 4)
		elseif status == 0 or status == nil then
			TrackNotify("追踪服务器", "玩家离线或未找到", 4)
		elseif status == 3 then
			TrackNotify("追踪服务器", "玩家在 Studio 中,无法传送", 4)
		else
			TrackNotify("追踪服务器", "无法传送,请先查询玩家状态", 4)
		end
	end

	function startTrack(userName)
		userName = (userName or ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
		if userName == "" then
			TrackNotify("追踪服务器", "请填写目标用户名", 3)
			return
		end
		if trackBusy then return end
		trackBusy = true
		task.spawn(function()
			resetTargetData()
			TrackConfig.userName = userName
			TrackNotify("追踪服务器", "正在获取玩家信息...", 3)
			TrackConfig.userId = resolveUserId(userName)
			if TrackConfig.userId and findUserPresence() then
				teleportToTarget()
			end
			trackBusy = false
		end)
	end

	task.defer(tryCompletePendingTrack)

	getgenv().AtomicCookie = getgenv().AtomicCookie or ""
	getgenv().AtomicTrackConfig = TrackConfig

end
do

end

do
	getgenv().autoCraftEnabled = getgenv().autoCraftEnabled or false

end

do
	getgenv().bananaEnabled = getgenv().bananaEnabled or false
	getgenv().bananaThrowId = nil
	getgenv().bananaCurrentTarget = nil
	getgenv().bananaActiveThreads = {}
	getgenv().bananaCircleEnabled = false
	getgenv().bananaSquareEnabled = false

	function BananaNotify(title, text, duration)
		pcall(function()
			WindUI:Notify({ Title = tostring(title or "提示"), Content = tostring(text or ""), Duration = duration or 5 })
		end)
	end

	function FindBananaNearestUserId()
		local char = LocalPlayer.Character
		local origin = char and char.PrimaryPart and char.PrimaryPart.Position
		if not origin then return nil end
		local nearest, best = nil, 1000
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if p ~= LocalPlayer and p.Character then
				local prim = p.Character.PrimaryPart
				if prim then
					local d = (prim.Position - origin).Magnitude
					if d < best then
						nearest, best = p.UserId, d
					end
				end
			end
		end
		return nearest
	end

	function GetBananaHitPart(char)
		return char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char.PrimaryPart
	end

	function BananaStompAura()
		local char = LocalPlayer.Character
		local origin = char and char.PrimaryPart and char.PrimaryPart.Position
		if not origin then return end
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if p ~= LocalPlayer and p.Character and p.Character.PrimaryPart then
				local d = (p.Character.PrimaryPart.Position - origin).Magnitude
				if d <= 25 and CR.Check(p, { knocked = true, carried = false }) then
					pcall(function()
						return Signal.FireServer("finish", p)
					end)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	function BananaThrowForId()
		local item = v3item.inventory.getFromName("Banana Peel")
		local guid = item and item.guid
		if not guid then return nil end
		local char = LocalPlayer.Character
		if not char then return nil end
		local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char.PrimaryPart
		if not hrp then return nil end
		local hand = char:FindFirstChild("RightHand")
		local origin = hand and hand.Position or hrp.Position
		local dir = hrp.CFrame.LookVector
		local clientId = GUID()
		local velocity = dir * 120
		local endPos = origin + dir * 5
		local ok, valid, serverId = pcall(function()
			return Signal.InvokeServer("throwSticky", clientId, "Banana Peel", guid, velocity, endPos)
		end)
		if not ok or valid == false then
			return nil
		end
		return serverId or clientId
	end

	function BananaDoThrow()
		if getgenv().bananaThrowId then return end
		local bItem = v3item.inventory.getFromName("Banana Peel")
		local guid = bItem and bItem.guid
		if not guid then return end
		local uid = FindBananaNearestUserId()
		if not uid then return end
		local targetPlayer = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(uid)
		local targetPrim = targetPlayer and targetPlayer.Character and targetPlayer.Character.PrimaryPart
		if not targetPrim then return end
		local char = LocalPlayer.Character
		if not char then return end
		local hand = char:FindFirstChild("RightHand")
		local origin = hand and hand.Position or char.PrimaryPart.Position
		local dir = (targetPrim.Position - origin).Unit
		local clientId = GUID()
		local velocity = dir * 120
		local endPos = targetPrim.Position + dir * 2
		local ok, valid, serverId = pcall(function()
			return Signal.InvokeServer("throwSticky", clientId, "Banana Peel", guid, velocity, endPos)
		end)
		if not ok or valid == false then
			return
		end
		getgenv().bananaThrowId = serverId or clientId
	end

	function EnsureBananaHitThreads()
		if not getgenv().bananaThrowId then return end
		local alive = {}
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if p ~= LocalPlayer and p.Character then
				alive[p.UserId] = true
				if not getgenv().bananaActiveThreads[p.UserId] then
					getgenv().bananaActiveThreads[p.UserId] = true
					task.spawn(function()
						while getgenv().bananaActiveThreads[p.UserId] and p.Character and p.Character.Parent do
							local hitPart = GetBananaHitPart(p.Character)
							if hitPart then
								local worldCF = hitPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0.5, 0)
								pcall(function()
									return Signal.InvokeServer("hitSticky", getgenv().bananaThrowId, hitPart, worldCF:ToObjectSpace(hitPart.CFrame), worldCF)
								end)
							end
							task.wait(0.01)
						end
						getgenv().bananaActiveThreads[p.UserId] = nil
					end)
				end
			end
		end
		for pid in getgenv().bananaActiveThreads do
			if not alive[pid] then
				getgenv().bananaActiveThreads[pid] = nil
			end
		end
		local uid = FindBananaNearestUserId()
		if not uid then
			getgenv().bananaCurrentTarget = nil
			return
		end
		if getgenv().bananaCurrentTarget == uid then return end
		getgenv().bananaCurrentTarget = uid
		for i = 1, 8 do
			task.spawn(function()
				task.wait((i - 1) * 0.01)
				while getgenv().bananaCurrentTarget == uid do
					local p = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(uid)
					local char = p and p.Character
					if not (char and char.Parent) then break end
					local hitPart = GetBananaHitPart(char)
					if hitPart then
						local worldCF = hitPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0.5, 0)
						pcall(function()
							return Signal.InvokeServer("hitSticky", getgenv().bananaThrowId, hitPart, worldCF:ToObjectSpace(hitPart.CFrame), worldCF)
						end)
					end
					task.wait(0.01)
				end
			end)
		end
	end

	function GetBananaTargetChar()
		local char = LocalPlayer.Character
		local origin = char and char.PrimaryPart and char.PrimaryPart.Position
		if not origin then return nil end
		local nearest, best = nil, 1000
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if p ~= LocalPlayer and p.Character and p.Character.PrimaryPart then
				local d = (p.Character.PrimaryPart.Position - origin).Magnitude
				if d < best then
					nearest, best = p.Character, d
				end
			end
		end
		return nearest
	end

	function FormationHitCircle()
		local targetChar = GetBananaTargetChar()
		local hitPart = targetChar and GetBananaHitPart(targetChar)
		if not hitPart or not getgenv().bananaThrowId then return end
		local center = hitPart.Position
		local radius = 4
		local count = 16
		for i = 1, count do
			local angle = (i - 1) * (math.pi * 2 / count)
			local offset = Vector3.new(math.cos(angle) * radius, 0, math.sin(angle) * radius)
			local worldCF = CFrame.new(center + offset) * CFrame.Angles(0, angle, 0)
			pcall(function()
				return Signal.InvokeServer("hitSticky", getgenv().bananaThrowId, hitPart, worldCF:ToObjectSpace(hitPart.CFrame), worldCF)
			end)
		end
	end

	function FormationHitSquare()
		local targetChar = GetBananaTargetChar()
		local hitPart = targetChar and GetBananaHitPart(targetChar)
		if not hitPart or not getgenv().bananaThrowId then return end
		local center = hitPart.Position
		local half = 3
		local points = {
			Vector3.new(-half, 0, -half), Vector3.new(half, 0, -half),
			Vector3.new(half, 0, half), Vector3.new(-half, 0, half),
			Vector3.new(-half, 0, 0), Vector3.new(half, 0, 0),
			Vector3.new(0, 0, -half), Vector3.new(0, 0, half),
			Vector3.new(-half * 0.7, 0, -half * 0.7), Vector3.new(half * 0.7, 0, -half * 0.7),
			Vector3.new(half * 0.7, 0, half * 0.7), Vector3.new(-half * 0.7, 0, half * 0.7),
		}
		for _, offset in ipairs(points) do
			local worldCF = CFrame.new(center + offset)
			pcall(function()
				return Signal.InvokeServer("hitSticky", getgenv().bananaThrowId, hitPart, worldCF:ToObjectSpace(hitPart.CFrame), worldCF)
			end)
		end
	end

end

do
	getgenv().autoRetaliateEnabled = getgenv().autoRetaliateEnabled or false
	getgenv().retaliationActive = false
	getgenv().retaliationStopFlag = false
	getgenv().retaliationDeathCount = 0
	getgenv().retaliateThrowId = nil
	getgenv().retaliateMethod = getgenv().retaliateMethod or "飞镖"

	function StopRetaliation()
		getgenv().retaliationStopFlag = true
		getgenv().retaliationActive = false
	end

	function FindTargetUserId()
		local char = LocalPlayer.Character
		local origin = char and char.PrimaryPart and char.PrimaryPart.Position
		if not origin then return nil end
		local nearest, best = nil, 1000
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if p ~= LocalPlayer and p.Character then
				local prim = p.Character.PrimaryPart
				if prim then
					local d = (prim.Position - origin).Magnitude
					if d < best then
						nearest, best = p.UserId, d
					end
				end
			end
		end
		return nearest
	end

	function GetHitPart(char)
		return char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char.PrimaryPart
	end

	function RetaliateStompAura()
		local char = LocalPlayer.Character
		local origin = char and char.PrimaryPart and char.PrimaryPart.Position
		if not origin then return end
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if p ~= LocalPlayer and p.Character and p.Character.PrimaryPart then
				local d = (p.Character.PrimaryPart.Position - origin).Magnitude
				if d <= 25 and CR.Check(p, { knocked = true, carried = false }) then
					pcall(function()
						return Signal.FireServer("finish", p)
					end)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	function RetaliateThrowId()
		local item = v3item.inventory.getFromName("Ninja Star")
		local guid = item and item.guid
		if not guid then return nil end
		local char = LocalPlayer.Character
		if not char then return nil end
		local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char.PrimaryPart
		if not hrp then return nil end
		local hand = char:FindFirstChild("RightHand")
		local origin = hand and hand.Position or hrp.Position
		local dir = hrp.CFrame.LookVector
		local clientId = GUID()
		local velocity = dir * 120
		local endPos = origin + dir * 5
		local ok, valid, serverId = pcall(function()
			return Signal.InvokeServer("throwSticky", clientId, "Ninja Star", guid, velocity, endPos)
		end)
		if not ok or valid == false then
			return nil
		end
		return serverId or clientId
	end

	function StartRetaliation(attacker)
		if not getgenv().autoRetaliateEnabled then return end
		if getgenv().retaliationActive then return end
		if not (attacker and attacker.Parent) then return end

		local method = getgenv().retaliateMethod or "飞镖"

		if method == "拳击" then
			pcall(ensureFists)
			local fistsG = nil
			local item = v3item.inventory.getFromName("Fists")
			if item then fistsG = item.guid end
			if not fistsG then
				fistsG = (v3item.inventory.getFromName("Fists") or {}).guid
			end
			if not fistsG then return end

			getgenv().retaliationActive = true
			getgenv().retaliationStopFlag = false
			local targetName = attacker.Name
			local endTime = os.clock() + 10

			task.spawn(function()
				while not getgenv().retaliationStopFlag and os.clock() < endTime and attacker.Parent do
					local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
					local targetChar = attacker.Character
					if myChar and targetChar and targetChar.PrimaryPart then
						local targetPos = targetChar.PrimaryPart.Position
						pcall(function()
							myChar:PivotTo(CFrame.new(targetPos + Vector3.new(0, 30, 0)))
						end)
					end
					RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
				end
			end)

			for i = 1, 8 do
				task.spawn(function()
					task.wait((i - 1) * 0.01)
					while not getgenv().retaliationStopFlag and os.clock() < endTime and attacker.Parent do
						local char = attacker.Character
						if char and char.Parent then
							pcall(function()
								Signal.FireServer("equip", fistsG)
							end)
							pcall(function()
								Signal.FireServer("attackOfMeleeSwing", "meleemegapunch")
							end)
							pcall(function()
								Signal.FireServer("attackMeleeHit", "player", {
									hitPlayerId = attacker.UserId,
									meleeType = "meleemegapunch",
									weaponGUID = fistsG,
								})
							end)
						end
						task.wait(0.01)
					end
				end)
			end

			task.spawn(function()
				while not getgenv().retaliationStopFlag and os.clock() < endTime and attacker.Parent do
					RetaliateStompAura()
					task.wait(1)
				end
			end)

			task.spawn(function()
				while os.clock() < endTime and attacker.Parent and not getgenv().retaliationStopFlag do
					task.wait(0.2)
				end
				StopRetaliation()
				StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", { Title = "自动反击", Text = "反击已结束(拳击)\n反击用户: " .. targetName, Duration = 5 })
			end)
			return
		end

		if not getgenv().retaliateThrowId then
			getgenv().retaliateThrowId = RetaliateThrowId()
		end
		if not getgenv().retaliateThrowId then return end

		getgenv().retaliationActive = true
		getgenv().retaliationStopFlag = false
		getgenv().retaliationDeathCount = 0
		local targetName = attacker.Name
		local endTime = os.clock() + 10
		local throwId = getgenv().retaliateThrowId

		task.spawn(function()
			while not getgenv().retaliationStopFlag and os.clock() < endTime and attacker.Parent do
				local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
				local targetChar = attacker.Character
				if myChar and targetChar and targetChar.PrimaryPart then
					local targetPos = targetChar.PrimaryPart.Position
					pcall(function()
						myChar:PivotTo(CFrame.new(targetPos + Vector3.new(0, 30, 0)))
					end)
				end
				RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			end
		end)

		for i = 1, 8 do
			task.spawn(function()
				task.wait((i - 1) * 0.01)
				while not getgenv().retaliationStopFlag and os.clock() < endTime and attacker.Parent do
					local char = attacker.Character
					if char and char.Parent then
						local hitPart = GetHitPart(char)
						if hitPart then
							local worldCF = hitPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0.5, 0)
							pcall(function()
								return Signal.InvokeServer("hitSticky", throwId, hitPart, worldCF:ToObjectSpace(hitPart.CFrame), worldCF)
							end)
						end
					end
					task.wait(0.01)
				end
			end)
		end

		task.spawn(function()
			while not getgenv().retaliationStopFlag and os.clock() < endTime and attacker.Parent do
				RetaliateStompAura()
				task.wait(1)
			end
		end)

		task.spawn(function()
			while os.clock() < endTime and attacker.Parent and not getgenv().retaliationStopFlag do
				task.wait(0.2)
			end
			StopRetaliation()
			StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", { Title = "自动反击", Text = "反击已结束\n反击用户: " .. targetName, Duration = 5 })
		end)
	end

	meleeTypes2 = {
		meleepunch = true,
		meleemegapunch = true,
		meleekick = true,
		meleejumpKick = true,
		meleeswing = true,
		meleemegaswing = true,
	}

	lastHealth2 = nil
	lastHurtTime2 = -10
	lastMeleeTime2 = -10
	lastMeleeAttacker2 = nil

	function OnAttackedBy2(attackerPlayer)
		if not getgenv().autoRetaliateEnabled then return end
		if attackerPlayer == nil or attackerPlayer == LocalPlayer then return end
		StartRetaliation(attackerPlayer)
	end

	function watchCharacter2(char)
		local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
		lastHealth2 = hum.Health
		hum.HealthChanged:Connect(function(newHealth)
			local diff = lastHealth2 - newHealth
			lastHealth2 = newHealth
			if diff > 0.5 then
				lastHurtTime2 = os.clock()
				if lastMeleeAttacker2 and os.clock() - lastMeleeTime2 <= 1 then
					OnAttackedBy2(lastMeleeAttacker2)
					lastMeleeAttacker2 = nil
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	if LocalPlayer.Character then
		watchCharacter2(LocalPlayer.Character)
	end
	LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(watchCharacter2)

	for _, remote in ipairs(ReplicatedStorage.devv.remoteStorage:GetChildren()) do
		if remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
			pcall(function()
				remote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(...)
					local a, b, c = ...
					if typeof(a) == "Instance" and a:IsA("Player") and typeof(b) == "string" and meleeTypes2[b] then
						local hitUs = false
						if c == LocalPlayer or c == LocalPlayer.UserId then
							hitUs = true
						elseif typeof(c) == "Instance" then
							if LocalPlayer.Character and (c == LocalPlayer.Character or c:IsDescendantOf(LocalPlayer.Character)) then
								hitUs = true
							end
						end
						if hitUs then
							OnAttackedBy2(a)
						else
							lastMeleeTime2 = os.clock()
							lastMeleeAttacker2 = a
							if os.clock() - lastHurtTime2 <= 1 then
								OnAttackedBy2(a)
								lastMeleeAttacker2 = nil
							end
						end
					elseif typeof(c) == "Instance" and c:IsA("Player") and typeof(a) == "string" and typeof(b) == "table" then
						OnAttackedBy2(c)
					elseif typeof(a) == "Vector3" then
						local myPos = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character.PrimaryPart and LocalPlayer.Character.PrimaryPart.Position
						local best
						if myPos then
							local bestDist
							for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
								if p ~= LocalPlayer and p.Character and p.Character.PrimaryPart then
									local d = (p.Character.PrimaryPart.Position - a).Magnitude
									if d <= 20 and (not best or d < bestDist) then
										best = p
										bestDist = d
									end
								end
							end
						end
						if best then
							OnAttackedBy2(best)
						end
					end
				end)
			end)
		end
	end

end

do
	getgenv().hitLogEnabled = false
	getgenv().hurtLogEnabled = false
	hitLogGui = nil
	hurtLogGui = nil
	lastCache = {}
	shooters3 = {}
	lastHealth3 = nil
	lastHurtTime3 = -10
	lastMeleeTime3 = -10
	lastMeleeName3 = nil

	meleeTypes3 = {
		meleepunch = true,
		meleemegapunch = true,
		meleekick = true,
		meleejumpKick = true,
		meleeswing = true,
		meleemegaswing = true,
	}

	function createLogGui()
		local screenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
		screenGui.Name = "HitNotifier"
		screenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
		screenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
		screenGui.Parent = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 10)
		local mainFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
		mainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -80)
		mainFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
		mainFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		mainFrame.Parent = screenGui
		local mainLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", mainFrame)
		mainLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical
		mainLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 3)
		mainLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left
		mainLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom
		mainLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
		return screenGui, mainFrame
	end

	function createHitFrame(text, color)
		local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
		frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
		frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
		frame.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY
		local padding = Instance.new("UIPadding", frame)
		padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 6)
		padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6)
		padding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 2)
		padding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 2)
		local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		label.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
		label.TextSize = 18
		label.TextColor3 = color or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
		label.Text = text
		label.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY
		label.Parent = frame
		return frame
	end

	function showHitLog(text)
		if not getgenv().hitLogEnabled then return end
		if lastCache[text] then return end
		lastCache[text] = true
		if not hitLogGui then
			local gui, frame = createLogGui()
			hitLogGui = { gui = gui, frame = frame }
		end
		local hitFrame = createHitFrame(text, Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
		hitFrame.Parent = hitLogGui.frame
		local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(2)
		local frameTween = TS:Create(hitFrame, tweenInfo, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
		local textTween = TS:Create(hitFrame:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel"), tweenInfo, { TextTransparency = 1, TextStrokeTransparency = 1 })
		frameTween:Play()
		textTween:Play()
		frameTween.Completed:Connect(function()
			hitFrame:Destroy()
			lastCache[text] = nil
		end)
	end

	function showHurtLog(text)
		if not getgenv().hurtLogEnabled then return end
		if lastCache[text] then return end
		lastCache[text] = true
		if not hurtLogGui then
			local gui, frame = createLogGui()
			hurtLogGui = { gui = gui, frame = frame }
		end
		local hurtFrame = createHitFrame(text, Color3.new(1, 0, 0))
		hurtFrame.Parent = hurtLogGui.frame
		local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(2)
		local frameTween = TS:Create(hurtFrame, tweenInfo, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
		local textTween = TS:Create(hurtFrame:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel"), tweenInfo, { TextTransparency = 1, TextStrokeTransparency = 1 })
		frameTween:Play()
		textTween:Play()
		frameTween.Completed:Connect(function()
			hurtFrame:Destroy()
			lastCache[text] = nil
		end)
	end

	function watchCharacter3(char)
		local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
		lastHealth3 = hum.Health
		hum.HealthChanged:Connect(function(newHealth)
			local diff = lastHealth3 - newHealth
			lastHealth3 = newHealth
			if diff > 0.5 then
				lastHurtTime3 = os.clock()
				if lastMeleeName3 and os.clock() - lastMeleeTime3 <= 1 then
					showHurtLog("被近战攻击: " .. lastMeleeName3 .. " 伤害 " .. string.format("%.1f", diff))
					lastMeleeName3 = nil
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	if LocalPlayer.Character then
		watchCharacter3(LocalPlayer.Character)
	end
	LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(watchCharacter3)

	lastPrinted = ""
	lastPrintTime = -10

	function printHurt(tag, name, extra)
		local now = os.clock()
		if name == lastPrinted and now - lastPrintTime < 1.5 then
			return
		end
		lastPrinted = name
		lastPrintTime = now
		showHurtLog(tag .. name .. (extra and (" " .. extra) or ""))
	end

	for _, remote in ipairs(ReplicatedStorage.devv.remoteStorage:GetChildren()) do
		if remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
			pcall(function()
				remote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(...)
					local a, b, c = ...
					if typeof(a) == "Instance" and a:IsA("Player") and typeof(b) == "string" and meleeTypes3[b] then
						local hitUs = false
						if c == LocalPlayer or c == LocalPlayer.UserId then
							hitUs = true
						elseif typeof(c) == "Instance" then
							if c == LocalPlayer.Character or c:IsDescendantOf(LocalPlayer.Character) then
								hitUs = true
							end
						end
						if hitUs then
							printHurt("被近战攻击: ", a.Name, "招式=" .. b)
						else
							lastMeleeTime3 = os.clock()
							lastMeleeName3 = a.Name
							if os.clock() - lastHurtTime3 <= 1 then
								printHurt("被近战攻击: ", a.Name, "招式=" .. b)
								lastMeleeName3 = nil
							end
						end
					elseif typeof(c) == "Instance" and c:IsA("Player") and typeof(a) == "string" and typeof(b) == "table" then
						shooters3[c.Name] = { t = os.clock(), weapon = a }
					elseif typeof(a) == "table" and typeof(a.player1) == "Instance" and a.player1:IsA("Player") then
						if a.player2 == LocalPlayer then
							printHurt("被攻击: ", a.player1.Name, "武器=" .. tostring(a.itemName) .. " 距离=" .. math.floor(a.distance or 0) .. "m")
						end
					elseif typeof(a) == "Vector3" then
						local myPos = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character.PrimaryPart and LocalPlayer.Character.PrimaryPart.Position
						local best, bestDist
						if myPos then
							for _, p in Players:GetPlayers() do
								if p ~= LocalPlayer and p.Character and p.Character.PrimaryPart then
									local d = (p.Character.PrimaryPart.Position - a).Magnitude
									if d <= 20 and (not best or d < bestDist) then
										best = p
										bestDist = d
									end
								end
							end
						end
						if best then
							local w = shooters3[best.Name] and shooters3[best.Name].weapon
							printHurt("被枪械攻击: ", best.Name, w and ("武器=" .. w) or nil)
						else
							local now = os.clock()
							local b2, b2n
							for name, info in pairs(shooters3) do
								if now - info.t <= 2 then
									if not b2 or info.t > b2.t then
										b2 = info
										b2n = name
									end
								end
							end
							if b2n then
								printHurt("被枪械攻击: ", b2n, "武器=" .. b2.weapon)
							end
						end
						shooters3 = {}
					end
				end)
			end)
		end
	end

end

		end
	end)

do
	getgenv().harassSettings = getgenv().harassSettings or {
		targetPlayer = nil,
		playerList = {},
		lastPlayerRefresh = 0,
		playerRefreshInterval = 5,
		tpMode = "后边",
		cycleAll = false,
		cycleIndex = 1,
		cycleDelay = 2,
	}
	harassSettings = getgenv().harassSettings

	function refreshPlayerList()
		local currentTime = os.time()
		if currentTime - harassSettings.lastPlayerRefresh < harassSettings.playerRefreshInterval then
			return harassSettings.playerList
		end
		harassSettings.lastPlayerRefresh = currentTime
		harassSettings.playerList = {}
		table.insert(harassSettings.playerList, { text = "All", value = "ALL" })
		for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if player ~= LocalPlayer then
				table.insert(harassSettings.playerList, { text = player.Name .. " (@" .. player.DisplayName .. ")", value = player.Name })
			end
		end
		table.sort(harassSettings.playerList, function(a, b)
			return a.text:lower() < b.text:lower()
		end)
		return harassSettings.playerList
	end

	function getPlayerListValues()
		refreshPlayerList()
		local values = {}
		for _, player in ipairs(harassSettings.playerList) do
			table.insert(values, player.text)
		end
		return values
	end

	function getOffset()
		local offset = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0)
		if harassSettings.tpMode == "前边" then
			offset = CFrame.new(0, 0, -2)
		elseif harassSettings.tpMode == "后边" then
			offset = CFrame.new(0, 0, 2)
		elseif harassSettings.tpMode == "左边" then
			offset = CFrame.new(-2, 0, 0)
		elseif harassSettings.tpMode == "右边" then
			offset = CFrame.new(2, 0, 0)
		elseif harassSettings.tpMode == "上面" then
			offset = CFrame.new(0, 2, 0)
		elseif harassSettings.tpMode == "下面" then
			offset = CFrame.new(0, -2, 0)
		end
		return offset
	end

end

do
	getgenv().AimbotConfig = getgenv().AimbotConfig or {
		Enabled = false,
		WallCheck = true,
		PredictAim = false,
		PredictValue = 1.5,
		AimPart = "Head",
		Radius = 200,
		AimMode = "Camera",
	}
	AimbotConfig = getgenv().AimbotConfig
	Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	aimConnection = nil
	aimSmooth = nil

	function hasShieldProtection(player)
		if not player or not player.Character then return false end
		local humanoid = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
		if not humanoid then return false end
		for _, desc in pairs(player.Character:GetDescendants()) do
			if desc:IsA("ForceField") or desc.Name:lower():find("shield") then
				return true
			end
		end
		return false
	end

	function getNearestTarget()
		local nearest = nil
		local minDist = math.huge
		local center = Vector2.new(Camera.ViewportSize.X / 2, Camera.ViewportSize.Y / 2)
		for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if plr == LocalPlayer then continue end
			local char = plr.Character
			if not char or not char:FindFirstChild(AimbotConfig.AimPart) or not char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then continue end
			if char.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then continue end
			if hasShieldProtection(plr) then continue end
			local worldPos = char[AimbotConfig.AimPart].Position
			local screenPos, onScreen = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(worldPos)
			local dist
			if AimbotConfig.AimMode == "Camera" then
				if not onScreen then continue end
				dist = (Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y) - center).Magnitude
				if dist > AimbotConfig.Radius then continue end
			else
				dist = (worldPos - Camera.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
			end
			if AimbotConfig.WallCheck then
				local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
				rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { LocalPlayer.Character }
				rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Blacklist
				local ray = workspace:Raycast(Camera.CFrame.Position, worldPos - Camera.CFrame.Position, rayParams)
				local canSee = ray and ray.Instance and ray.Instance:IsDescendantOf(char)
				if not canSee then continue end
			end
			if AimbotConfig.PredictAim then
				local part = char[AimbotConfig.AimPart]
				local vel = part.Velocity
				if vel.Magnitude > 1 then
					worldPos = worldPos + vel * AimbotConfig.PredictValue / 10
				end
			end
			if dist < minDist then
				minDist = dist
				nearest = { Part = char[AimbotConfig.AimPart], WorldPos = worldPos }
			end
		end
		return nearest
	end

end

	getgenv().selectedOpenCase = getgenv().selectedOpenCase or casesList[1]

do
	SETTINGS = {
		ENABLED = false,
		HITBOX_SIZE = 15,
		HITBOX_TRANSPARENCY = 0.7,
		TARGET_PART = "Head",
		RAINBOW_TRACE = false,
	}
	getgenv().RangeSETTINGS = SETTINGS

	Proj = require(ReplicatedStorage.devv.client.Objects.v3item.modules.projectiles.projectile)
	ProjMod = require(ReplicatedStorage.devv.client.Objects.v3item.modules.projectiles)
	oldHitreg = Proj.hitreg
	oldNewProj = ProjMod.newProjectileOfType

	function createTrace(cf)
		local startPos = cf.Position
		local targetPos = startPos + (cf.LookVector * 1000)
		local mag = (targetPos - startPos).Magnitude
		local P = Instance.new("Part")
		P.Name = "Trace"
		P.Anchored = true
		P.CanCollide = false
		P.CanTouch = false
		P.CanQuery = false
		P.CastShadow = false
		P.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
		P.Color = Color3.fromHSV(tick() % 1, 0.8, 1)
		P.Size = Vector3.new(0.15, 0.15, mag)
		P.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(startPos, targetPos) * CFrame.new(0, 0, -mag / 2)
		P.Parent = workspace
		local s = Instance.new("Sound", P)
		s.SoundId = "rbxassetid://5633695679"
		s:Play()
		TS:Create(P, TweenInfo.new(1), { Transparency = 1, Size = Vector3.new(0, 0, mag) }):Play()
		DB:AddItem(P, 1)
	end

	Proj.hitreg = function(self, hitPart, pos, normal, material)
		if SETTINGS.ENABLED and self.isPlayerOwned then
			local target, shortestDist = nil, SETTINGS.HITBOX_SIZE
			for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				local char = p ~= LocalPlayer and p.Character
				local head = char and char:FindFirstChild(SETTINGS.TARGET_PART)
				if head and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and char.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
					local dist = (pos - head.Position).Magnitude
					if dist < shortestDist then
						shortestDist, target = dist, head
					end
				end
			end
			if target then hitPart, pos = target, target.Position end
		end
		return oldHitreg(self, hitPart, pos, normal, material)
	end

	ProjMod.newProjectileOfType = function(pType, pData)
		local obj = oldNewProj(pType, pData)
		if obj and obj.instance then
			obj.instance.Transparency = 1
		end
		if SETTINGS.RAINBOW_TRACE and pData and pData.cframe and pData.Owner == LocalPlayer then
			createTrace(pData.cframe)
		end
		return obj
	end

	RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		if not SETTINGS.ENABLED then return end
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if p ~= LocalPlayer and p.Character then
				local hrp = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if hrp then
					hrp.Size = Vector3.new(SETTINGS.HITBOX_SIZE, SETTINGS.HITBOX_SIZE, SETTINGS.HITBOX_SIZE)
					hrp.Transparency = SETTINGS.HITBOX_TRANSPARENCY
					hrp.Color = Color3.new(1, 0, 0)
					hrp.CanCollide = false
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end

    ["8bit皇冠"] = "10159600649"
}

function EquipAccessoryOnCharacter(char, name)
    if not name then return end
    local id = AccessoryIDs[name]
    if not id then return end
    for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("Model") and child:FindFirstChild("Handle") then
            child:Destroy()
        end
    end
    local acc = game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://"..id)[1]
    if not acc then return end
    local handle = acc:FindFirstChild("Handle")
    if not handle then return end
    local A1 = handle:FindFirstChildOfClass("Attachment")
    if not A1 then return end
    local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if not head then return end
    local A0 = head:FindFirstChild(A1.Name, true)
    if not A0 then return end
    acc.Parent = char
    handle.Anchored = false
    handle.Massless = true
    handle.CFrame = A0.WorldCFrame * A1.CFrame:Inverse()
    local weld = Instance.new("WeldConstraint", handle)
    weld.Part0 = handle
    weld.Part1 = A0.Parent
end

end

end

task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(0.1)
        local enabled = false
        for _, group in ipairs(pickGroups) do
            if getgenv()["_pick_" .. group.flag] then
                enabled = true
                break
            end
        end
        if not enabled then continue end
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local rootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not rootPart then continue end
        local itemPickup = workspace.Game and workspace.Game.Entities and workspace.Game.Entities:FindFirstChild("ItemPickup")
        if not itemPickup then continue end
        for _, l in pairs(itemPickup:GetChildren()) do
            for _, v in pairs(l:GetChildren()) do
                if not (v:IsA("MeshPart") or v:IsA("Part")) then continue end
                local prompt = v:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt")
                if not prompt then continue end
                for _, group in ipairs(pickGroups) do
                    if getgenv()["_pick_" .. group.flag] and table.find(group.items, prompt.ObjectText) then
                        if getgenv().BlockRedCardRoom and (v.Position - getgenv().RedCardRoomPos).Magnitude <= getgenv().BlockRadius then continue end
                        rootPart.CFrame = v.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
                        prompt.RequiresLineOfSight = false
                        prompt.HoldDuration = 0
                        task.wait(0.05)
                        if fireproximityprompt then fireproximityprompt(prompt) else firesignal(prompt.Triggered) end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

customPickItems = {}
selectedCustomPick = ""

task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait()
        if not getgenv().CustomPickEnabled then continue end
        if #customPickItems == 0 then continue end

        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local rootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not rootPart then continue end

        local itemPickup = workspace.Game and workspace.Game.Entities and workspace.Game.Entities:FindFirstChild("ItemPickup")
        if not itemPickup then continue end

        for _, model in pairs(itemPickup:GetChildren()) do
            local parts = model:IsA("BasePart") and {model} or model:GetChildren()
            for _, part in pairs(parts) do
                if not (part:IsA("MeshPart") or part:IsA("Part")) then continue end
                local prompt = part:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt")
                if not prompt then continue end

                if table.find(customPickItems, prompt.ObjectText) then
                    rootPart.CFrame = part.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
                    prompt.RequiresLineOfSight = false
                    prompt.HoldDuration = 0
                    task.wait(0.05)
                    if fireproximityprompt then
                        fireproximityprompt(prompt)
                    else
                        firesignal(prompt.Triggered)
                    end
                    break
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)
end

do

	phoneRemote = ReplicatedStorage.devv.remoteStorage[utf8.char(115, 101, 110, 100, 77, 101, 115, 115, 97, 103, 101)]
	getgenv()._bombEnabled = false
	getgenv()._bombMessage = "LyRaX ONTOP"
	getgenv()._callBombEnabled = false
	getgenv()._airplaneMode = false
	getgenv()._bombTarget = "ALL"

	phonePlayers = {}
	function refreshPhonePlayers()
		phonePlayers = { "All" }
		for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if plr ~= LocalPlayer then
				table.insert(phonePlayers, plr.Name)
			end
		end
	end
	refreshPhonePlayers()

	RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		if getgenv()._bombEnabled then
			local msg = getgenv()._bombMessage
			if getgenv()._bombTarget == "All" then
				for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
					if plr and plr ~= LocalPlayer and plr.UserId then
						pcall(function()
							phoneRemote:FireServer(plr.UserId, msg)
						end)
					end
				end
			else
				local tar = Players:FindFirstChild(getgenv()._bombTarget)
				if tar and tar.UserId then
					pcall(function()
						phoneRemote:FireServer(tar.UserId, msg)
					end)
				end
			end
		end
		if getgenv()._callBombEnabled then
			local sig = require(ReplicatedStorage.devv).load("Signal")
			local function bomb(tar)
				if tar and tar.UserId then
					pcall(function()
						local ok, cid = sig.InvokeServer("attemptCall", tar.UserId)
						sig.FireServer("sendPhoneAction", cid, "hangup")
					end)
				end
			end
			if getgenv()._bombTarget == "All" then
				for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
					if plr and plr ~= LocalPlayer then
						bomb(plr)
					end
				end
			else
				bomb(Players:FindFirstChild(getgenv()._bombTarget))
			end
		end
	end)
end

do
	maskShopCFrame = CFrame.new(604.114014, 5.09485245, -1018.1275)
	function buyMaskWithCheck()
		local char = LocalPlayer.Character
		if not char then return false end
		local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not root then return false end
		if char:FindFirstChild("Black Bandana") then return true end
		local oldCFrame = root.CFrame
		local offsetX = math.random(-8, 8)
		local offsetZ = math.random(-8, 8)
		root.CFrame = maskShopCFrame + Vector3.new(offsetX, 0, offsetZ)
		task.wait(0.5)
		Signal.InvokeServer("attemptPurchase", "Black Bandana")
		task.wait(0.3)
		local boughtGuid = nil
		for _, v in pairs(v3item.inventory.items) do
			if type(v) == "table" and v.name == "Black Bandana" and v.guid then
				boughtGuid = v.guid
				break
			end
		end
		if not boughtGuid then
			root.CFrame = oldCFrame
			return false
		end
		Signal.FireServer("equip", boughtGuid)
		Signal.FireServer("wearMask", boughtGuid)
		task.wait(0.3)
		local worn = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Black Bandana") ~= nil
		if root and root.Parent then root.CFrame = oldCFrame end
		return worn
	end

end

	Title = T("香蕉皮光环Promax", "Banana Aura Promax"),
	Value = getgenv().bananaEnabled,
	Callback = function(state)
			getgenv().bananaEnabled = state
			if state then
				task.spawn(function()
					while getgenv().bananaEnabled do
						if not getgenv().bananaThrowId then
							BananaDoThrow()
						end
						BananaStompAura()
						EnsureBananaHitThreads()
						task.wait(1)
					end
					getgenv().bananaThrowId = nil
					getgenv().bananaCurrentTarget = nil
					getgenv().bananaActiveThreads = {}
				end)
			end
		end
})

GunTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_043",
	Title = T("火箭筒翻倍伤害", "RPG Double Damage"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state)
			if state then
				_G.RPGx10Conn = task.spawn(function()
					local oldFire = Signal.FireServer
					Signal.FireServer = function(...)
						local args = { ... }
						if args[1] == "rocketHit" then
							for i = 1, 10 do
								task.spawn(oldFire, unpack(args))
							end
						end
						return oldFire(unpack(args))
					end
				end)
			end
		end
})

GunTab:Button({
	Title = T("RPG全图轰炸", "RPG Map Bomb"),
	Callback = function()
			task.spawn(function()
				local of = Signal.FireServer
				Signal.FireServer = function(...)
					local args = { ... }
					if args[1] == "rocketHit" then
						local guid = args[2]
						local explosionGuid = args[3]
						for i = 1, 2 do
							for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
								if player ~= LocalPlayer and player.Character then
									local hrp = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
									if hrp then
										task.spawn(of, args[1], guid, explosionGuid, hrp.Position)
									end
								end
							end
							task.wait(0.05)
						end
						return
					end
					return of(unpack(args))
				end
				OhioNotify("RPG全图轰炸 已启用 (每次发射RPG前执行一次)")
			end)
		end
})

GunTab:Section({ Title = T("全枪属性", "All Gun Props") })

GunTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_044",
	Title = T("放大自身手持武器", "Enlarge Held Weapon"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state)
			if state then
				_G.WeaponScaleEnabled = true
				task.spawn(function()
					local v2 = workspace:FindFirstChild("Game")
					if v2 then v2 = v2:FindFirstChild("Local") end
					if v2 then v2 = v2:FindFirstChild("v3items") end
					if not v2 then
						warn("[Wow] 放大武器: 找不到v3items容器")
						return
					end
					local originalSizes = {}
					local extraParts = { "Bolt", "Details", "Grip", "Handguard", "Magazine", "Stock" }
					while _G.WeaponScaleEnabled do
						task.wait(3)
						local scale = _G.WeaponScaleMultiplier or 3
						pcall(function()
							for _, v5 in ipairs(v2:GetChildren()) do
								local hasPlayerId = false
								if v5:GetAttribute("playerId") then hasPlayerId = true end
								if v5:FindFirstChild("playerId") then hasPlayerId = true end
								if hasPlayerId then continue end
								if not originalSizes[v5] then
									originalSizes[v5] = {}
									local handle = v5:FindFirstChild("Handle")
									if handle and handle:IsA("BasePart") then
										originalSizes[v5]["Handle"] = handle.Size
									end
									for _, partName in ipairs(extraParts) do
										local p = v5:FindFirstChild(partName)
										if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then
											originalSizes[v5][partName] = p.Size
										end
									end
								end
								for name, origSize in pairs(originalSizes[v5]) do
									local part = v5:FindFirstChild(name)
									if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
										part.Size = origSize * scale
									end
								end
							end
						end)
					end
					pcall(function()
						for v5, parts in pairs(originalSizes) do
							if v5 and v5.Parent then
								for name, origSize in pairs(parts) do
									local part = v5:FindFirstChild(name)
									if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
										part.Size = origSize
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end)
				end)
			else
				_G.WeaponScaleEnabled = false
			end
		end
})

GunTab:Slider({
	Flag = "wow_045",
	Title = T("放大倍数", "Zoom Multiplier"),
	Value = { Min = 2, Max = 15, Default = 3 },
	Step = 1,
	Callback = function(value)
			_G.WeaponScaleMultiplier = value
		end
})

GunTab:Button({
	Title = T("全枪无后座", "All Gun No Recoil"),
	Callback = function()
			for _, v in pairs(game:GetDescendants()) do
				if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
					v:Destroy()
				end
			end
			game.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(desc)
				if desc:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
					desc:Destroy()
				end
			end)
			for _, v in pairs(require(ReplicatedStorage.devv).load("v3item").inventory.items) do
				if v.type == "Gun" then
					v.recoilAdd = 0
					v.maxRecoil = 0
					v.recoilDiminishFactor = 0
					v.recoilFastDiminishFactor = 0
				end
			end
			local gunFolder = ReplicatedStorage.devv.shared.Indicies.v3items.bin:FindFirstChild("Gun")
			if gunFolder then
				for _, v in pairs(gunFolder:GetChildren()) do
					if v:IsA("ModuleScript") then
						local m = require(v)
						m.recoilAdd = 0
						m.maxRecoil = 0
						m.recoilDiminishFactor = 0
						m.recoilFastDiminishFactor = 0
					end
				end
			end
			OhioNotify("已开启 全枪无后座")
		end
})

AimTab = Window:Tab({ Title = T("自瞄", "Aimbot"), Icon = "target" })

AimTab:Section({ Title = T("自瞄", "Aimbot") })

AimTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_046",
	Title = T("自瞄开关", "Aimbot Toggle"),
	Value = AimbotConfig.Enabled,
	Callback = function(Value)
			AimbotConfig.Enabled = Value
			if aimConnection then
				aimConnection:Disconnect()
				aimConnection = nil
			end
			if Value then
				aimSmooth = nil
				aimConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
					if not AimbotConfig.Enabled then return end
					local tar = getNearestTarget()
					if tar and Camera then
						local targetCF = CFrame.new(Camera.CFrame.Position, tar.WorldPos)
						if not aimSmooth then
							aimSmooth = targetCF
						end
						local alpha = 1 - math.exp(-20 * (dt or 0.016))
						aimSmooth = aimSmooth:Lerp(targetCF, alpha)
						Camera.CFrame = aimSmooth
					end
				end)
			end
		end
})

AimTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_047",
	Title = T("墙壁检测", "Wall Check"),
	Value = AimbotConfig.WallCheck,
	Callback = function(Value)
