EditorTab = Window:Tab({ Title = T("键位UIEDIT", "UI Editor"), Icon = "crown" })

do
	local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
	local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
	local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
	local MarketplaceService = game:GetService("MarketplaceService")
	local PlayerGui = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

	local TargetParent = PlayerGui
	pcall(function()
		if gethui and gethui() then TargetParent = gethui()
		elseif CoreGui then TargetParent = CoreGui end
	end)

	pcall(function()
		for _, v in pairs(TargetParent:GetChildren()) do
			if v.Name == "PerfectUIManager_Shield" or v.Name == "PerfectUIManager_UI" then v:Destroy() end
		end
		RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("OmniTracker_Enforce")
	end)

	local EditModeActive, SelectedElement = false, nil
	local SelectedCluster = {}
	local RuleMap = {}
	local KnownTrackedObjects = {}
	local isMenuLocked = false
	local Connections = {}
	local isDraggingElement = false
	local OriginalPositions, OriginalSizes = {}, {}
	local EditorToggleRef

	local IGNORE_NAMES = {
		windui = true,
		wind_ui = true,
		perfectuimanager_ui = true,
		perfectuimanager_shield = true,
	}

	local function isIgnoredUI(obj)
		local cur, depth = obj, 0
		while cur and cur ~= PlayerGui and depth < 12 do
			depth += 1
			local n = string.lower(cur.Name)
			if IGNORE_NAMES[n] then return true end
			if n:find("windui") or n:find("wind_ui") then return true end
			cur = cur.Parent
		end
		return false
	end

	local function GetKey(obj)
		local keyStr = obj.Name
		local trait = ""
		if obj.Name == "ContextActionButton" then
			local title = obj:FindFirstChild("ActionTitle")
			if title and title:IsA("TextLabel") and title.Text ~= "" then
				keyStr = "CAB_" .. title.Text
			else
				local icon = obj:FindFirstChild("ActionIcon")
				if icon and icon:IsA("ImageLabel") and icon.Image ~= "" then
					keyStr = "CAB_IMG_" .. (string.match(icon.Image, "%d+") or "ICO")
				end
			end
		else
			if obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("TextBox") then
				if obj.Text ~= "" then trait = trait .. "_" .. string.sub(obj.Text, 1, 16) end
			elseif obj:IsA("ImageLabel") or obj:IsA("ImageButton") then
				if obj.Image ~= "" then
					local id = string.match(obj.Image, "%d+")
					if id then trait = trait .. "_IMG" .. id end
				end
			end
		end
		trait = trait .. "_" .. math.floor(obj.AbsoluteSize.X) .. "x" .. math.floor(obj.AbsoluteSize.Y)
		local parentName = obj.Parent and obj.Parent.Name or "ROOT"
		return parentName .. "_" .. keyStr .. trait
	end

	local function GetSafeVisualPosition(gui)
		local parent = gui.Parent
		if parent and parent:IsA("GuiObject") then
			local pX, pY = parent.AbsolutePosition.X, parent.AbsolutePosition.Y
			if parent:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
				pX = pX - parent.CanvasPosition.X
				pY = pY - parent.CanvasPosition.Y
			end
			local aX, aY = gui.AnchorPoint.X, gui.AnchorPoint.Y
			local w, h = gui.AbsoluteSize.X, gui.AbsoluteSize.Y
			local dX, dY = gui.AbsolutePosition.X, gui.AbsolutePosition.Y
			return UDim2.new(0, (dX - pX) + (aX * w), 0, (dY - pY) + (aY * h))
		end
		return gui.Position
	end

	local ShieldGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
	ShieldGui.Name = "PerfectUIManager_Shield"
	ShieldGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
	ShieldGui.DisplayOrder = 99998
	ShieldGui.Parent = TargetParent

	local TapShield = Instance.new("TextButton")
	TapShield.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	TapShield.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	TapShield.Text = ""
	TapShield.Active = false
	TapShield.Visible = false
	TapShield.Parent = ShieldGui

	local EditorGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
	EditorGui.Name = "PerfectUIManager_UI"
	EditorGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
	EditorGui.DisplayOrder = 99999
	EditorGui.Parent = TargetParent

	local function ScanSingleObject(obj)
		if not obj:IsA("GuiObject") then return end
		if obj:IsDescendantOf(EditorGui) or obj:IsDescendantOf(ShieldGui) then return end
		if isIgnoredUI(obj) then return end
		local key = GetKey(obj)
		if RuleMap[key] then KnownTrackedObjects[obj] = key end
	end

	local function ForceScanUI()
		pcall(function()
			for _, obj in ipairs(PlayerGui:GetDescendants()) do ScanSingleObject(obj) end
			if TargetParent ~= PlayerGui and TargetParent.GetDescendants then
				for _, obj in ipairs(TargetParent:GetDescendants()) do ScanSingleObject(obj) end
			end
		end)
	end

	table.insert(Connections, PlayerGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
		if not next(RuleMap) then return end
		task.defer(function() ScanSingleObject(obj) end)
	end))

	local function CreateCorner(parent, radius)
		local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
		corner.CornerRadius = (radius == "100%") and UDim.new(1, 0) or UDim.new(0, radius)
		corner.Parent = parent
	end

	local function MakeDraggable(gui, isActionPopup)
		local dragging, dragInput, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil, nil
		gui.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				if isActionPopup and isMenuLocked then return end
				dragging = true; dragInput = input; dragStart = input.Position; startPos = gui.Position
			end
		end)
		table.insert(Connections, UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
			if input == dragInput and dragging then
				local delta = input.Position - dragStart
				gui.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
			end
		end))
		table.insert(Connections, UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
			if input == dragInput then dragging = false; dragInput = nil end
		end))
	end

	local ConfirmPopup = Instance.new("Frame")
	ConfirmPopup.Size = UDim2.new(0, 260, 0, 120)
	ConfirmPopup.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -130, 0.5, -60)
	ConfirmPopup.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 35)
	ConfirmPopup.Visible = false
	ConfirmPopup.ZIndex = 9999999
	ConfirmPopup.Active = true
	ConfirmPopup.Parent = EditorGui
	CreateCorner(ConfirmPopup, 15)

	local ConfirmText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	ConfirmText.Size = UDim2.new(0.9, 0, 0.5, 0)
	ConfirmText.Position = UDim2.new(0.05, 0, 0.05, 0)
	ConfirmText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	ConfirmText.TextWrapped = true
	ConfirmText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	ConfirmText.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	ConfirmText.ZIndex = 9999999
	ConfirmText.Parent = ConfirmPopup

	local function makeConfirmBtn(text, posX, color)
		local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
		b.Size = UDim2.new(0.4, 0, 0.3, 0)
		b.Position = UDim2.new(posX, 0, 0.6, 0)
		b.BackgroundColor3 = color
		b.Text = text
		b.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		b.ZIndex = 9999999
		b.Parent = ConfirmPopup
		CreateCorner(b, 8)
		return b
	end
	local ConfirmYesBtn = makeConfirmBtn("确定", 0.06, Color3.fromRGB(200, 50, 50))
	local ConfirmNoBtn  = makeConfirmBtn("取消", 0.54, Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 80))

	local currentYesConn, currentNoConn
	local function ShowConfirm(text, onYes)
		ConfirmText.Text = text
		ConfirmPopup.Visible = true
		if currentYesConn then currentYesConn:Disconnect() end
		if currentNoConn then currentNoConn:Disconnect() end
		currentYesConn = ConfirmYesBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			ConfirmPopup.Visible = false
			if currentYesConn then currentYesConn:Disconnect() end
			if currentNoConn then currentNoConn:Disconnect() end
			if onYes then onYes() end
		end)
		currentNoConn = ConfirmNoBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			ConfirmPopup.Visible = false
			if currentYesConn then currentYesConn:Disconnect() end
			if currentNoConn then currentNoConn:Disconnect() end
		end)
	end

	local function createPanel(titleText, width)
		local panel = Instance.new("Frame")
		panel.Size = UDim2.new(0, width or 240, 0, 250)
		panel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -(width or 240)/2, 0.5, -125)
		panel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 30)
		panel.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
		panel.Visible = false
		panel.Active = true
		panel.Parent = EditorGui
		CreateCorner(panel, 20)
		MakeDraggable(panel, false)

		local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		title.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 25)
		title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		title.Text = titleText
		title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		title.TextSize = 13
		title.Parent = panel

		local scroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
		scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, -35)
		scroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 30)
		scroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		scroll.ScrollBarThickness = 4
		scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
		scroll.Parent = panel

		local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
		layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 5)
		layout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
		layout.Parent = scroll
		layout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
			scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, layout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 10)
		end)
		return panel, scroll, title
	end

	local TrashPanel, TrashScroll, TrashTitle = createPanel("已删除的界面", 240)
	local LoadPanel, LoadScroll, LoadTitle    = createPanel("选择存档文件", 280)
	local SplitPanel, SplitScroll, SplitTitle = createPanel("拆分选择", 280)

	local ActionPopup = Instance.new("Frame")
	ActionPopup.Size = UDim2.new(0, 415, 0, 50)
	ActionPopup.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -207, 0.15, 0)
	ActionPopup.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 25)
	ActionPopup.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
	ActionPopup.Active = true
	ActionPopup.Visible = false
	ActionPopup.Parent = EditorGui
	CreateCorner(ActionPopup, "100%")
	MakeDraggable(ActionPopup, true)

	local function createActionButton(name, pos, width, color)
		local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
		btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, width, 0, 40)
		btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, pos, 0, 5)
		btn.BackgroundColor3 = color
		btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
		btn.Text = name
		btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		btn.TextSize = 12
		btn.Parent = ActionPopup
		CreateCorner(btn, "100%")
		return btn
	end

	local BtnLock   = createActionButton("🔓",     5, 35, Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 70))
	local BtnSplit  = createActionButton("拆分",   45, 55, Color3.fromRGB(150, 80, 200))
	local BtnPlus   = createActionButton("放大",   105, 60, Color3.fromRGB(50, 150, 255))
	local BtnMinus  = createActionButton("缩小",   170, 60, Color3.fromRGB(50, 150, 255))
	local BtnDelete = createActionButton("删除",   235, 80, Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50))
	local BtnExit   = createActionButton("退出",   320, 85, Color3.fromRGB(200, 40, 40))

	local SelectionFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	SelectionFrame.Name = "PerfectUI_SelectionFrame"
	SelectionFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	SelectionFrame.Visible = false
	SelectionFrame.ZIndex = 999999
	SelectionFrame.Active = false
	pcall(function() SelectionFrame.Interactable = false end)
	SelectionFrame.Parent = EditorGui

	local SelectionStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
	SelectionStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255)
	SelectionStroke.Thickness = 2
	SelectionStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
	SelectionStroke.Parent = SelectionFrame

	local function ClearSelection()
		SelectedElement = nil
		SelectedCluster = {}
		isDraggingElement = false
		SelectionFrame.Visible = false
	end

	RunService:BindToRenderStep("OmniTracker_Enforce", 2000, function()
		if EditModeActive and SelectedElement and SelectedElement.Parent and SelectedElement.Visible then
			SelectionFrame.Visible = true
			SelectionFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, SelectedElement.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, SelectedElement.AbsoluteSize.Y)
			SelectionFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, SelectedElement.AbsolutePosition.X, 0, SelectedElement.AbsolutePosition.Y)
		else
			SelectionFrame.Visible = false
		end

		if not next(RuleMap) then return end

		for obj, key in pairs(KnownTrackedObjects) do
			if not obj.Parent then
				KnownTrackedObjects[obj] = nil
			else
				local rule = RuleMap[key]
				if not rule then
					KnownTrackedObjects[obj] = nil
				elseif rule.IsDeleted then
					if obj.Visible then obj.Visible = false end
				else
					local parent = obj.Parent
					if parent and not parent:GetAttribute("Baked_Layout") then
						local layout = parent:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("UILayout") or parent:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("UIGridStyleLayout")
						if layout and parent.AbsoluteSize.X > 0 then
							local ready, cache = true, {}
							for _, child in ipairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
								if child:IsA("GuiObject") then
									if child.AbsoluteSize.X == 0 and child.Visible then ready = false break end
									if child.Visible then
										cache[child] = { X=child.AbsolutePosition.X, Y=child.AbsolutePosition.Y, W=child.AbsoluteSize.X, H=child.AbsoluteSize.Y }
									end
								end
							end
							if ready then
								parent:SetAttribute("Baked_Layout", true)
								for c in pairs(cache) do
									local cKey = GetKey(c)
									if not OriginalPositions[cKey] then
										OriginalPositions[cKey] = c.Position
										OriginalSizes[cKey] = c.Size
									end
								end
								for c, d in pairs(cache) do c.Size = UDim2.new(0, d.W, 0, d.H) end
								layout:Destroy()
								local pX, pY = parent.AbsolutePosition.X, parent.AbsolutePosition.Y
								if parent:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
									pX = pX - parent.CanvasPosition.X
									pY = pY - parent.CanvasPosition.Y
								end
								for c, d in pairs(cache) do
									local aX, aY = c.AnchorPoint.X, c.AnchorPoint.Y
									local bakedPos = UDim2.new(0, (d.X - pX) + (aX * d.W), 0, (d.Y - pY) + (aY * d.H))
									c.Position = bakedPos
									local cKey = GetKey(c)
									OriginalPositions[cKey] = bakedPos
									OriginalSizes[cKey] = UDim2.new(0, d.W, 0, d.H)
								end
							end
						elseif not layout then
							parent:SetAttribute("Baked_Layout", true)
						end
					end

					local isBeingDragged = false
					if isDraggingElement then
						for _, clusterEl in ipairs(SelectedCluster) do
							if clusterEl == obj then isBeingDragged = true break end
						end
					end

					if not isBeingDragged then
						pcall(function()
							if rule.Detached then
								local curAbs = obj.AbsolutePosition
								if rule.FrozenAbsX and rule.FrozenAbsY then
									local dX = rule.FrozenAbsX - curAbs.X
									local dY = rule.FrozenAbsY - curAbs.Y
									if math.abs(dX) > 0.1 or math.abs(dY) > 0.1 then
										obj.Position = UDim2.new(
											obj.Position.X.Scale, obj.Position.X.Offset + dX,
											obj.Position.Y.Scale, obj.Position.Y.Offset + dY
										)
										rule.Position = obj.Position
									end
								else
									rule.FrozenAbsX = curAbs.X
									rule.FrozenAbsY = curAbs.Y
								end
							else
								if obj.Position ~= rule.Position then obj.Position = rule.Position end
							end
							if rule.SizeModified and obj.Size ~= rule.Size then obj.Size = rule.Size end
						end)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	local GameID = tostring(game.GameId > 0 and game.GameId or game.PlaceId)
	local FolderName = "PerfectUIManager_Configs"
	local CleanGameName = "UnknownGame"
	local UpdateTrashUI

	pcall(function()
		local info = MarketplaceService:GetProductInfo(game.PlaceId)
		if info and info.Name then CleanGameName = string.gsub(info.Name, '[\\/:*?"<>|]', '') end
	end)

	local function SafeMakeFolder()
		if makefolder and not isfolder(FolderName) then pcall(function() makefolder(FolderName) end) end
	end

	local function GetAutoExecuteData()
		local path = FolderName .. "/AutoExecuteData.json"
		if isfile and isfile(path) then
			local ok, data = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(path)) end)
			if ok and type(data) == "table" then return data end
		end
		return {}
	end

	local function SaveAutoExecuteData(data)
		local path = FolderName .. "/AutoExecuteData.json"
		if writefile then pcall(function() writefile(path, HttpService:JSONEncode(data)) end) end
	end

	local function LoadSpecificFile(filePath)
		local ok, result = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(filePath)) end)
		if ok and result then
			for key, savedData in pairs(result) do
				RuleMap[key] = {
					Name = savedData.Name,
					IsDeleted = savedData.IsDeleted,
					Position = UDim2.new(unpack(savedData.Position)),
					Size = UDim2.new(unpack(savedData.Size)),
					SizeModified = savedData.SizeModified or false,
					Detached = savedData.Detached or false,
					FrozenAbsX = savedData.FrozenAbsX,
					FrozenAbsY = savedData.FrozenAbsY,
				}
			end
			ForceScanUI()
			if UpdateTrashUI then UpdateTrashUI() end

			task.spawn(function()
				task.wait(1.2)

				for key, rule in pairs(RuleMap) do
					if not rule.Detached then
						for obj, k in pairs(KnownTrackedObjects) do
							if k == key and obj.Parent then
								pcall(function()
									obj.Position = rule.Position
									if rule.SizeModified then obj.Size = rule.Size end
								end)
							end
						end
					end
				end

				for key, rule in pairs(RuleMap) do
					if rule.Detached then
						for obj, k in pairs(KnownTrackedObjects) do
							if k == key and obj.Parent then
								pcall(function()
									local parent = obj.Parent
									local pAbs = (parent and parent:IsA("GuiObject")) and parent.AbsolutePosition or Vector2.new(0, 0)
									if parent and parent:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
										pAbs = Vector2.new(pAbs.X - parent.CanvasPosition.X, pAbs.Y - parent.CanvasPosition.Y)
									end

									if rule.FrozenAbsX and rule.FrozenAbsY then
										local aX, aY = obj.AnchorPoint.X, obj.AnchorPoint.Y
										local w, h = obj.AbsoluteSize.X, obj.AbsoluteSize.Y
										obj.Position = UDim2.new(
											0, (rule.FrozenAbsX - pAbs.X) + aX * w,
											0, (rule.FrozenAbsY - pAbs.Y) + aY * h
										)
									else
										obj.Position = rule.Position
									end
									if rule.SizeModified then obj.Size = rule.Size end
								end)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end)

			LoadPanel.Visible = false
			WindUI:Notify({ Title = T("提示", "Notice"), Content = T("配置已读取", "Config loaded"), Duration = 3 })
		end
	end

	local function RefreshLoadMenu()
		if not listfiles then return end
		for _, child in pairs(LoadScroll:GetChildren()) do if child:IsA("Frame") then child:Destroy() end end

		local files = listfiles(FolderName)
		local foundSaves = false
		local autoData = GetAutoExecuteData()

		for _, file in ipairs(files) do
			if string.find(file, GameID .. ".json") then
				foundSaves = true
				local displayName = file:match("([^/]+)_" .. GameID .. ".json$")

				local row = Instance.new("Frame")
				row.Size = UDim2.new(0.95, 0, 0, 30)
				row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				row.Parent = LoadScroll

				local loadBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
				loadBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 1, 0)
				loadBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 150, 0)
				loadBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
				loadBtn.Text = "📥 读取：" .. (displayName or "存档")
				loadBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
				loadBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
				loadBtn.TextSize = 10
				loadBtn.TextScaled = true
				loadBtn.Parent = row
				CreateCorner(loadBtn, "100%")

				local isAuto = (autoData[GameID] == file)
				local autoBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
				autoBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.24, 0, 1, 0)
				autoBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0.58, 0, 0, 0)
				autoBtn.BackgroundColor3 = isAuto and Color3.fromRGB(50, 200, 50) or Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 100)
				autoBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
				autoBtn.Text = isAuto and "自动：开" or "自动：关"
				autoBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
				autoBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
				autoBtn.TextSize = 10
				autoBtn.Parent = row
				CreateCorner(autoBtn, "100%")

				local delFileBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
				delFileBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.15, 0, 1, 0)
				delFileBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0.85, 0, 0, 0)
				delFileBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70)
				delFileBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
				delFileBtn.Text = "🗑️"
				delFileBtn.Parent = row
				CreateCorner(delFileBtn, "100%")

				loadBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() LoadSpecificFile(file) end)
				autoBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					local cur = GetAutoExecuteData()
					if cur[GameID] == file then cur[GameID] = nil else cur[GameID] = file end
					SaveAutoExecuteData(cur)
					RefreshLoadMenu()
				end)
				delFileBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					ShowConfirm("确定要删除这个设置吗？", function()
						if delfile then pcall(function() delfile(file) end) end
						RefreshLoadMenu()
					end)
				end)
			end
		end
		LoadTitle.Text = foundSaves and "选择存档文件" or "本游戏暂无存档！"
	end

	UpdateTrashUI = function()
		for _, child in pairs(TrashScroll:GetChildren()) do if child:IsA("TextButton") then child:Destroy() end end
		local delCount = 0
		for key, data in pairs(RuleMap) do
			if data.IsDeleted then
				delCount = delCount + 1
				local restoreBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
				restoreBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.95, 0, 0, 30)
				restoreBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 100, 200)
				restoreBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
				restoreBtn.Text = "  ↺ 恢复：" .. (data.Name or "元素")
				restoreBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
				restoreBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
				restoreBtn.TextSize = 12
				restoreBtn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
				restoreBtn.Parent = TrashScroll
				CreateCorner(restoreBtn, "100%")

				restoreBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					for obj, matchedKey in pairs(KnownTrackedObjects) do
						if matchedKey == key then
							if obj.Parent then
								obj.Visible = true
								if OriginalPositions[key] then obj.Position = OriginalPositions[key] end
								if OriginalSizes[key] then obj.Size = OriginalSizes[key] end
							end
							KnownTrackedObjects[obj] = nil
						end
					end
					RuleMap[key] = nil
					UpdateTrashUI()
				end)
			end
		end
		TrashTitle.Text = "已删除的界面 (" .. delCount .. ")"
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		SafeMakeFolder()
		local autoData = GetAutoExecuteData()
		if autoData[GameID] and isfile(autoData[GameID]) then LoadSpecificFile(autoData[GameID]) end
	end)

	local activeDragInput, dragStartInputPos = nil, nil
	local initialClusterPositions = {}

	EditorTab:Section({ Title = T("编辑控制", "Edit Control") })

	EditorToggleRef = EditorTab:Toggle({
		Flag = "wow_240",
		Title = T("编辑模式", "Edit Mode"),
		Value = false,
		Callback = function(s)
			EditModeActive = s
			if s then
				TapShield.Visible, TapShield.Active, ActionPopup.Visible = true, true, true
			else
				TapShield.Visible, TapShield.Active, ActionPopup.Visible = false, false, false
				SplitPanel.Visible = false
				ClearSelection()
				activeDragInput = nil
			end
		end,
	})

	EditorTab:Toggle({
		Flag = "wow_241",
		Title = T("锁定菜单", "Lock Menu"),
		Value = false,
		Callback = function(s)
			isMenuLocked = s
			BtnLock.Text = s and "🔒" or "🔓"
		end,
	})

	EditorTab:Button({
		Title = T("保存配置", "Save Config"),
		Callback = function()
			if writefile then
				SafeMakeFolder()
				local v = 1
				local savePath = ""
				while true do
					savePath = FolderName .. "/" .. CleanGameName .. "_v" .. v .. "_" .. GameID .. ".json"
					if not isfile(savePath) then break end
					v = v + 1
				end
				local dataToSave = {}
				for key, rule in pairs(RuleMap) do
					dataToSave[key] = {
						Name = rule.Name,
						IsDeleted = rule.IsDeleted,
						Position = { rule.Position.X.Scale, rule.Position.X.Offset, rule.Position.Y.Scale, rule.Position.Y.Offset },
						Size = { rule.Size.X.Scale, rule.Size.X.Offset, rule.Size.Y.Scale, rule.Size.Y.Offset },
						SizeModified = rule.SizeModified or false,
						Detached = rule.Detached or false,
						FrozenAbsX = rule.FrozenAbsX,
						FrozenAbsY = rule.FrozenAbsY,
					}
				end
				writefile(savePath, HttpService:JSONEncode(dataToSave))
				RefreshLoadMenu()
				WindUI:Notify({ Title = T("提示", "Notice"), Content = T("已保存为 v", "Saved as v") .. v, Duration = 3 })
			end
		end,
	})

	EditorTab:Button({
		Title = T("读取配置", "Load Config"),
		Callback = function()
			TrashPanel.Visible = false
			SplitPanel.Visible = false
			LoadPanel.Visible = not LoadPanel.Visible
			if LoadPanel.Visible then RefreshLoadMenu() end
		end,
	})

	EditorTab:Button({
		Title = T("回收站", "Trash"),
		Callback = function()
			LoadPanel.Visible = false
			SplitPanel.Visible = false
			TrashPanel.Visible = not TrashPanel.Visible
			if TrashPanel.Visible then UpdateTrashUI() end
		end,
	})

	EditorTab:Button({
		Title = T("拆分选择", "Split Select"),
		Callback = function()
			TrashPanel.Visible = false
			LoadPanel.Visible = false
			if SplitPanel.Visible then
				SplitPanel.Visible = false
			else
				if RefreshSplitPanel then
					RefreshSplitPanel()
					SplitPanel.Visible = true
				end
			end
		end,
	})

	EditorTab:Button({
		Title = T("卸载脚本", "Uninstall"),
		Callback = function()
			ShowConfirm("确定要卸载脚本吗？", function()
				for _, conn in pairs(Connections) do
					if conn and conn.Connected then conn:Disconnect() end
				end
				pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("OmniTracker_Enforce") end)
				if ShieldGui then ShieldGui:Destroy() end
				if EditorGui then EditorGui:Destroy() end
				KnownTrackedObjects = nil
				RuleMap = nil
			end)
		end,
	})

	BtnLock.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		isMenuLocked = not isMenuLocked
		BtnLock.Text = isMenuLocked and "🔒" or "🔓"
	end)

	BtnExit.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		EditModeActive = false
		TapShield.Visible, TapShield.Active, ActionPopup.Visible = false, false, false
		SplitPanel.Visible = false
		ClearSelection()
		activeDragInput = nil
		if EditorToggleRef then
			pcall(function() EditorToggleRef:Set(false) end)
			pcall(function() EditorToggleRef:SetValue(false) end)
		end
		WindUI:Notify({ Title = T("编辑", "Editor"), Content = T("已退出编辑模式", "Exited edit mode"), Duration = 2 })
	end)

	local function EnsureRuleEntry(obj)
		local key = GetKey(obj)
		if not RuleMap[key] then
			local sz = obj.Size
			if sz == UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0) then
				sz = UDim2.new(0, obj.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, obj.AbsoluteSize.Y)
			end
			RuleMap[key] = {
				Name = obj.Name,
				IsDeleted = false,
				Position = GetSafeVisualPosition(obj),
				Size = sz,
				SizeModified = false,
				Detached = false,
				FrozenAbsX = nil,
				FrozenAbsY = nil,
			}
		end
		return key
	end

	local function DetachElement(obj)
		local key = EnsureRuleEntry(obj)
		if RuleMap[key].Detached then return end
		local abs = obj.AbsolutePosition
		RuleMap[key].Detached = true
		RuleMap[key].FrozenAbsX = abs.X
		RuleMap[key].FrozenAbsY = abs.Y
		RuleMap[key].Size = UDim2.new(0, obj.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, obj.AbsoluteSize.Y)
		RuleMap[key].SizeModified = true
		RuleMap[key].Position = obj.Position
		KnownTrackedObjects[obj] = key
	end

	local function MergeElement(obj)
		local key = EnsureRuleEntry(obj)
		if not RuleMap[key].Detached then return end
		RuleMap[key].Detached = false
		RuleMap[key].FrozenAbsX = nil
		RuleMap[key].FrozenAbsY = nil
		RuleMap[key].Position = obj.Position
		KnownTrackedObjects[obj] = key
	end

	local RefreshSplitPanel

	RefreshSplitPanel = function()
		for _, child in pairs(SplitScroll:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("TextButton") or child:IsA("Frame") then child:Destroy() end
		end

		if not SelectedElement then
			SplitTitle.Text = "未选中任何元素"
			return
		end

		local candidates, seen = {}, {}
		local function addCandidate(o)
			if seen[o] then return end
			if not o:IsA("GuiObject") then return end
			if not o.Visible then return end
			if o.AbsoluteSize.X <= 0 or o.AbsoluteSize.Y <= 0 then return end
			if o:IsDescendantOf(EditorGui) or o:IsDescendantOf(ShieldGui) then return end
			if isIgnoredUI(o) then return end
			seen[o] = true
			table.insert(candidates, o)
		end
		for _, el in ipairs(SelectedCluster) do addCandidate(el) end
		for _, d in ipairs(SelectedElement:GetDescendants()) do addCandidate(d) end
		addCandidate(SelectedElement)

		table.sort(candidates, function(a, b)
			return (a.AbsoluteSize.X * a.AbsoluteSize.Y) < (b.AbsoluteSize.X * b.AbsoluteSize.Y)
		end)

		SplitTitle.Text = "拆分 (" .. #candidates .. ")"

		for _, obj in ipairs(candidates) do
			local objKey = GetKey(obj)
			local isDetached = RuleMap[objKey] and RuleMap[objKey].Detached
			local isCurrent = (obj == SelectedElement)

			local row = Instance.new("Frame")
			row.Size = UDim2.new(0.95, 0, 0, 30)
			row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			row.Parent = SplitScroll

			local pickBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
			pickBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.75, 0, 1, 0)
			pickBtn.BackgroundColor3 = isCurrent and Color3.fromRGB(50, 150, 255)
									   or (isDetached and Color3.fromRGB(210, 130, 0) or Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 90))
			pickBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
			pickBtn.Text = (isDetached and "🔓 " or "🔒 ") .. obj.Name .. " · " .. obj.ClassName
			pickBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			pickBtn.TextSize = 11
			pickBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
			pickBtn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
			pickBtn.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
			pickBtn.Parent = row
			CreateCorner(pickBtn, "100%")

			pickBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				EnsureRuleEntry(obj)
				SelectedElement = obj
				SelectedCluster = { obj }
				KnownTrackedObjects[obj] = GetKey(obj)
				RefreshSplitPanel()
			end)

			local toggleBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
			toggleBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.22, 0, 1, 0)
			toggleBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0.78, 0, 0, 0)
			toggleBtn.BackgroundColor3 = isDetached and Color3.fromRGB(50, 200, 100) or Color3.fromRGB(150, 80, 200)
			toggleBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
			toggleBtn.Text = isDetached and "合并" or "独立"
			toggleBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			toggleBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
			toggleBtn.TextSize = 11
			toggleBtn.Parent = row
			CreateCorner(toggleBtn, "100%")

			toggleBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				if RuleMap[objKey] and RuleMap[objKey].Detached then
					MergeElement(obj)
				else
					DetachElement(obj)
				end
				SelectedElement = obj
				SelectedCluster = { obj }
				RefreshSplitPanel()
			end)
		end
	end

	BtnSplit.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		TrashPanel.Visible, LoadPanel.Visible = false, false
		if SplitPanel.Visible then
			SplitPanel.Visible = false
		else
			RefreshSplitPanel()
			SplitPanel.Visible = true
		end
	end)

	local function ModifySize(increment)
		if SelectedElement and #SelectedCluster > 0 then
			for _, el in ipairs(SelectedCluster) do
				local key = EnsureRuleEntry(el)
				local cur = el.Size
				if cur == UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0) then
					cur = UDim2.new(0, el.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, el.AbsoluteSize.Y)
				end
				RuleMap[key].Size = cur
				RuleMap[key].SizeModified = true
				local s = RuleMap[key].Size
				local ns = UDim2.new(s.X.Scale, s.X.Offset + increment, s.Y.Scale, s.Y.Offset + increment)
				RuleMap[key].Size = ns
				el.Size = ns
				KnownTrackedObjects[el] = key
			end
		end
	end

	BtnPlus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() ModifySize(15) end)
	BtnMinus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() ModifySize(-15) end)

	BtnDelete.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if SelectedElement and #SelectedCluster > 0 then
			for _, el in ipairs(SelectedCluster) do
				local key = EnsureRuleEntry(el)
				RuleMap[key].IsDeleted = true
				el.Visible = false
			end
			ClearSelection()
			UpdateTrashUI()
		end
	end)

	local function isJoystick(gui)
		local curr, depth = gui, 0
		while curr and curr:IsA("GuiObject") and depth < 3 do
			depth = depth + 1
			local n = string.lower(curr.Name)
			if n == "thumbstick" or n == "joystick"
				or n == "thumbstickframe" or n == "joystickframe"
				or n:match("^thumbstick%d*$") or n:match("^joystick%d*$") then
				return true
			end
			curr = curr.Parent
		end
		return false
	end

	local function GetPerfectTarget(elementsAtTap, viewport)
		local maxW, maxH = viewport.X * 0.95, viewport.Y * 0.95
		local topElement, fallback = nil, nil

		for _, gui in ipairs(elementsAtTap) do
			if gui:IsDescendantOf(EditorGui) or gui:IsDescendantOf(ShieldGui) then continue end
			if isIgnoredUI(gui) then continue end
			if not gui.Visible then continue end
			if isJoystick(gui) then continue end
			local s = gui.AbsoluteSize
			local tooBig = (s.X >= maxW or s.Y >= maxH)
			local isButton = gui:IsA("GuiButton") or gui:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TouchTransmitter") ~= nil
			if isButton and not tooBig then topElement = gui break end
			if not fallback and not tooBig then
				if gui.BackgroundTransparency < 1
					or gui:IsA("ImageLabel") or gui:IsA("TextLabel") or gui:IsA("TextBox") then
					fallback = gui
				end
			end
		end

		local target = topElement or fallback
		if not target then return nil end

		local highestValid = target
		local current = target.Parent
		local depth = 0
		while current and current:IsA("GuiObject") and depth < 5 do
			depth = depth + 1
			if current.AbsoluteSize.X >= maxW or current.AbsoluteSize.Y >= maxH then break end
			if isJoystick(current) then break end
			if isIgnoredUI(current) then break end

			local curKey = GetKey(current)
			if RuleMap[curKey] and RuleMap[curKey].Detached then break end

			local hasSiblingButton = false
			for _, child in ipairs(current:GetChildren()) do
				if child ~= highestValid and child:IsA("GuiObject") and child.Visible then
					if child:IsA("GuiButton") or child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TouchTransmitter") then
						hasSiblingButton = true
						break
					end
				end
			end
			if hasSiblingButton then break end
			if current.AbsoluteSize.X > highestValid.AbsoluteSize.X * 1.35
				or current.AbsoluteSize.Y > highestValid.AbsoluteSize.Y * 1.35 then break end

			highestValid = current
			current = current.Parent
		end
		return highestValid
	end

	local function GetCluster(target)
		local tKey = GetKey(target)
		if RuleMap[tKey] and RuleMap[tKey].Detached then
			return { target }
		end

		local cluster = { target }
		local added = { [target] = true }

		if target.Parent then
			local tX, tY = target.AbsolutePosition.X, target.AbsolutePosition.Y
			local tW, tH = target.AbsoluteSize.X, target.AbsoluteSize.Y
			local tCX, tCY = tX + tW / 2, tY + tH / 2

			for _, child in ipairs(target.Parent:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("GuiObject") and not added[child] and child.Visible then
					if isIgnoredUI(child) then continue end
					local cKey = GetKey(child)
					if not (RuleMap[cKey] and RuleMap[cKey].Detached) then
						local cW, cH = child.AbsoluteSize.X, child.AbsoluteSize.Y
						if cW <= 350 and cH <= 350 then
							local cCX = child.AbsolutePosition.X + cW / 2
							local cCY = child.AbsolutePosition.Y + cH / 2
							local dist = math.sqrt((cCX - tCX) ^ 2 + (cCY - tCY) ^ 2)
							local sizeSimilar = (math.abs(cW - tW) <= 15) and (math.abs(cH - tH) <= 15)
							if dist <= 5 and sizeSimilar then
								table.insert(cluster, child)
								added[child] = true
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		local parent = target.Parent
		if parent and parent:IsA("GuiObject") and not added[parent] then
			local pKey = GetKey(parent)
			if not (RuleMap[pKey] and RuleMap[pKey].Detached) and not isIgnoredUI(parent) then
				local pW, pH = parent.AbsoluteSize.X, parent.AbsoluteSize.Y
				if pW <= target.AbsoluteSize.X * 1.15 and pH <= target.AbsoluteSize.Y * 1.15 then
					table.insert(cluster, parent)
					added[parent] = true
				end
			end
		end

		local pureCluster = {}
		for _, el in ipairs(cluster) do
			local isChild = false
			for _, other in ipairs(cluster) do
				if el ~= other and el:IsDescendantOf(other) then isChild = true break end
			end
			if not isChild then table.insert(pureCluster, el) end
		end
		return pureCluster
	end

	table.insert(Connections, UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			if ConfirmPopup.Visible then return end
			if not isMenuLocked then isDraggingElement = false; activeDragInput = nil end

			local guis = { TrashPanel, LoadPanel, ActionPopup, SplitPanel }
			for _, ui in ipairs(guis) do
				if ui.Visible then
					local p, s, pos = ui.AbsolutePosition, ui.AbsoluteSize, input.Position
					if pos.X >= p.X and pos.X <= p.X + s.X and pos.Y >= p.Y and pos.Y <= p.Y + s.Y then return end
				end
			end
			if not EditModeActive then return end

			TapShield.Visible, SelectionFrame.Visible = false, false

			local ok, elementsAtTap = pcall(function()
				return PlayerGui:GetGuiObjectsAtPosition(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
			end)
			local validElement = nil
			if ok and elementsAtTap then
				validElement = GetPerfectTarget(elementsAtTap, workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize)
			end

			TapShield.Visible = true

			if validElement then
				SelectedElement = validElement
				SelectedCluster = GetCluster(validElement)
				isDraggingElement = true
				activeDragInput = input
				dragStartInputPos = input.Position
				initialClusterPositions = {}

				for _, el in ipairs(SelectedCluster) do
					local key = EnsureRuleEntry(el)
					local currentVisualPos = GetSafeVisualPosition(el)
					local currentVisualSize = el.Size
					if currentVisualSize == UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0) then
						currentVisualSize = UDim2.new(0, el.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, el.AbsoluteSize.Y)
					end
					if not OriginalPositions[key] then
						OriginalPositions[key] = currentVisualPos
						OriginalSizes[key] = currentVisualSize
					end
					initialClusterPositions[el] = RuleMap[key].Position
					KnownTrackedObjects[el] = key
					el.Position = RuleMap[key].Position
				end
				if SplitPanel.Visible then RefreshSplitPanel() end
			else
				ClearSelection()
			end
		end
	end))

	table.insert(Connections, UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if isDraggingElement and SelectedElement and input == activeDragInput
			and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			pcall(function()
				local totalDelta = input.Position - dragStartInputPos
				for _, el in ipairs(SelectedCluster) do
					local key = GetKey(el)
					local startPos = initialClusterPositions[el]
					if startPos then
						local newPos = UDim2.new(
							startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + totalDelta.X,
							startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + totalDelta.Y
						)
						local rule = RuleMap[key]
						if rule then
							rule.Position = newPos
						end
						el.Position = newPos
						if rule and rule.Detached then
							rule.FrozenAbsX = el.AbsolutePosition.X
							rule.FrozenAbsY = el.AbsolutePosition.Y
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end))

	table.insert(Connections, UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if input == activeDragInput
			and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			isDraggingElement = false
			activeDragInput = nil
		end
	end))
end
CrosshairTab = Window:Tab({ Title = T("准星", "Crosshair"), Icon = "crosshair" })

CrosshairTab:Section({ Title = T("自定义准星", "Custom Crosshair") })

getgenv()._ch = {
	Enabled = true,
	Shape = "十字",
	Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255),
	Length = 8,
	Thickness = 2,
	Gap = 6,
	Transparency = 0.3,
	DotSize = 2,
	DotTransparency = 0.3,
	PulseSpeed = 3,
	PulseRange = 2,
	Outline = true,
	OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
	OutlineThickness = 1,
	FollowAim = true,
	OffsetX = 0,
	OffsetY = 0,
}

local function ch()
	return getgenv()._ch
end

	Flag = "wow_300",
	Title = T("启用准星", "Enable Crosshair"),
	Value = true,
	Callback = function(s) ch().Enabled = s end,

	Callback = function(s) ch().FollowAim = s end,
})

	Flag = "wow_301",

	Values = { "十字", "圆点", "圆圈", "十字+点", "十字+圈" },
	Callback = function(v) ch().Shape = v end,

})

	Flag = "wow_308",

	Value = { Min = 0, Max = 100, Default = 30 },
	Step = 1,
	Callback = function(v) ch().DotTransparency = v / 100 end,
})

CrosshairTab:Slider({
	Flag = "wow_309",
	Title = T("呼吸速度", "Pulse Speed"),
	Value = { Min = 0, Max = 20, Default = 3 },
	Step = 1,
	Callback = function(v) ch().PulseSpeed = v end,
})

	Callback = function(v) ch().OffsetX = v end,
})

CrosshairTab:Slider({
	Flag = "wow_317",
	Title = T("垂直偏移", "Offset Y"),
	Value = { Min = -300, Max = 300, Default = 0 },
	Step = 1,
	Callback = function(v) ch().OffsetY = v end,
})

do
	local PlayersSvc = game:GetService("Players")
	local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

	task.spawn(function()
		local lp = PlayersSvc.LocalPlayer
		if not lp then return end
		local pg = lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 15)
		if not pg then return end

		local old = pg:FindFirstChild("MyCustomCrosshair")
		if old then old:Destroy() end

		local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
		gui.Name = "MyCustomCrosshair"
		gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
		gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
		gui.DisplayOrder = 100000
		gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
		gui.Parent = pg

		local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
		holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		holder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		holder.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
		holder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1)
		holder.ZIndex = 9999
		holder.Parent = gui

		local function makeLine()
			local parent = Instance.new("Frame")
			parent.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			parent.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
			parent.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
			parent.ZIndex = 9999
			parent.Parent = holder

			local line = Instance.new("Frame")
			line.BorderSizePixel = 0
			line.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
			line.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
			line.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
			line.ZIndex = 10000
			line.Parent = parent

			local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
			stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
			stroke.LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter
			stroke.Parent = line

			return parent, line, stroke
		end

		local topP, topL, topS = makeLine()
		local bottomP, bottomL, bottomS = makeLine()
		local leftP, leftL, leftS = makeLine()
		local rightP, rightL, rightS = makeLine()
		local dotP, dotL, dotS = makeLine()

		local circle = Instance.new("Frame")
		circle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		circle.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		circle.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
		circle.ZIndex = 9999
		circle.Parent = holder

		local circleCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
		circleCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
		circleCorner.Parent = circle

		local circleStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
		circleStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
		circleStroke.Parent = circle

		local hidden = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
		local function hideVanilla()
			local crosshair = pg:FindFirstChild("Crosshair")
			if not crosshair then return end
			local container = crosshair:FindFirstChild("CrosshairContainer")
			if not container then return end

			local function hide(instance)
				if not instance:IsA("GuiObject") then return end
				if hidden[instance] == nil then
					hidden[instance] = instance.Visible
				end
				instance.Visible = false
			end

			hide(container)
			for _, d in ipairs(container:GetDescendants()) do
				hide(d)
			end
		end

		task.spawn(function()
			hideVanilla()
			local crosshair = pg:WaitForChild("Crosshair", 15)
			if crosshair then
				local container = crosshair:WaitForChild("CrosshairContainer", 15)
				if container then
					container.DescendantAdded:Connect(function()
						task.wait()
						hideVanilla()
					end)
				end
			end
		end)

		local function isAimButtonVisible()
			local touchGui = pg:FindFirstChild("TouchGui")
			if not touchGui then return false end
			local tcf = touchGui:FindFirstChild("TouchControlFrame")
			if not tcf then return false end
			local jumpButton = tcf:FindFirstChild("JumpButton")
			if not jumpButton then return false end
			local aimBtn = jumpButton:FindFirstChild("4")
			if not aimBtn then return false end
			return aimBtn.Visible and aimBtn.AbsoluteSize.X > 0 and aimBtn.AbsoluteSize.Y > 0
		end

		local t = 0
		RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
			local cfg = ch()

			local show = cfg.Enabled
			if cfg.FollowAim and not isAimButtonVisible() then
				show = false
			end

			holder.Visible = show
			if not show then return end

			holder.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5) + UDim2.fromOffset(cfg.OffsetX or 0, cfg.OffsetY or 0)

			t = t + dt * cfg.PulseSpeed
			local pulse = math.sin(t) * cfg.PulseRange

			local shape = cfg.Shape
			local showTop    = shape == "十字" or shape == "十字+点" or shape == "十字+圈"
			local showDot    = shape == "圆点" or shape == "十字+点"
			local showCircle = shape == "圆圈" or shape == "十字+圈"

			topP.Visible = showTop
			bottomP.Visible = showTop
			leftP.Visible = showTop
			rightP.Visible = showTop

			if showTop then
				local len = math.max(0, cfg.Length + pulse)
				local gap = math.max(0, cfg.Gap + pulse)

				local function setLine(parent, line, stroke, w, h, x, y)
					parent.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(w, 0.01), math.max(h, 0.01))
					parent.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5) + UDim2.fromOffset(x, y)
					line.BackgroundColor3 = cfg.Color
					line.BackgroundTransparency = cfg.Transparency
					stroke.Color = cfg.OutlineColor
					stroke.Thickness = cfg.Outline and cfg.OutlineThickness or 0
					stroke.Transparency = cfg.Outline and 0 or 1
				end

				setLine(topP, topL, topS, cfg.Thickness, len, 0, -(gap + len / 2))
				setLine(bottomP, bottomL, bottomS, cfg.Thickness, len, 0, gap + len / 2)
				setLine(leftP, leftL, leftS, len, cfg.Thickness, -(gap + len / 2), 0)
				setLine(rightP, rightL, rightS, len, cfg.Thickness, gap + len / 2, 0)
			end

			dotP.Visible = showDot and cfg.DotSize > 0
			if dotP.Visible then
				dotP.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(cfg.DotSize, cfg.DotSize)
				dotP.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
				dotL.BackgroundColor3 = cfg.Color
				dotL.BackgroundTransparency = cfg.DotTransparency
				dotS.Color = cfg.OutlineColor
				dotS.Thickness = cfg.Outline and cfg.OutlineThickness or 0
				dotS.Transparency = cfg.Outline and 0 or 1
			end

			circle.Visible = showCircle
			if showCircle then
				local radius = (cfg.Gap + cfg.Length + pulse) * 2
				circle.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(radius, radius)
				circleStroke.Color = cfg.Color
				circleStroke.Thickness = cfg.Thickness
				circleStroke.Transparency = cfg.Transparency
			end
		end)
	end)
end
AimTab:Section({ Title = T("命中框", "Hitbox") })
if getgenv()._uiRenderConn then
	getgenv()._uiRenderConn:Disconnect()
	getgenv()._uiRenderConn = nil

AimTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_206",
	Title = T("瞄准按钮渐变", "Aim Button Gradient"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(s) getgenv()._grad_Aim = s end,
})

do
	local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
	local PlayersSvc = game:GetService("Players")

	if getgenv()._uiRenderConn then
		getgenv()._uiRenderConn:Disconnect()
		getgenv()._uiRenderConn = nil
	end

	local origSizes = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	local hues = { Crosshair = 0, ItemBar = 0, Fire = 0, Reload = 0, Aim = 0 }

	local function getPlayerGui()
		local lp = PlayersSvc.LocalPlayer
		return lp and lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
	end

	local function wowPaintUI(instance, color)
		if not instance then return end
		if instance:IsA("GuiObject") then
			if instance:IsA("ImageLabel") or instance:IsA("ImageButton") then
				instance.ImageColor3 = color
			else
				instance.BackgroundColor3 = color
			end
			if instance:IsA("TextLabel") or instance:IsA("TextButton") or instance:IsA("TextBox") then
				instance.TextColor3 = color
			end
		end
		for _, d in ipairs(instance:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("GuiObject") then
				if d:IsA("ImageLabel") or d:IsA("ImageButton") then
					d.ImageColor3 = color
				else
					d.BackgroundColor3 = color
				end
				if d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("TextBox") then
					d.TextColor3 = color
				end
			elseif d:IsA("UIStroke") then
				d.Color = color
			end
		end
	end

	local function wowApplySize(instance, scale)
		if not instance:IsA("GuiObject") then return end
		if not origSizes[instance] then
			origSizes[instance] = instance.Size
		end
		local base = origSizes[instance]
		instance.Size = UDim2.new(
			base.X.Scale * scale, base.X.Offset * scale,
			base.Y.Scale * scale, base.Y.Offset * scale
		)
	end

	local function wowApplySizeRecursive(instance, scale)
		wowApplySize(instance, scale)
		for _, d in ipairs(instance:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("GuiObject") then
				wowApplySize(d, scale)
			end
		end
	end

	local function resolveColor(key, dt)
		if getgenv()["_grad_" .. key] then
			local speed = getgenv()._uiGradSpeed or 1
			hues[key] = (hues[key] + dt * speed * 0.15) % 1
			return Color3.fromHSV(hues[key], 1, 1)
		end
		return getgenv()["_color_" .. key] or Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255)
	end

	local function getHotbarHolder()
		local pg = getPlayerGui()
		local hotbar = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("Hotbar")
		return hotbar and hotbar:FindFirstChild("Holder")
	end

	local function getJumpButton()
		local pg = getPlayerGui()
		local tg = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("TouchGui")
		local tcf = tg and tg:FindFirstChild("TouchControlFrame")
		return tcf and tcf:FindFirstChild("JumpButton")
	end

	local function getCrosshairContainer()
		local pg = getPlayerGui()
		local crosshair = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("Crosshair")
		return crosshair and crosshair:FindFirstChild("CrosshairContainer")
	end

	getgenv()._uiRenderConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
		if not getgenv()._uiRenderOn then return end

		if getgenv()._on_Crosshair then
			local cc = getCrosshairContainer()
			if cc then
				wowPaintUI(cc, resolveColor("Crosshair", dt))
				local scale = getgenv()._crosshairScale or 1
				if scale ~= 1 then
					wowApplySizeRecursive(cc, scale)
				end
			end
		end

		if getgenv()._on_ItemBar then
			local holder = getHotbarHolder()
			if holder then
				local color = resolveColor("ItemBar", dt)
				for _, child in ipairs(holder:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("GuiObject") and child.Name:match("^ItemButton") then
						wowPaintUI(child, color)
					end
				end
			end
		end

		local jb = getJumpButton()
		if jb then
			if getgenv()._on_Fire then
				local fire = jb:FindFirstChild("5")
				if fire then wowPaintUI(fire, resolveColor("Fire", dt)) end
			end
			if getgenv()._on_Reload then
				local reload = jb:FindFirstChild("6")
				if reload then wowPaintUI(reload, resolveColor("Reload", dt)) end
			end
			if getgenv()._on_Aim then
				local aim = jb:FindFirstChild("4")
				if aim then wowPaintUI(aim, resolveColor("Aim", dt)) end
			end
		end
	end)
end
AimTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_052",
	Title = T("枪械范围", "Gun Range"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state)
			SETTINGS.ENABLED = state
			if not state then
				for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
					if p ~= LocalPlayer and p.Character then
						local hrp = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						if hrp then
							hrp.Size = Vector3.new(2, 2, 1)
							hrp.Transparency = 1
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
})

AimTab:Section({ Title = T("子弹追踪", "Bullet Track") })

AimTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_056",
	Title = T("子弹追踪", "Bullet Track"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state)
			getgenv()._bulletTraceEnabled = state
			if state then
				if getgenv()._bulletTraceHooked then return end
				getgenv()._bulletTraceHooked = true
				task.spawn(function()
					local ProjectileModule = require(ReplicatedStorage.devv.client.Objects.v3item.modules.projectiles)
					local Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
					getgenv()._oldNewProjectile = ProjectileModule.newProjectileOfType
					local ScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui", CoreGui)
					ScreenGui.Name = "BulletTraceUI"
					ScreenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
					ScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
					local FovCircle = Instance.new("Frame")
					FovCircle.Name = "FOV"
					FovCircle.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(getgenv()._fovRadius * 2, getgenv()._fovRadius * 2)
					FovCircle.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
					FovCircle.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
					FovCircle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
					FovCircle.Parent = ScreenGui
					getgenv()._fovCircle = FovCircle
					local UICorner = Instance.new("UICorner", FovCircle)
					UICorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
					local UIStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", FovCircle)
					UIStroke.Color = getgenv()._fovColor or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
					getgenv()._fovStroke = UIStroke
					UIStroke.Thickness = 1
					UIStroke.Transparency = 0.4
					local ESPFolder = Instance.new("Folder", CoreGui)
					ESPFolder.Name = "BulletTraceESP"
					local function createESP(target)
						for _, v in pairs(ESPFolder:GetChildren()) do v:Destroy() end
						if not target then return end
						local bill = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
						bill.Name = "Locked"
						bill.AlwaysOnTop = true
						bill.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(100, 40)
						bill.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)
						bill.Adornee = target
						bill.Parent = ESPFolder
						local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", bill)
						stroke.Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
						stroke.Thickness = 2
						local label = Instance.new("TextLabel", bill)
						label.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
						label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
						label.Text = "LOCKED"
						label.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
						label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
						label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
						label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
						label.TextSize = 14
					end
					local function clearESP()
						for _, v in pairs(ESPFolder:GetChildren()) do v:Destroy() end
					end
					local function isInFOV(pos)
						local sp, on = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(pos)
						if not on then return false end
						local cx = Camera.ViewportSize.X / 2
						local cy = Camera.ViewportSize.Y / 2
						local dx = sp.X - cx
						local dy = sp.Y - cy
						return math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) <= getgenv()._fovRadius
					end
					local function getTarget()
						local best = nil
						local min = math.huge
						for _, plr in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
							if plr ~= LocalPlayer and plr.Character then
								local head = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
								local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
								if head and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
									local _, on = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(head.Position)
									if on and isInFOV(head.Position) then
										local dist = (head.Position - Camera.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
										if dist < min then
											min = dist
											best = plr
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
						return best
					end
					local function aiPredict(plr, origin, speed)
						if not plr.Character then return nil end
						local root = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						local head = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
						if not root or not head then return head and head.Position end
						local vel = root.Velocity * Vector3.new(1, 0.3, 1)
						local dist = (origin - head.Position).Magnitude
						local time = dist / (speed > 0 and speed or 1000)
						return head.Position + vel * time
					end
					RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
						FovCircle.Visible = getgenv()._bulletTraceEnabled and getgenv()._showFov
						if not getgenv()._bulletTraceEnabled then
							clearESP()
							getgenv()._lockedTarget = nil
							return
						end
						local tar = getTarget()
						if tar then
							if getgenv()._lockedTarget ~= tar then
								getgenv()._lockedTarget = tar
								createESP(tar.Character:FindFirstChild("Head"))
							end
						else
							clearESP()
							getgenv()._lockedTarget = nil
						end
					end)
					if getgenv()._bulletTraceMode == "RayCast" and not getgenv()._raycastHook then
						getgenv()._raycastHook = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", newcclosure(function(self, ...)
							local method = getnamecallmethod()
							if method == "Raycast" and not checkcaller() and getgenv()._bulletTraceEnabled and getgenv()._bulletTraceMode == "RayCast" then
								local tar = getgenv()._lockedTarget
								local part = tar and tar.Character and tar.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
								if part then
									local origin = Camera.CFrame.Position
									return {
										Instance = part,
										Position = part.Position,
										Normal = (origin - part.Position).Unit,
										Material = Enum.Material.Plastic,
										Distance = (part.Position - origin).Magnitude
									}
								end
							end
							return getgenv()._raycastHook(self, ...)
						end))
					end
					ProjectileModule.newProjectileOfType = function(pType, pData)
						if getgenv()._bulletTraceEnabled and getgenv()._bulletTraceMode ~= "RayCast" and pData and pData.cframe then
							local tar = getgenv()._lockedTarget
							local origin = pData.cframe.Position
							if tar and tar.Character then
								local head = tar.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
								if head then
									local aim
									if getgenv()._predictionEnabled then
										aim = aiPredict(tar, origin, pData.speed or 1000)
									else
										aim = head.Position
									end
									if aim then
										pData.cframe = CFrame.lookAt(origin, aim)
									end
								end
							end
						end
						return getgenv()._oldNewProjectile(pType, pData)
					end
				end)
			else
				if getgenv()._bulletTraceHooked and getgenv()._oldNewProjectile then
					local ProjectileModule = require(ReplicatedStorage.devv.client.Objects.v3item.modules.projectiles)
					ProjectileModule.newProjectileOfType = getgenv()._oldNewProjectile
					getgenv()._bulletTraceHooked = false
				end
				if getgenv()._raycastHook then
					hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", getgenv()._raycastHook)
					getgenv()._raycastHook = nil
				end
				if CoreGui:FindFirstChild("BulletTraceESP") then CoreGui.BulletTraceESP:Destroy() end
				if CoreGui:FindFirstChild("BulletTraceUI") then CoreGui.BulletTraceUI:Destroy() end
				getgenv()._lockedTarget = nil
			end
		end
})

ESPTab:Button({
	Title = T("传送至最近矿石", "Teleport to Nearest Ore"),
	Callback = function()
		local char = LP.Character
		local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not hrp then
			Notify("角色未加载", 2)
			return
		end
		local best, bestDist = nil, math.huge
		for _, prompt in CollectionService:GetTagged("MinePrompt") do
			if not prompt:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then continue end
			local rock = prompt.Parent
			local pos = rock and (rock:IsA("Model") and rock.PrimaryPart and rock.PrimaryPart.Position or (rock:IsA("BasePart") and rock.Position))
			if not pos then continue end
			local d = (hrp.Position - pos).Magnitude
			if d < bestDist then
				bestDist = d
				best = pos
			end
		end
		if best then
			hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(best + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
			Notify("已传送至最近矿石", 2)
		else
			Notify("未找到矿石", 2)
		end
	end
})

PlayerTab:Button({
	Title = T("传送", "Teleport"),
	Callback = function()
		local loc = getgenv().selectedLocation
		if loc and teleportLocations[loc] then
			local targetCF = teleportLocations[loc]
			task.spawn(function()
				local t0 = tick()
				while tick() - t0 < 0.6 do
					local character = LocalPlayer.Character
					local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if hrp then
						character:PivotTo(targetCF)
						hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					end
					task.wait()
				end
			end)
			OhioNotify("已传送到 " .. loc)
		else
			OhioNotify("请先选择一个位置")
		end
	end
})

PlayerTab:Section({ Title = T("目标", "Target") })

playerDropdown = PlayerTab:Dropdown({
	Flag = "wow_093",
	Title = T("选择目标玩家", "Select Target Player"),
	Values = getPlayerListValues(),
	Callback = function(value)
			if value == "All" then
				harassSettings.cycleAll = true
				harassSettings.targetPlayer = nil
				OhioNotify("已选择全部玩家")
				return
			end
			harassSettings.cycleAll = false
			if value and value ~= "" then
				local playerName = value:match("^([^%s]+)")
				if playerName then
					harassSettings.targetPlayer = Players:FindFirstChild(playerName)
					if harassSettings.targetPlayer then
						OhioNotify("已选择: " .. harassSettings.targetPlayer.Name)
					else
						OhioNotify("未找到玩家: " .. playerName)
					end
				end
			else
				harassSettings.targetPlayer = nil
			end
		end
})

PlayerTab:Button({
	Title = T("刷新玩家列表", "Refresh Player List"),
	Callback = function()
			local newValues = getPlayerListValues()
			playerDropdown:Refresh(newValues)
			OhioNotify("已刷新,找到 " .. #newValues .. " 个玩家")
		end
})

PlayerTab:Dropdown({
	Flag = "wow_094",

		end

PlayerTab:Button({
	Title = T("传送至目标", "Teleport to Target"),
	Callback = function()
			local tar = harassSettings.targetPlayer
			if not tar or not LocalPlayer.Character or not tar.Character then return end
			local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local thrp = tar.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not hrp or not thrp then return end
			hrp.CFrame = thrp.CFrame * getOffset()
			OhioNotify("已传送到目标" .. harassSettings.tpMode)
		end
})

PlayerTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_095",
	Title = T("锁定传送", "Lock Teleport"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state)
		_G.lockTp = state
		if state then
			OhioNotify("锁定传送 已开启")
			_G.lockTpToken = (_G.lockTpToken or 0) + 1
			local token = _G.lockTpToken
			task.spawn(function()
				while _G.lockTp and token == _G.lockTpToken do
					local waitTime = 0.05
					local ok = pcall(function()
						local lp = LocalPlayer
						if harassSettings.cycleAll then
							waitTime = harassSettings.cycleDelay
							local list = {}
							for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
								if p ~= lp and p.Character then
									table.insert(list, p)
								end
							end
							if #list > 0 then
								harassSettings.cycleIndex = harassSettings.cycleIndex % #list + 1
								local tar = list[harassSettings.cycleIndex]
								local hrp = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
								local thrp = tar and tar.Character and tar.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
								if hrp and thrp then
									hrp.CFrame = thrp.CFrame * getOffset()
								end
							end
						else
							waitTime = 0.001
							local tar = harassSettings.targetPlayer
							local hrp = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							local thrp = tar and tar.Character and tar.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							if hrp and thrp then
								hrp.CFrame = thrp.CFrame * getOffset()
							end
						end
					end)
					task.wait(ok and waitTime or 0.2)
				end
			end)
		else
			OhioNotify("锁定传送 已关闭")
		end
	end
})

PlayerTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_096",
	Title = T("观战目标", "Spectate Target"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state)
			local tar = harassSettings.targetPlayer
			if state then
				if tar and tar.Character then
					workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = tar.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or tar.Character.HumanoidRootPart
					OhioNotify("观看: " .. tar.Name)
				end
			else
				if LocalPlayer.Character then
					workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
					OhioNotify("已退出观战")
				end
			end
		end
})

PlayerTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_097",
	Title = T("吸人到身边", "Pull Players"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state)
			_G.bringLoop = state
			if state then
				OhioNotify("吸人已开启")
				task.spawn(function()
					while _G.bringLoop do
						local lp = LocalPlayer
						local tar = harassSettings.targetPlayer
						if lp.Character and tar and tar.Character then
							local myHRP = lp.Character.HumanoidRootPart
							local tarHRP = tar.Character.HumanoidRootPart
							if myHRP and tarHRP then
								tarHRP.CFrame = myHRP.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -3)
							end
						end
						task.wait(0.1)
					end
				end)
			else
				OhioNotify("吸人已关闭")
			end
		end
})

						if LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
							LocalPlayer.Character:TranslateBy(LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.MoveDirection * (getgenv().Speed or 1) / 10)
						end

				end)

				_G.sudu:Disconnect()
				_G.sudu = nil
			end

PlayerTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_102",
	Title = T("原地复活", "Respawn in Place"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(v)
			if v == true then
				if _G.RespawnConnection then _G.RespawnConnection:Disconnect() _G.RespawnConnection = nil end
				_G.RespawnConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
					pcall(function()
						local lp = LocalPlayer
						if not lp.Character then return end
						local hum = lp.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
						if not hum then return end
						if hum.Health == 0 and not lp.Character:FindFirstChild("RespawnTag") then
							local pos = lp.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
							local tag = Instance.new("BoolValue")
							tag.Name = "RespawnTag"
							tag.Parent = lp.Character
							repeat task.wait() until lp.Character and tag.Parent ~= lp.Character
							task.wait(1)
							if lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
								lp.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = pos
							end
						end
					end)
				end)
			else
				if _G.RespawnConnection then
					_G.RespawnConnection:Disconnect()
					_G.RespawnConnection = nil
				end
			end
		end
})

ItemTab = Window:Tab({ Title = T("物品", "Items"), Icon = "package" })

ItemTab:Section({ Title = T("生成物品", "Spawn Items") })

ItemDropdown = ItemTab:Dropdown({
	Flag = "wow_108",
	Title = T("选择物品", "Select Item"),
	Values = itemNames,
	Callback = function(Value)
		selectedItem = Value
	end
})

ItemTab:Button({
	Title = T("获取选中物品", "Get Selected Item"),
	Callback = function()
		if selectedItem and selectedItem ~= "" then
			local data = itemData[selectedItem]
			if data then
				local itemToAdd = {}
				for k, v in pairs(data) do
					itemToAdd[k] = v
				end
				itemToAdd.guid = randomString(32)
				addToInventory(itemToAdd)
				Notify("已获取 " .. selectedItem, 2)
			end
		end
	end
})

ItemTab:Button({
	Title = T("重新扫描物品", "Rescan Items"),
	Callback = function()
		local names = {}
		local data = {}
		for _, v in pairs(ReplicatedStorage.devv.shared.Indicies.v3items.bin:GetDescendants()) do
			if v:IsA("ModuleScript") then
				table.insert(names, v.Name)
				local ok, d = pcall(function()
					return require(v)
				end)
				if ok and d then
					data[v.Name] = d
				end
			end
		end
		table.sort(names)
		itemNames = names
		itemData = data
		selectedItem = itemNames[1]
		ItemDropdown:Refresh(itemNames)
		if selectedItem then
			ItemDropdown:Select(selectedItem)
		end
		Notify("扫描到 " .. tostring(#itemNames) .. " 个物品", 2)
	end
})

ItemTab:Section({ Title = T("自动制作", "Auto Craft") })

ItemTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_109",
	Title = T("自动制作飞镖", "Auto Craft Darts"),
	Value = getgenv().autoCraftEnabled,
	Callback = function(state)
			getgenv().autoCraftEnabled = state
			if state then
				task.spawn(function()
					local player = LocalPlayer
					local devv = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("devv"))
					local v3i = devv.load("v3item")
					local Sig = devv.load("Signal")
					local function getGuidByName(name)
						local inv2 = require(ReplicatedStorage.devv.client.Objects.v3item).inventory
						for _, slot in pairs(inv2) do
							if type(slot) == "table" then
								for _, item in pairs(slot) do
									if type(item) == "table" and string.lower(item.name or "") == string.lower(name) then
										return item.guid
									end
								end
							end
						end
						return nil
					end
					while getgenv().autoCraftEnabled do
						if not v3i.inventory.getFromName("Ninja Star") then
							local char = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
							local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							if root then
								root.CFrame = CFrame.new(1654.21472, 5.19297314, -527.222656)
							end
							task.wait(0.2)
							Sig.InvokeServer("attemptPurchase", "Fire Extinguisher")
							task.wait(0.5)
							local extinguisherGuid = getGuidByName("fire extinguisher")
							if extinguisherGuid and not v3i.inventory.getFromName("Ninja Star") then
								Sig.InvokeServer("craftingDisassemble", { [extinguisherGuid] = 1 })
								task.wait(0.3)
							end
							Sig.InvokeServer("claimCraft")
							Sig.InvokeServer("beginCraft", "NinjaStarCraft")
							for i = 1, 21 do
								if not getgenv().autoCraftEnabled then break end
								task.wait(1)
							end
							if not getgenv().autoCraftEnabled then break end
							Sig.InvokeServer("claimCraft")
							local starGuid = getGuidByName("ninja star")
							if starGuid then
								Sig.FireServer("equip", starGuid)
								task.wait(0.3)
							end
							local char2 = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
							local rightHand = char2:WaitForChild("RightHand", 5)
							local root2 = char2:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
							if rightHand and root2 and starGuid then
								local throwOrigin = rightHand.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0)
								local targetPoint = throwOrigin + Vector3.new(50, 0, 0)
								local velocity = (targetPoint - throwOrigin).Unit * 150
								pcall(function()
									local ret = { Sig.InvokeServer("throwSticky", starGuid, "Ninja Star", starGuid, velocity, targetPoint) }
									print(ret[1] or ret[2])
								end)
							end
						else
							task.wait(2)
						end
					end
				end)
			end
		end
})

accNameList = {}
