ShopTab = Window:Tab({ Title = T("商店", "Shop"), Icon = "shopping-cart" })

ShopTab:Section({ Title = T("购买物品", "Buy Items") })

ShopTab:Input({
	Flag = "wow_113",
	Title = T("搜索物品名", "Search Item Name"),
	Value = "",
	Placeholder = "输入物品名",
	Callback = function(Value)
		local text = (Value or ""):lower()
		local filtered = {}
		for _, name in ipairs(buyNames) do
			if text == "" or name:lower():find(text, 1, true) then
				table.insert(filtered, name)
			end
		end
		local exact
		for _, name in ipairs(filtered) do
			if name:lower() == text then
				exact = name
				break
			end
		end
		buyTarget = exact or filtered[1]
		BuyDropdown:Refresh(filtered)
		if buyTarget then
			BuyDropdown:Select(buyTarget)
		end
	end
})

BuyDropdown = ShopTab:Dropdown({
	Flag = "wow_114",
	Title = T("物品列表", "Item List"),
	Value = buyTarget,
	Values = buyNames,
	Callback = function(Value)
		buyTarget = Value
	end
})

ShopTab:Button({
	Title = T("去购买", "Go Buy"),
	Callback = function()
		goBuy()
		if buyLastPos then
			Notify("已传送至 " .. tostring(buyTarget), 2)
		else
			Notify("未找到 " .. tostring(buyTarget), 2)
		end
	end
})

ShopTab:Button({
	Title = T("回传", "Teleport Back"),
	Callback = function()
		goBack()
		Notify("已返回原位", 2)
	end
})

ShopTab:Button({
	Title = T("刷新列表", "Refresh List"),
	Callback = function()
		refreshBuyItems()
		buyTarget = buyNames[1]
		BuyDropdown:Refresh(buyNames)
		if buyTarget then
			BuyDropdown:Select(buyTarget)
		end
		Notify("扫描到 " .. tostring(#buyNames) .. " 个购买点", 2)
	end
})

ShopTab:Section({ Title = T("刷钱", "Money Farm") })

ShopTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_115",
	Title = T("自动售卖", "Auto Sell"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state)
			getgenv()._autoSellEnabled = state
			if not state then return end
			task.spawn(function()
				while getgenv()._autoSellEnabled do
					local ok, v3i = pcall(function()
						return require(ReplicatedStorage.devv).load("v3item")
					end)
					if not ok or not v3i then task.wait(0.5) continue end
					local items = v3i.inventory.items
					if not items then task.wait(0.5) continue end
					for _, v in pairs(items) do
						if not getgenv()._autoSellEnabled then break end
						if type(v) ~= "table" then continue end
						if not v.guid then continue end
						local shouldSell = false
						if v.type == "Holdable" and v.subtype == "gem" and (v.sellPrice or 0) < 25000 then
							shouldSell = true
						elseif v.subtype == "valuable" then
							shouldSell = true
						elseif v.type == "Gun" and (v.cost or 0) < 99999 and v.name ~= "Raygun" then
							shouldSell = true
						end
						if shouldSell then
							require(ReplicatedStorage.devv.client.Helpers.remotes.Signal).FireServer("sellItem", v.guid)
							task.wait(0.1)
						end
					end
					task.wait(0.5)
				end
			end)
		end
})

ShopTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_116",
	Title = T("自动农场", "Auto Farm"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state)
			getgenv()._farmEnabled = state
			if not state then
				if getgenv().currentConnection then
					getgenv().currentConnection:Disconnect()
					getgenv().currentConnection = nil
				end
				return
			end
			local RED_CARD_CENTER = Vector3.new(358.408173, 15.1495256, -1355.27625)
			local RED_CARD_RADIUS = 72.5
			local targetItems = {
				"Dark Matter Gem", "Void Gem", "Diamond Ring", "Diamond", "Rollie", "Watch",
				"Amethyst", "Topaz", "Emerald", "Gold Bar", "Sapphire", "Ruby",
				"Emerald Ring", "Topaz Ring", "Amethyst Ring", "Sapphire Ring", "Ruby Ring",
				"Gold Crown", "Gold Cup", "Pearl Necklace",
				"Money Printer",
				"Gold AK-47", "Gold Deagle", "AS Val", "AUG", "P90", "RPK", "Sawn Off", "Scar L",
				"Flamethrower", "M249 SAW", "Barrett M107", "RPG",
				"Treasure Map",
			}
			local function isTargetItem(name)
				for _, v in ipairs(targetItems) do
					if v == name then return true end
				end
				return false
			end
			task.spawn(function()
				while getgenv()._farmEnabled do
					local lp = LocalPlayer
					local char = lp.Character
					local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if not hrp then task.wait(1) continue end
					local devv = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("devv"))
					local v3i = devv.load("v3item")
					local Sig = devv.load("Signal")
					local actionTaken = false

					if not v3i.inventory.getFromName("Lockpick") then
						getgenv().currentConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
							local h = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							if h then h.CFrame = CFrame.new(660.528442, 3.40811276, -716.48999) end
						end)
						task.wait(0.2)
						Signal.InvokeServer("attemptPurchase", "Lockpick")
						task.wait(0.2)
						getgenv().currentConnection:Disconnect()
						getgenv().currentConnection = nil
						actionTaken = true
					end
					if actionTaken then task.wait(0.1) continue end

					local airdrops = workspace:FindFirstChild("Game") and workspace.Game:FindFirstChild("Airdrops")
					if airdrops then
						for _, airdrop in pairs(airdrops:GetChildren()) do
							if airdrop:FindFirstChild("Airdrop") and airdrop.Airdrop:FindFirstChild("ProximityPrompt") then
								getgenv().currentConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
									local h = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
									if h then h.CFrame = airdrop.Airdrop.CFrame end
								end)
								task.wait(0.2)
								fireproximityprompt(airdrop.Airdrop.ProximityPrompt)
								task.wait(1.8)
								getgenv().currentConnection:Disconnect()
								getgenv().currentConnection = nil
								actionTaken = true
								break
							end
						end
					end
					if actionTaken then task.wait(0.1) continue end

					local bankCash = workspace:FindFirstChild("BankRobbery") and workspace.BankRobbery:FindFirstChild("BankCash")
					if bankCash and bankCash:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
						getgenv().currentConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
							local h = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							if h then h.CFrame = bankCash:GetPivot() end
						end)
						local prompt = bankCash:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt") or bankCash:FindFirstChild("ProximityPrompt", true)
						while getgenv()._farmEnabled and bankCash and bankCash.Parent do
							if prompt then fireproximityprompt(prompt) end
							pcall(function()
								ReplicatedStorage.devv.remoteStorage["stealBankCash"]:FireServer()
								Signal.FireServer("stealBankCash")
							end)
							task.wait(0.1)
						end
						getgenv().currentConnection:Disconnect()
						getgenv().currentConnection = nil
						actionTaken = true
					end
					if actionTaken then task.wait(0.1) continue end

					local targetBombDoor = (workspace:FindFirstChild("BankRobbery") and workspace.BankRobbery:FindFirstChild("VaultDoor")) or (workspace:FindFirstChild("GemRobbery") and workspace.GemRobbery:FindFirstChild("Rubble"))
					if targetBombDoor and targetBombDoor:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
						local tntGuid = nil
						for i, v in next, v3i.inventory.items do
							if v.name == "TNT" then tntGuid = v.guid break end
						end
						if not tntGuid and targetBombDoor.Name == "VaultDoor" then
							getgenv().currentConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
								local h = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
								if h then h.CFrame = CFrame.new(1522.59155, 3.33273768, -911.139343) end
							end)
							task.wait(0.2)
							Signal.InvokeServer("attemptPurchase", "TNT")
							task.wait(0.5)
							for i, v in next, v3i.inventory.items do
								if v.name == "TNT" then tntGuid = v.guid break end
							end
							getgenv().currentConnection:Disconnect()
							getgenv().currentConnection = nil
						end
						if tntGuid then
							local standCFrame, throwTarget
							if targetBombDoor.Name == "VaultDoor" then
								standCFrame = targetBombDoor:GetPivot() * CFrame.new(0, 0, -9)
								throwTarget = Vector3.new(1123.6, 5.2, -336.2)
							else
								standCFrame = CFrame.new(1694, 22, -725)
								throwTarget = Vector3.new(1700, 16, -721)
							end
							getgenv().currentConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
								local h = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
								if h then h.CFrame = standCFrame end
							end)
							Signal.FireServer("equip", tntGuid)
							task.wait(0.2)
							Signal.FireServer("throwItem", tntGuid, Vector3.new(5.2, 29.9, 79.3), throwTarget)
							task.wait(0.5)
							Signal.FireServer("removeItem", tntGuid)
							getgenv().currentConnection:Disconnect()
							getgenv().currentConnection = nil
							actionTaken = true
						end
					end
					if actionTaken then task.wait(0.1) continue end

					local itemFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Game") and workspace.Game:FindFirstChild("Entities") and workspace.Game.Entities:FindFirstChild("ItemPickup")
					if itemFolder then
						for _, model in pairs(itemFolder:GetChildren()) do
							local itemName = model:GetAttribute("itemName")
							if not itemName or not isTargetItem(itemName) then continue end
							local part = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Part")
							if not part then continue end
							if (part.Position - RED_CARD_CENTER).Magnitude < RED_CARD_RADIUS then continue end
							getgenv().currentConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
								local h = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
								if h then h.CFrame = CFrame.new(part.Position) end
							end)
							task.wait(0.1)
							local detector = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("ClickDetector")
							if not detector then
								local p = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Part")
								if p then detector = p:FindFirstChildOfClass("ClickDetector") end
							end
							if detector then
								fireclickdetector(detector)
							end
							getgenv().currentConnection:Disconnect()
							getgenv().currentConnection = nil
							actionTaken = true
							break
						end
					end
					if actionTaken then task.wait(0.1) continue end

					local regFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Game") and workspace.Game:FindFirstChild("Props") and workspace.Game.Props:FindFirstChild("CashRegister")
					if regFolder then
						local aliveRegs = {}
						for _, v in pairs(regFolder:GetChildren()) do
							if v:IsA("Model") and v:GetAttribute("state") ~= "destroyed" then
								table.insert(aliveRegs, v)
							end
						end
						if #aliveRegs > 0 then
							local fistsGuid = nil
							for _, list in pairs(v3i.inventory) do
								if type(list) == "table" then
									for _, item in pairs(list) do
										if type(item) == "table" and string.lower(item.name or "") == "fists" then
											fistsGuid = item.guid
										end
									end
								end
							end
							local target = aliveRegs[math.random(1, #aliveRegs)]
							getgenv().currentConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
								local h = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
								if h then h.CFrame = target.WorldPivot * CFrame.new(0, -5, 0) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), 0, 0) end
							end)
							local startTime = tick()
							while tick() - startTime < 1.2 and getgenv()._farmEnabled do
								if fistsGuid then Signal.FireServer("equip", fistsGuid) end
								local chrp = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
								for _, reg in pairs(aliveRegs) do
									local rGuid = reg:GetAttribute("guid")
									if rGuid and reg:GetAttribute("state") ~= "destroyed" and chrp and (chrp.Position - reg.WorldPivot.Position).Magnitude <= 30 then
										pcall(function()
											local attackName = tostring(getupvalue(Signal.FireServer, 1)["attackMeleeHit"])
											ReplicatedStorage.devv.remoteStorage[attackName]:FireServer("prop", { meleeType = "meleepunch", guid = rGuid })
										end)
									end
								end
								task.wait(0.05)
							end
							getgenv().currentConnection:Disconnect()
							getgenv().currentConnection = nil
							actionTaken = true
						end
					end
					if actionTaken then task.wait(0.1) continue end

					local function farmProp(folderName)
						local folder = workspace:FindFirstChild(folderName)
						if folder then
							local validTargets = {}
							for _, v in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
								if v:IsA("Model") and v:GetAttribute("health") ~= 0 then
									table.insert(validTargets, v)
								end
							end
							if #validTargets > 0 then
								local target = validTargets[math.random(1, #validTargets)]
								getgenv().currentConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
									local h = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
									if h then h.CFrame = target:GetPivot() * CFrame.new(0, -5, 0) end
								end)
								task.wait(0.8)
								if target and target.Parent then target:SetAttribute("health", 0) end
								task.wait(1)
								getgenv().currentConnection:Disconnect()
								getgenv().currentConnection = nil
								return true
							end
						end
						return false
					end
					if farmProp("ATMs") then actionTaken = true end
					if not actionTaken and farmProp("Cashiers") then actionTaken = true end
					if actionTaken then task.wait(0.1) continue end

					local safeTypes = { "LargeSafe", "MediumSafe", "SmallSafe", "JewelSafe", "GoldJewelSafe" }
					for _, sType in pairs(safeTypes) do
						local safeFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Game") and workspace.Game:FindFirstChild("Entities") and workspace.Game.Entities:FindFirstChild(sType)
						if safeFolder then
							for _, safe in pairs(safeFolder:GetChildren()) do
								local prompt = safe:FindFirstChild("ProximityPrompt", true)
								if prompt and prompt.Enabled then
									local targetPos = (safe:IsA("Model") and safe.PrimaryPart) and safe.PrimaryPart.Position or safe.WorldPivot.Position
									getgenv().currentConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
										local h = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
										if h then h.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos) end
									end)
									task.wait(0.3)
									fireproximityprompt(prompt)
									task.wait(1)
									for r = 1, 15 do
										if not getgenv()._farmEnabled then break end
										local foundDrop = false
										for _, t in pairs(workspace.Game.Entities:GetDescendants()) do
											if (t:IsA("BasePart") and t.Name == "Cash") or (t:IsA("Model") and (t.Name == "GoldBar" or string.find(t.Name, "Gem") or string.find(t.Name, "Diamond"))) then
												foundDrop = true
												local dropPart = t:IsA("Model") and t.PrimaryPart or t
												if dropPart then
													getgenv().currentConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
														local h = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
														if h then h.CFrame = CFrame.new(dropPart.Position) end
													end)
													local chrp = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
													firetouchinterest(dropPart, chrp, 0)
													firetouchinterest(dropPart, chrp, 1)
													task.wait(0.1)
												end
											end
										end
										if not foundDrop then break end
									end
									getgenv().currentConnection:Disconnect()
									getgenv().currentConnection = nil
									actionTaken = true
									break
								end
							end
						end
						if actionTaken then break end
					end
					if actionTaken then task.wait(0.1) continue end

					if v3i.inventory.ordered and #v3i.inventory.ordered > 20 then
						for _, itemGuid in pairs(v3i.inventory.ordered) do
							if not getgenv()._farmEnabled then break end
							Signal.FireServer("sellItem", itemGuid)
							task.wait(0.05)
						end
					end

					if not actionTaken then
						getgenv().currentConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
							local h = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							if h then h.CFrame = CFrame.new(1432.93445, 6.09644604, -367.879089) end
						end)
						local jobPrompt = workspace.Jobs and workspace.Jobs.ShoeStore and workspace.Jobs.ShoeStore.Triggers and workspace.Jobs.ShoeStore.Triggers.BeginJobTrigger and workspace.Jobs.ShoeStore.Triggers.BeginJobTrigger.ProximityPrompt
						if jobPrompt then fireproximityprompt(jobPrompt) end
						local rubbishFolder = workspace.Game and workspace.Game.Local and workspace.Game.Local:FindFirstChild("Rubbish")
						if rubbishFolder then
							for _, v in pairs(rubbishFolder:GetChildren()) do
								local guid = v:GetAttribute("guid")
								if guid then
									Signal.FireServer("cleanRubbish", guid)
									v:Destroy()
								end
							end
						end
						task.wait(0.5)
						getgenv().currentConnection:Disconnect()
						getgenv().currentConnection = nil
					end
					task.wait(0.5)
				end
			end)
		end
})

ShopTab:Toggle({

				LocalPlayer.Idled:Connect(function()

					wait(1)
					game:GetService("VirtualUser"):Button2Up(Vector2.new(0, 0), workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame)
				end)

			end
		end
})

ShopTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_119",
	Title = T("自动炸银行门", "Auto Bomb Bank Door"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state)
			getgenv()._bankRobRunning = state
			local running = state
			local lp = LocalPlayer
			local devv = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("devv"))
			local Sig = devv.load("Signal")
			local function getTNTGuid()
				local lastGuid = nil
				for i, v in next, devv.load("v3item").inventory.items do
					if v.name == "TNT" then
						lastGuid = v.guid
					end
				end
				return lastGuid
			end
			local function dropTNT(guid)
				Sig.FireServer("removeItem", guid)
			end
			local function buyTNT()
				Sig.InvokeServer("attemptPurchase", "TNT")
			end
			local function executeRob()
				if not getgenv()._bankRobRunning then return end
				local tntGuid = getTNTGuid()
				if not tntGuid then
					if getgenv()._buyBind then getgenv()._buyBind:Disconnect() getgenv()._buyBind = nil end
					getgenv()._buyBind = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
						if not getgenv()._bankRobRunning then
							if getgenv()._buyBind then getgenv()._buyBind:Disconnect() getgenv()._buyBind = nil end
							return
						end
						local hrp = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						if hrp then
							hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(1522.59155, 3.33273768, -911.139343)
						end
						buyTNT()
						if getTNTGuid() then
							if getgenv()._buyBind then getgenv()._buyBind:Disconnect() getgenv()._buyBind = nil end
						end
					end)
					while not getTNTGuid() and getgenv()._bankRobRunning do
						task.wait(0.01)
					end
					if not getgenv()._bankRobRunning then return end
					tntGuid = getTNTGuid()
					if not tntGuid then return end
				end
				local vaultPath = workspace:FindFirstChild("BankRobbery")
				if not vaultPath then return end
				local vaultDoor = vaultPath:FindFirstChild("VaultDoor")
				if not vaultDoor then return end
				if getgenv()._vaultBind then getgenv()._vaultBind:Disconnect() getgenv()._vaultBind = nil end
				getgenv()._vaultBind = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
					if not getgenv()._bankRobRunning then
						if getgenv()._vaultBind then getgenv()._vaultBind:Disconnect() getgenv()._vaultBind = nil end
						return
					end
					local hrp = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if hrp and vaultDoor:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
						hrp.CFrame = vaultDoor:GetPivot() * CFrame.new(0, 0, -9)
					else
						if getgenv()._vaultBind then getgenv()._vaultBind:Disconnect() getgenv()._vaultBind = nil end
					end
				end)
				Sig.FireServer("equip", tntGuid)
				task.wait(0.1)
				Sig.FireServer("throwItem", tntGuid, Vector3.new(5.2671332359313965, 29.919464111328125, 79.32382202148438), Vector3.new(1123.618408203125, 5.221871376037598, -336.22491455078125))
				task.wait(0.5)
				dropTNT(tntGuid)
				if getgenv()._vaultBind then getgenv()._vaultBind:Disconnect() getgenv()._vaultBind = nil end
			end
			if state then
				task.spawn(function()
					while getgenv()._bankRobRunning do
						executeRob()
						if not getgenv()._bankRobRunning then break end
						task.wait(5)
					end
				end)
			else
				getgenv()._bankRobRunning = false
				if getgenv()._vaultBind then getgenv()._vaultBind:Disconnect() getgenv()._vaultBind = nil end
				if getgenv()._buyBind then getgenv()._buyBind:Disconnect() getgenv()._buyBind = nil end
			end
		end
})

ShopTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_120",
	Title = T("自动炸珠宝店岩石", "Auto Bomb Jewelry Rocks"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state)
			getgenv()._bankRobRunning = state
			local running = state
			local lp = LocalPlayer
			local devv = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("devv"))
			local Sig = devv.load("Signal")
			local function getTNTGuid()
				local lastGuid = nil
				for i, v in next, devv.load("v3item").inventory.items do
					if v.name == "TNT" then
						lastGuid = v.guid
					end
				end
				return lastGuid
			end
			local function dropTNT(guid)
				Sig.FireServer("removeItem", guid)
			end
			local function buyTNT()
				Sig.InvokeServer("attemptPurchase", "TNT")
			end
			local function executeRob()
				if not getgenv()._bankRobRunning then return end
				local tntGuid = getTNTGuid()
				if not tntGuid then
					if getgenv()._buyBind then getgenv()._buyBind:Disconnect() getgenv()._buyBind = nil end
					getgenv()._buyBind = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
						if not getgenv()._bankRobRunning then
							if getgenv()._buyBind then getgenv()._buyBind:Disconnect() getgenv()._buyBind = nil end
							return
						end
						local hrp = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						if hrp then
							hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(1522.59155, 3.33273768, -911.139343)
						end
						buyTNT()
						if getTNTGuid() then
							if getgenv()._buyBind then getgenv()._buyBind:Disconnect() getgenv()._buyBind = nil end
						end
					end)
					while not getTNTGuid() and getgenv()._bankRobRunning do
						task.wait(0.01)
					end
					if not getgenv()._bankRobRunning then return end
					tntGuid = getTNTGuid()
					if not tntGuid then return end
				end
				local vaultPath = workspace:FindFirstChild("GemRobbery")
				if not vaultPath then return end
				local vaultDoor = vaultPath:FindFirstChild("Rubble")
				if not vaultDoor then return end
				if getgenv()._vaultBind then getgenv()._vaultBind:Disconnect() getgenv()._vaultBind = nil end
				getgenv()._vaultBind = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
					if not getgenv()._bankRobRunning then
						if getgenv()._vaultBind then getgenv()._vaultBind:Disconnect() getgenv()._vaultBind = nil end
						return
					end
					local hrp = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if hrp and vaultDoor:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
						hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(1694, 22, -725)
					else
						if getgenv()._vaultBind then getgenv()._vaultBind:Disconnect() getgenv()._vaultBind = nil end
					end
				end)
				Sig.FireServer("equip", tntGuid)
				task.wait(0.1)
				Sig.FireServer("throwItem", tntGuid, Vector3.new(5.2671332359313965, 29.919464111328125, 79.32382202148438), Vector3.new(1700, 16, -721))
				task.wait(0.5)
				dropTNT(tntGuid)
				if getgenv()._vaultBind then getgenv()._vaultBind:Disconnect() getgenv()._vaultBind = nil end
			end
			if state then
				task.spawn(function()
					while getgenv()._bankRobRunning do
						executeRob()
						if not getgenv()._bankRobRunning then break end
						task.wait(5)
					end
				end)
			else
				getgenv()._bankRobRunning = false
				if getgenv()._vaultBind then getgenv()._vaultBind:Disconnect() getgenv()._vaultBind = nil end
				if getgenv()._buyBind then getgenv()._buyBind:Disconnect() getgenv()._buyBind = nil end
			end
		end
})

ShopTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_121",
	Title = T("自动购买撬锁", "Auto Buy Lockpick"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state)
			local running = state
			if state then
				task.spawn(function()
					while running do
						local v3i = require(ReplicatedStorage.devv).load("v3item")
						if not v3i.inventory.getFromName("Lockpick") then
							local originalCFrame = LocalPlayer.Character:GetPivot()
							LocalPlayer.Character:PivotTo(CFrame.new(660.528442, 3.40811276, -716.48999))
							task.wait(0.01)
							require(ReplicatedStorage.devv).load("Signal").InvokeServer("attemptPurchase", "Lockpick")
							task.wait(0.01)
							LocalPlayer.Character:PivotTo(originalCFrame)
						end
						task.wait(0.01)
					end
				end)
			end
		end
})

ShopTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_122",
	Title = T("自动收银机", "Auto Cash Register"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state)
			getgenv().autocash = state
			if not state then return end
			task.spawn(function()
				while getgenv().autocash do
					local g
					for _, l in pairs(require(ReplicatedStorage.devv.client.Objects.v3item).inventory) do
						if type(l) == "table" then
							for _, i in pairs(l) do
								if type(i) == "table" and string.lower(i.name or "") == "fists" then
									g = i.guid
								end
							end
						end
					end
					if g then
						pcall(function()
							require(ReplicatedStorage.devv).load("Signal").FireServer("equip", g)
						end)
					end
					pcall(function()
						local cashBundle = workspace.Game and workspace.Game.Entities and workspace.Game.Entities:FindFirstChild("CashBundle")
						if cashBundle then
							for _, b in pairs(cashBundle:GetChildren()) do
								local c = b:FindFirstChildOfClass("ClickDetector") or (b.PrimaryPart and b.PrimaryPart:FindFirstChildOfClass("ClickDetector"))
								if c then fireclickdetector(c) end
							end
						end
					end)
					local h = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if h then
						local t = {}
						pcall(function()
							local regFolder = workspace.Game and workspace.Game.Props and workspace.Game.Props:FindFirstChild("CashRegister")
							if regFolder then
								for _, r in pairs(regFolder:GetChildren()) do
									if r:GetAttribute("state") ~= "destroyed" then
										table.insert(t, r)
									end
								end
							end
						end)
						if #t > 0 then
							local r = t[math.random(1, #t)]
							h.CFrame = r.WorldPivot * CFrame.new(0, -5, 0) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), 0, 0)
							local st = tick()
							while tick() - st < 1.2 and getgenv().autocash do
								for _, x in pairs(t) do
									local rg = x:GetAttribute("guid")
									if rg and x:GetAttribute("state") ~= "destroyed" and (h.Position - x.WorldPivot.Position).Magnitude <= 30 then
										pcall(function()
											local attackName = tostring(getupvalue(require(ReplicatedStorage.devv.client.Helpers.remotes.Signal).FireServer, 1)["attackMeleeHit"])
											ReplicatedStorage.devv.remoteStorage[attackName]:FireServer("prop", { meleeType = "meleepunch", guid = rg })
										end)
									end
								end
								task.wait(0.05)
							end
						else
							task.wait(2)
						end
					end
					task.wait(0.1)
				end
			end)
		end
})

ShopTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_125",
	Title = T("自动放置特殊印钞机", "Auto Place Special Printer"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state)
			getgenv().PrinterLoop = state
			if state then
				task.spawn(function()
					while getgenv().PrinterLoop do
						local inv2 = require(ReplicatedStorage.devv.client.Objects.v3item).inventory
						local guid
						for _, list in pairs(inv2) do
							if type(list) == "table" then
								for _, item in pairs(list) do
									if type(item) == "table" and string.lower(item.name or "") == "unusual money printer" then
										guid = item.guid
									end
								end
							end
						end
						if guid then
							for i = 1, 10 do
								require(ReplicatedStorage.devv.client.Helpers.remotes.Signal).FireServer("equip", guid)
								task.wait()
							end
							require(ReplicatedStorage.devv.client.Helpers.remotes.Signal).FireServer("dropItem", guid)
							task.wait(1.3)
							local devv = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("devv"))
							local Sig = devv.load("Signal")
							local v3i = devv.load("v3item")
							for _, item in pairs(v3i.inventory.items) do
								if item.name == "Unusual Money Printer" and item.guid then
									Sig.FireServer("removeItem", item.guid)
									task.wait(0.05)
								end
							end
						end
						task.wait(0.01)
					end
				end)
			end
		end
})

ShopTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_126",
	Title = T("自动印钞机", "Auto Money Printer"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state)
			getgenv().AutoMoneyEnabled = state
			while task.wait(0.01) do
				if not getgenv().AutoMoneyEnabled then
					break
				end
				local found = false
				if LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
					local original = LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
					local cashBundle = workspace.Game and workspace.Game.Entities and workspace.Game.Entities:FindFirstChild("CashBundle")
					if cashBundle then
						for _, l in ipairs(cashBundle:GetChildren()) do
							if l:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("IntValue") and l:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("IntValue").Value >= 1000 and l:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Part") then
								LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = l:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Part").CFrame
								task.wait(0.05)
								LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = original
								found = true
								break
							end
						end
					end
				end
				if found then
					continue
				end
				local droppables = workspace.Game and workspace.Game.Local and workspace.Game.Local:FindFirstChild("droppables")
				if droppables and droppables:FindFirstChild("Money Printer") then
					for _, child in pairs(droppables["Money Printer"]:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("MeshPart") and LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
							LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(child.Position)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
})

ShopTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_128",
	Title = T("自动撬车", "Auto Lockpick Cars"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state)
			getgenv().z = state
			if not state then return end

				task.wait(0.01)
				task.spawn(function()
					local vehicles = workspace.Game and workspace.Game.Entities and workspace.Game.Entities:FindFirstChild("vehicles")
					if not vehicles then return end
					for _, veh in pairs(vehicles:GetChildren()) do
						if not getgenv().z then break end
						if not veh:IsA("Model") then continue end
						for _, desc in pairs(veh:GetDescendants()) do
							if desc:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
								desc.HoldDuration = 0
								if not require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("devv")).load("v3item").inventory.getFromName("Lockpick") then
									getgenv().c = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
										if LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
											LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart").CFrame = CFrame.new(660.528442, 3.40811276, -716.48999)
										end
									end)
									task.wait(0.2)
									require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("devv")).load("Signal").InvokeServer("attemptPurchase", "Lockpick")
									task.wait(0.2)
									getgenv().c:Disconnect()
									getgenv().c = nil
								end
								task.wait(0.1)
								if require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("devv")).load("v3item").inventory.getFromName("Lockpick") then
									getgenv().c = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
										if LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
											LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart").CFrame = veh:GetPivot() * CFrame.new(0, 0, -4)
										end
									end)
									task.wait(0.2)
									require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("devv")).load("Signal").FireServer("equip", require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("devv")).load("v3item").inventory.getFromName("Lockpick").guid)
									task.wait(0.3)
									fireproximityprompt(desc)
									task.wait(1.5)
									getgenv().c:Disconnect()
									getgenv().c = nil
								end
								break
							end
						end
					end
				end)
			end
		end
})

ShopTab:Dropdown({
	Flag = "wow_130",
	Title = T("选择银行模式", "Select Bank Mode"),
	Value = "Auto",
	Values = { "Auto", "AFK" },
	Callback = function(v)
			getgenv().bankType = v
		end
})

ShopTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_131",
	Title = T("自动抢银行", "Auto Bank Rob"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(enabled)
			getgenv()._autoBank = enabled
			if getgenv()._bankAddedConn then getgenv()._bankAddedConn:Disconnect() end
			if getgenv()._bankBind then getgenv()._bankBind:Disconnect() end
			if getgenv()._afkBankBind then getgenv()._afkBankBind:Disconnect() end
			if getgenv()._autoBankLoop then task.cancel(getgenv()._autoBankLoop) end
			if not enabled then
				return
			end
			local targetEvent = ReplicatedStorage.devv.remoteStorage[utf8.char(115, 116, 101, 97, 1231, 66, 97, 110, 107, 67, 97, 115, 104)]
			local bankType = getgenv().bankType or "Auto"
			if bankType == "AFK" then
				local afkPosition = CFrame.new(1653.3216552734375, -16.953155517578125, -529.6856079101562)
				getgenv()._autoBankLoop = task.spawn(function()
					while getgenv()._autoBank do
						local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						if hrp then
							local bankFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("BankRobbery")
							local hasCash = bankFolder and bankFolder:FindFirstChild("BankCash")
							if hasCash then
								if getgenv()._afkBankBind then getgenv()._afkBankBind:Disconnect() end
								getgenv()._afkBankBind = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
									local currentHrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
									if currentHrp and hasCash:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
										currentHrp.CFrame = hasCash:GetPivot()
									else
										getgenv()._afkBankBind:Disconnect()
									end
								end)
								for _ = 1, 500 do
									if not getgenv()._autoBank then break end
									targetEvent:FireServer()
									task.wait(0.01)
								end
								if getgenv()._afkBankBind then getgenv()._afkBankBind:Disconnect() end
							else
								hrp.CFrame = afkPosition
							end
						end
						task.wait(5)
					end
				end)
				return
			end
			local bankFolder = workspace:WaitForChild("BankRobbery", 5)
			if not bankFolder then return end
			local function executeRobbery(model)
				if getgenv()._bankBind then getgenv()._bankBind:Disconnect() end
				getgenv()._bankBind = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
					local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if hrp and model:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
						hrp.CFrame = model:GetPivot()
					else
						getgenv()._bankBind:Disconnect()
					end
				end)
				task.spawn(function()
					for _ = 1, 500 do
						if not getgenv()._autoBank then break end
						targetEvent:FireServer()
						task.wait(0.01)
					end
				end)
			end
			getgenv()._autoBankLoop = task.spawn(function()
				while getgenv()._autoBank do
					local existingCash = bankFolder:FindFirstChild("BankCash")
					if existingCash then
						executeRobbery(existingCash)
					end
					task.wait(5)
				end
			end)
		end
})

ShopTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_132",
	Title = T("现金堆光环", "Cash Pile Aura"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(enabled)
			getgenv().isRunning = enabled
			if enabled then
				local remote = ReplicatedStorage.devv.remoteStorage[utf8.char(115, 116, 101, 97, 1231, 66, 97, 110, 107, 67, 97, 115, 104)]
				task.spawn(function()
					while getgenv().isRunning do
						for i = 1, 10 do
							remote:FireServer()
						end
						task.wait(0.05)
					end
				end)
			end
		end
})

ShopTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_134",
	Title = T("自动保险柜", "Auto Safe"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state)
			local a = state
			local d = LocalPlayer
			if state then
				task.spawn(function()
					local e = d.Character or d.CharacterAdded:Wait()
					local f = e:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					local b = f.CFrame
					local c = require(ReplicatedStorage.devv).load("v3item").inventory.getEquippedItem()
					c = c and c.guid
					while a do
						e = d.Character or d.CharacterAdded:Wait()
						f = e:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						local h = require(ReplicatedStorage.devv).load("v3item")
						local lockpick = h.inventory.getFromName("Lockpick")
						if lockpick == nil or lockpick == false then
							local i = f.CFrame
							f.CFrame = CFrame.new(660.528442, 3.40811276, -716.48999)
							task.wait(0.01)
							require(ReplicatedStorage.devv).load("Signal").InvokeServer("attemptPurchase", "Lockpick")
							task.wait(0.01)
							f.CFrame = i
						end
						task.wait(0.2)
						local j = {}
						local k = { "LargeSafe", "MediumSafe", "SmallSafe", "JewelSafe", "GoldJewelSafe" }
						for _, l in pairs(k) do
							local m = workspace.Game and workspace.Game.Entities:FindFirstChild(l)
							if m then
								for _, n in pairs(m:GetChildren()) do
									table.insert(j, n)
								end
							end
						end
						for _, o in pairs(j) do
							if not a then break end
							local p = o:FindFirstChild("ProximityPrompt", true)
							if p and p.Enabled then
								local q
								if o:IsA("Model") and o.PrimaryPart then
									q = o.PrimaryPart.Position
								else
									q = o.WorldPivot.Position
								end
								f.CFrame = CFrame.new(q)
								task.wait(0.3)
								fireproximityprompt(p)
								task.wait(1)
								for r = 1, 20 do
									if not a then break end
									local s = false
									for _, t in pairs(workspace.Game.Entities:GetDescendants()) do
										if not a then break end
										if t:IsA("BasePart") and t.Name == "Cash" then
											s = true
											f.CFrame = CFrame.new(t.Position)
											firetouchinterest(t, e:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), 0)
											firetouchinterest(t, e:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), 1)
											task.wait(0.1)
										elseif t:IsA("Model") and (t.Name == "GoldBar" or t.Name == "Diamond" or t.Name == "Emerald" or t.Name == "Ruby" or t.Name == "Sapphire" or t.Name == "Amethyst" or t.Name == "Topaz" or string.find(t.Name, "Ring") or string.find(t.Name, "Gem") or t.Name == "Watch" or t.Name == "Rollie") then
											s = true
											if t.PrimaryPart then
												f.CFrame = CFrame.new(t.PrimaryPart.Position)
												firetouchinterest(t.PrimaryPart, e:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), 0)
												firetouchinterest(t.PrimaryPart, e:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), 1)
												task.wait(0.1)
											end
										end
									end
									if not s then break end
									task.wait(0.2)
								end
								task.wait(0.5)
							end
						end
						task.wait(1)
					end
					if b then f.CFrame = b end
					if c then require(ReplicatedStorage.devv).load("Signal").FireServer("equip", c) end
				end)
			end
		end
})

ShopTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_135",
	Title = T("自动老虎机", "Auto Slot Machine"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state)
			getgenv().slotMachineEnabled = state
			if state then
				task.spawn(function()
					while getgenv().slotMachineEnabled do
						local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						if not hrp then continue end
						local originalCFrame = hrp.CFrame
						local ServerFurniture = workspace:FindFirstChild("ServerFurniture")
						local slotSpins = LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("slotSpins") or 0
						if ServerFurniture then
							for _, furniture in pairs(ServerFurniture:GetDescendants()) do
								if not getgenv().slotMachineEnabled then break end
								if furniture:GetAttribute("furnitureName") == "SlotMachine" then
									local attach = furniture:FindFirstChild("Attachment", true)
									local prompt = attach and attach.ProximityPrompt
									if prompt then
										prompt.MaxActivationDistance = 40
										hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(846.239685, 0.435377538, -919.226746) * CFrame.new(10, -1.6, -5)
										while getgenv().slotMachineEnabled and (LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("slotSpins") or 0) > 0 do
											fireproximityprompt(prompt)
											task.wait(0.5)
										end
										break
									end
								end
							end
						end
						hrp.CFrame = originalCFrame
						task.wait(1)
					end
				end)
			end
		end
})

											end
										end
										task.wait(0.5)

								end

						end

					end
				end)

	Callback = function(state)

			local function openLuckyBlocks()
				for i, v in next, v3item.inventory.items do
					if table.find(luckyBlockItems, v.name) then
						local useid = v.guid
						pcall(function()
							Signal.FireServer("equip", useid)
							task.wait(0.1)
							Signal.FireServer("useConsumable", useid)
							task.wait(0.1)
							Signal.FireServer("removeItem", useid)
						end)
						break
					end
				end
			end
			while task.wait(0.05) do
				if not getgenv().AutoOpenLuckyBlock then break end
				pcall(openLuckyBlocks)
			end
		end

ShopTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_146",
	Title = T("自动领取空投", "Auto Claim Airdrop"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state)
			getgenv().AutoPickAirdrop = state
			if not state then return end
			local function teleportToAirdrop()
				local character = LocalPlayer.Character
				if not character then return false end
				local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not rootPart then return false end
				local originalPosition = rootPart.CFrame
				local foundAirdrop = false
				local airdrops = workspace:FindFirstChild("Game") and workspace.Game:FindFirstChild("Airdrops") and workspace.Game.Airdrops:GetChildren() or {}
				for _, airdrop in pairs(airdrops) do
					if airdrop:FindFirstChild("Airdrop") and airdrop.Airdrop:FindFirstChild("ProximityPrompt") then
						local prompt = airdrop.Airdrop.ProximityPrompt
						prompt.RequiresLineOfSight = false
						prompt.HoldDuration = 0
						rootPart.CFrame = airdrop.Airdrop.CFrame
						task.wait(0.1)
						for i = 1, 15 do
							if fireproximityprompt then fireproximityprompt(prompt) else firesignal(prompt.Triggered) end
							task.wait(0.02)
						end
						foundAirdrop = true
						break
					end
				end
				if not foundAirdrop then
					rootPart.CFrame = originalPosition
				else
					task.wait(0.3)
					rootPart.CFrame = originalPosition
				end
				return foundAirdrop
			end
			while task.wait(0.1) do
				if not getgenv().AutoPickAirdrop then break end
				local collected = teleportToAirdrop()
				if not collected then
					for i = 1, 10 do
						if not getgenv().AutoPickAirdrop then break end
						task.wait(0.1)
					end
				else
					for i = 1, 3 do
						if not getgenv().AutoPickAirdrop then break end
						task.wait(0.1)
					end
				end
			end
		end
})

ShopTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_147",
	Title = T("自动租房", "Auto Rent"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state)
			getgenv().AutoRentHouse = state

			while task.wait(0.1) do
				if not getgenv().AutoRentHouse then break end
				local currentTime = tick()
				if currentTime - lastRentTime >= 2 then
					pcall(function()
						for _, v in pairs(workspace.HousingPlots:GetChildren()) do
							if not v:GetAttribute("Owner") then
								Signal.InvokeServer("rentHouse", v)
							end
						end
					end)
					lastRentTime = currentTime
				end
			end
		end
})

ShopTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_150",
	Title = T("自动出售背包", "Auto Sell Backpack"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state)
			getgenv().AutoSellAll = state
			if not state then return end
			local lastSellTime = tick()
			local function autoSellItems()
				for i, v in next, v3item.inventory.items do
					local sellid = v.guid
					pcall(function()
						Signal.FireServer("equip", sellid)
						Signal.FireServer("sellItem", sellid)
					end)
				end
			end
			while task.wait(0.1) do
				if not getgenv().AutoSellAll then break end
				local currentTime = tick()
				if currentTime - lastSellTime >= 1 then
					pcall(autoSellItems)
					lastSellTime = currentTime
				end
			end
		end
})

ShopTab:Section({ Title = T("活动", "Events") })

ShopTab:Button({
	Title = T("自动捡彩蛋", "Auto Pick Eggs"),
	Callback = function()
			task.spawn(function()
				local ClientEaster = require(ReplicatedStorage.devv.client.Handlers.Easter.ClientEaster)
				local eggsData = debug.getupvalue(ClientEaster.Initialize, 1)
				while true do
					task.wait(0.5)
					for eggName, eggPos in pairs(eggsData) do
						local easterData = Signal.InvokeServer("getEasterData")
						if easterData and not easterData.eggs[eggName] then
							if LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart then
								LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(eggPos)
								task.wait(0.2)
								Signal.InvokeServer("attemptCollectEgg", eggName)
							end
							break
						end
					end
				end
			end)
			OhioNotify("自动捡彩蛋 已开启")
		end
})

SkinTab:Dropdown({
	Flag = "wow_160",
	Title = T("选择动画包", "Select Animation Pack"),
	Values = animList,
	Callback = function(Value)
			if not Value or Value == "" then return end
			local lp = LocalPlayer
			if not lp or not lp.Character or not lp.Character:FindFirstChild("Animate") then return end
			local Animate = lp.Character:FindFirstChild("Animate")
			Animate.Disabled = true
			if type(StopAnim) == "function" then StopAnim() end
			local pack = AnimationPackages[Value]
			if pack then
				if Animate:FindFirstChild("idle") and Animate.idle:FindFirstChild("Animation1") then Animate.idle.Animation1.AnimationId = pack.idle1 end
				if Animate:FindFirstChild("idle") and Animate.idle:FindFirstChild("Animation2") then Animate.idle.Animation2.AnimationId = pack.idle2 end
				if Animate:FindFirstChild("walk") and Animate.walk:FindFirstChild("WalkAnim") then Animate.walk.WalkAnim.AnimationId = pack.walk end
				if Animate:FindFirstChild("run") and Animate.run:FindFirstChild("RunAnim") then Animate.run.RunAnim.AnimationId = pack.run end
				if Animate:FindFirstChild("jump") and Animate.jump:FindFirstChild("JumpAnim") then Animate.jump.JumpAnim.AnimationId = pack.jump end
				if Animate:FindFirstChild("climb") and Animate.climb:FindFirstChild("ClimbAnim") then Animate.climb.ClimbAnim.AnimationId = pack.climb end
				if Animate:FindFirstChild("fall") and Animate.fall:FindFirstChild("FallAnim") then Animate.fall.FallAnim.AnimationId = pack.fall end
			end
			if lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid") then
				lp.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid"):ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
			end
			Animate.Disabled = false
		end
})

SkinTab:Dropdown({
	Flag = "wow_161",
	Title = T("天空颜色", "Sky Color"),
	Values = { "血天空", "金天空", "红天空", "橙天空", "黄天空", "绿天空", "蓝天空", "靛天空", "紫天空" },
	Callback = function(v)
			if getgenv().skyLock then getgenv().skyLock:Disconnect() getgenv().skyLock = nil end
			local a = game:GetService("Lighting").Atmosphere
			local c
			if v == "血天空" then c = Color3.fromRGB(252, 3, 90)
			elseif v == "金天空" then c = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0)
			elseif v == "红天空" then c = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
			elseif v == "橙天空" then c = Color3.fromRGB(255, 165, 0)
			elseif v == "黄天空" then c = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)
			elseif v == "绿天空" then c = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
			elseif v == "蓝天空" then c = Color3.fromRGB(0, 127, 255)
			elseif v == "靛天空" then c = Color3.fromRGB(75, 0, 130)
			elseif v == "紫天空" then c = Color3.fromRGB(148, 0, 211) end
			a.Color = c
			getgenv().skyLock = a:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Color"):Connect(function()
				a.Color = c
			end)
		end
})

SkinTab:Button({
	Title = T("无头", "Headless"),
	Callback = function()
			local function setHeadInvisible(char)
				local function hideHead()
					local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
					if head then
						head.Transparency = 1
						for _, v in pairs(head:GetChildren()) do
							if v:IsA("Decal") then v:Destroy() end
						end
					end
				end
				hideHead()
				char.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
					if child.Name == "Head" then
						task.wait(0.01)
						child.Transparency = 1
						for _, v in pairs(child:GetChildren()) do
							if v:IsA("Decal") then v:Destroy() end
						end
					end
				end)
			end
			if LocalPlayer.Character then setHeadInvisible(LocalPlayer.Character) end
			LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(setHeadInvisible)
			OhioNotify("已开启 无头")
		end
})

	Title = T("半透明彩虹美化", "Translucent Rainbow"),
	Callback = function()
			_G.RainbowConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
				if LocalPlayer.Character then
					for _, v in pairs(LocalPlayer.Character:GetDescendants()) do
						if v:IsA("BasePart") and v.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
							if v.Name == "Head" then
								v.Transparency = 1
								local face = v:FindFirstChild("face")
								if face then face:Destroy() end
							else
								v.Material = Enum.Material.ForceField
								v.Color = Color3.fromHSV((tick() % 5) / 5, 1, 1)
								v.Transparency = 0.5
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end)
			OhioNotify("已开启 半透明彩虹美化")
		end
})

SkinTab:Section({ Title = T("更多", "More") })

SkinTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_162",
	Title = "炉管",
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(v)
			getgenv().autoAnimEnabled = v
			if not v then return end
			task.spawn(function()
				local lp = LocalPlayer
				local animObject = Instance.new("Animation")
				local currentTrack = nil
				local function getAnimId()
					local char = lp.Character
					local hum = char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
					if hum and hum.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
						return "rbxassetid://698251653"
					else
						return "rbxassetid://72042024"
					end
				end
				local function stopAnim()
					if currentTrack then
						currentTrack:Stop()
						currentTrack:Destroy()
						currentTrack = nil
					end
				end
				local function playAnim()
					if not getgenv().autoAnimEnabled then return end
					local char = lp.Character or lp.CharacterAdded:Wait()
					local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
					local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator") or hum:WaitForChild("Animator")
					animObject.AnimationId = getAnimId()
					if not currentTrack then
						currentTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(animObject)
					end
					currentTrack:Play()
					currentTrack:AdjustSpeed(0.7)
					currentTrack.TimePosition = 0.6
					task.wait(0.1)
					while currentTrack and currentTrack.TimePosition < 0.7 do
						task.wait(0.05)
					end
					if currentTrack then
						currentTrack:Stop()
						currentTrack:Destroy()
						currentTrack = nil
					end
				end
				playAnim()
				local conn = lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(ch)
					ch:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					task.wait(0.01)
					playAnim()
				end)
				while getgenv().autoAnimEnabled do
					task.wait(0.01)
					playAnim()
				end
				conn:Disconnect()
				stopAnim()
			end)
		end
})

SkinTab:Button({
	Title = T("解锁所有物品格", "Unlock All Inventory Slots"),
	Callback = function()
			LocalPlayer:SetAttribute("lockerSlots", 999)
			pcall(function()
				local devv = require(ReplicatedStorage.devv)
				local lockerModule = devv.load("locker")
				lockerModule.getNextTier = function()
					return 5
				end
				lockerModule.getRobuxPrice = function(slots)
					return 0
				end
				local GUILoader = devv.load("GUILoader")
				local backpackUI = GUILoader.Get("Backpack")
				for _, slot in pairs(backpackUI.Holder.Locker.Frame:GetChildren()) do
					if slot:IsA("GuiObject") then
						local locked = slot:FindFirstChild("Locked")
						local unlocked = slot:FindFirstChild("Unlocked")
						if locked then locked.Visible = false end
						if unlocked then unlocked.Visible = true end
					end
				end
				local unlockAll = backpackUI.Holder.Locker.Buttons:FindFirstChild("UnlockAll")
				local pageSel = backpackUI.Holder.Locker.Buttons:FindFirstChild("PageSelector")
				if unlockAll then unlockAll.Visible = false end
				if pageSel then pageSel.Visible = false end
				if lockerModule.PromptPurchase then
					lockerModule.PromptPurchase = function(itemId, infoType)
						game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Signal:InvokeServer("completePurchase", itemId, infoType)
					end
				end
				lockerModule.update()
			end)
			OhioNotify("已解锁所有物品格")
		end
})

SkinTab:Button({
	Title = T("一键套装(无头断腿动画包)", "One-Click Outfit (Headless)"),
	Callback = function()
			local character = LocalPlayer.Character
			local function mh()
				character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
				character:WaitForChild("Animate")
				local Animate = character:WaitForChild("Animate")
				Animate.Disabled = true
				local animTracks = character.Humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()
				for _, track in pairs(animTracks) do
					track:Stop()
				end
				Animate.idle.Animation1.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://616158929"
				Animate.idle.Animation2.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://616160636"
				Animate.walk.WalkAnim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://616168032"
				Animate.run.RunAnim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://616163682"
				Animate.jump.JumpAnim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://656117878"
				Animate.climb.ClimbAnim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://616156119"
				Animate.fall.FallAnim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://707829716"
				character.Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
				Animate.Disabled = false
				local rightUpperLeg = character:WaitForChild("RightUpperLeg")
				local rightLowerLeg = character:WaitForChild("RightLowerLeg")
				local rightFoot = character:WaitForChild("RightFoot")
				local head = character:WaitForChild("Head")
				rightUpperLeg.MeshId = "https://assetdelivery.roblox.com/v1/asset/?id=9598310133"
				rightUpperLeg.TextureID = "https://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=902843398"
				rightLowerLeg.MeshId = "123"
				rightFoot.MeshId = "123"
				head.MeshId = "https://assetdelivery.roblox.com/v1/asset/?id=16673245747"
				for _, child in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("Accessory") then
						child:Destroy()
					end
				end
			end
			mh()
			OhioNotify("已套用套装")
		end
})

SkinTab:Button({
	Title = T("全枪无限子弹", "All Gun Infinite Ammo"),
	Callback = function()
			local function setInfiniteAmmo()
				local itemSystem = require(ReplicatedStorage.devv).load("v3item")
				local inventory = itemSystem.inventory
				for _, item in pairs(inventory.items) do
					if item and item.ammoManager then
						item.ammoManager:setAmmo(9999)
						item.ammoManager:setAmmoOut(9999)
					end
				end
			end
			setInfiniteAmmo()
			task.spawn(function()
				while true do
					pcall(setInfiniteAmmo)
					task.wait(25)
				end
			end)
			OhioNotify("已开启 全枪无限子弹")
		end
})

SkinTab:Button({
	Title = T("全枪射速提升", "All Gun Rate Boost"),
	Callback = function()
			local function increaseFireRate()
				local itemSystem = require(ReplicatedStorage.devv).load("v3item")
				local inventory = itemSystem.inventory
				for _, item in pairs(inventory.items) do
					if item then
						item.fireDebounce = 0.01
						item.reloadTime = 0.1
						if item.ammoManager then
							item.ammoManager.ammo = 9999
						end
					end
				end
			end
			increaseFireRate()
			task.spawn(function()
				while true do
					pcall(increaseFireRate)
					task.wait(30)
				end
			end)
			OhioNotify("已开启 全枪射速提升")
		end
})

SkinTab:Button({
	Title = T("全枪无后坐力", "All Gun No Recoil"),
	Callback = function()
			local function removeRecoil()
				local itemSystem = require(ReplicatedStorage.devv).load("v3item")
				local inventory = itemSystem.inventory
				for _, item in pairs(inventory.items) do
					if item then
						item.recoilAdd = 0
						item.maxRecoil = 0
						item.recoilDiminishFactor = 0
						item.recoilFastDiminishFactor = 0
						item.baseSpread = 0
						item.baseAimSpread = 0
						item.spread = 0
						item.aimSpread = 0
					end
				end
			end
			removeRecoil()
			task.spawn(function()
				while true do
					pcall(removeRecoil)
					task.wait(30)
				end
			end)
			OhioNotify("已开启 全枪无后坐力")
		end
})

SkinTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_166",
	Title = T("人物光环", "Player Halo"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state)
			local char = LocalPlayer.Character
			if not char then return end
			local torso = char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
			if not torso then return end
			for _, child in pairs(torso:GetChildren()) do
				if child.Name == "AuraSafe" then
					child:Destroy()
				end
			end
			if not state then return end
			local container = Instance.new("Folder")
			container.Name = "AuraSafe"
			container.Parent = torso
			local pointLight = Instance.new("PointLight")
			pointLight.Range = 5
			pointLight.Brightness = 3
			pointLight.Color = Color3.fromRGB(129, 47, 47)
			pointLight.Enabled = true
			pointLight.Shadows = false
			pointLight.Parent = container
			local glowAttach = Instance.new("Attachment")
			glowAttach.Name = "Glow"
			glowAttach.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0)
			glowAttach.Parent = container
			local particleGlow = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
			particleGlow.Brightness = 1
			particleGlow.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(129, 47, 47))
			particleGlow.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(1, 1)
			particleGlow.LightEmission = 1
			particleGlow.LockedToPart = true
			particleGlow.Rate = 4
			particleGlow.Size = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 4.25), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 3.375) })
			particleGlow.Speed = NumberRange.new(0, 0)
			particleGlow.Texture = "rbxassetid://1075864321"
			particleGlow.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) })
			particleGlow.Parent = glowAttach
			local attach2Upper = Instance.new("Attachment")
			attach2Upper.Name = "2"
			attach2Upper.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 2.125, 0)
			attach2Upper.Parent = container
			local attach2Lower = Instance.new("Attachment")
			attach2Lower.Name = "2"
			attach2Lower.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -3, 0)
			attach2Lower.Parent = container
			local function makeBeam(tex, width, texLen)
				local b = Instance.new("Beam")
				b.Attachment0 = attach2Lower
				b.Attachment1 = attach2Upper
				b.Brightness = 1
				b.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(129, 47, 47))
				b.Enabled = true
				b.FaceCamera = true
				b.LightEmission = 1
				b.Texture = tex
				b.TextureLength = texLen
				b.TextureSpeed = 0
				b.Width0 = width
				b.Width1 = width
				b.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) })
				b.Parent = container
			end
			makeBeam("rbxassetid://7673945506", 6, 0.3)
			makeBeam("rbxassetid://6045867277", 5, 0.1)
			makeBeam("rbxassetid://1849531275", 6, 0.1)
			local topSymbolAttach = Instance.new("Attachment")
			topSymbolAttach.Name = "1TopSymbol"
			topSymbolAttach.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 5.75, 1) * CFrame.fromMatrix(Vector3.zero, Vector3.new(0, 0, -1), Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0), Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))
			topSymbolAttach.Parent = container
			local bottomSymbolAttach = Instance.new("Attachment")
			bottomSymbolAttach.Name = "1BottomSymbol"
			bottomSymbolAttach.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -1.25, 1) * CFrame.fromMatrix(Vector3.zero, Vector3.new(0, 0, -1), Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0), Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))
			bottomSymbolAttach.Parent = container
			local beamSymbol = Instance.new("Beam")
			beamSymbol.Attachment0 = topSymbolAttach
			beamSymbol.Attachment1 = bottomSymbolAttach
			beamSymbol.Brightness = 1
			beamSymbol.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(129, 47, 47))
			beamSymbol.CurveSize0 = 0.5
			beamSymbol.CurveSize1 = 0.5
			beamSymbol.Enabled = true
			beamSymbol.Segments = 100
			beamSymbol.Texture = "rbxassetid://8285797183"
			beamSymbol.TextureLength = 1
			beamSymbol.TextureSpeed = 0
			beamSymbol.Width0 = 7
			beamSymbol.Width1 = 7
			beamSymbol.Parent = container
		end
})

SkinTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_173",
	Title = T("自动开启选中箱子", "Auto Open Selected Case"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state)
			getgenv().autoOpenSelected = state
			if state then
				task.spawn(function()
					local st = require(ReplicatedStorage.devv).load("state")
					while getgenv().autoOpenSelected do
						local ownedCount = st.data.ownedCases[getgenv().selectedOpenCase] or 0
						if ownedCount > 0 then
							Signal.InvokeServer("openCase", getgenv().selectedOpenCase)
							task.wait(0.5)
						else
							task.wait(1)
						end
					end
				end)
			end
		end
})

SkinTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_174",
	Title = T("自动开全部箱子", "Auto Open All Cases"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state)
			getgenv().autoOpenAll = state
			if state then
				task.spawn(function()
					local st = require(ReplicatedStorage.devv).load("state")
					while getgenv().autoOpenAll do
						for caseName, ownedCount in pairs(st.data.ownedCases or {}) do
							if ownedCount > 0 and not getgenv().autoOpenSelected then
								Signal.InvokeServer("openCase", caseName)
								task.wait(0.3)
							end
						end
						task.wait(1)
					end
				end)
			end
		end
})

MiscTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_175",
	Title = T("反管理员", "Anti Admin"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state)
			if state then
				for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
					if player:GetAttribute("clanId") == "6557c057b60ffcc7226f532c" then
						LocalPlayer:Kick("[Anti Admin] Admin UserName = " .. player.Name)
						break
					end
				end
			end
		end
})

MiscTab:Button({
	Title = T("反吸人", "Anti Pull"),
	Callback = function()
			local plr = LocalPlayer
			local function ForceStandFromSeat()
				local character = plr.Character
				if not character then return end
				local hum = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				local hrp = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not hum or not hrp or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
				if hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated then
					hum.Sit = false
					hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
				end
				for _, seat in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
					if seat:IsA("Seat") and seat.Occupant == hum then
						seat.Occupant = nil
					end
				end
			end
			ForceStandFromSeat()
			OhioNotify("反吸人 已执行")
		end
})

MiscTab:Section({ Title = T("自动防护", "Auto Protection") })

MiscTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_183",
	Title = T("自动使用一级护甲", "Auto Use Armor"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(v)
			getgenv().autoArmorEnabled = v
			if not v then return end
			task.spawn(function()
				local lp = LocalPlayer
				local sig = require(ReplicatedStorage.devv).load("Signal")
				local inv = require(ReplicatedStorage.devv).load("v3item").inventory.items
				local targetArmorName = "Light Vest"
				local armorCF = CFrame.new(660.421753, 3.41803741, -684.527832)
				local function f()
					if not getgenv().autoArmorEnabled then return end
					local item = nil
					for _, i in inv do
						if i.name == targetArmorName then
							item = i
							break
						end
					end
					if not item then
						local root = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						if root then
							local oldCf = root.CFrame
							local dist = (root.Position - armorCF.Position).Magnitude
							if dist > 8 then
								root.CFrame = armorCF
								task.wait(0.04)
							end
							sig.InvokeServer("attemptPurchase", targetArmorName)
							task.wait(0.05)
							if dist > 8 then
								root.CFrame = oldCf
							end
						end
					end
					for _, i in inv do
						if i.name == targetArmorName then
							local cur = lp:GetAttribute("armor") or 0
							if cur <= 0 then
								sig.FireServer("equip", i.guid)
								sig.FireServer("useConsumable", i.guid)
								sig.FireServer("removeItem", i.guid)
							end
							break
						end
					end
				end
				f()
				local conn = lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(ch)
					ch:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					task.wait(0.01)
					f()
				end)
				while getgenv().autoArmorEnabled do
					task.wait(0.01)
					f()
				end
				conn:Disconnect()
			end)
		end
})

MiscTab:Slider({
	Flag = "wow_184",
	Title = T("自动回血触发血量设置", "Auto Heal Trigger HP"),
	Value = { Min = 1, Max = 99, Default = 50 },
	Step = 1,

			getgenv()._BandageHP = Value
		end
})

MiscTab:Toggle({
	Flag = "wow_185",
	Title = T("自动使用绷带回血", "Auto Use Bandage"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(Value)
			if getgenv().healThread then
				getgenv().healThread:Disconnect()
				getgenv().healThread = nil
			end
			if Value then
				local shopCF = CFrame.new(1193.40015, 41.3713951, -748.65741)
				getgenv().healThread = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
					task.wait(0.2)
					local char = LocalPlayer.Character
					local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
					if not hum or hum.Health > getgenv()._BandageHP then return end
					local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if not root then return end
					local dist = (root.Position - shopCF.Position).Magnitude
					local oldCf = root.CFrame
					if dist > 8 then
						root.CFrame = shopCF
						task.wait(0.04)
					end
					require(ReplicatedStorage.devv).load("Signal").InvokeServer("attemptPurchase", "Bandage")
					task.wait(0.05)
					if dist > 8 then
						root.CFrame = oldCf
					end
					local inv = require(ReplicatedStorage.devv).load("v3item").inventory
					for _, v in next, inv.items do
						if v.name == "Bandage" then
							if hum.Health < hum.MaxHealth then
								require(ReplicatedStorage.devv).load("Signal").FireServer("equip", v.guid)
								require(ReplicatedStorage.devv).load("Signal").FireServer("useConsumable", v.guid)
								require(ReplicatedStorage.devv).load("Signal").FireServer("removeItem", v.guid)
							end
							break
						end
					end
				end)
			end
		end
})

	Value = false,
	Callback = function(v)
			getgenv().AutoEscape = v
			if getgenv().AutoEscapeConn then
				getgenv().AutoEscapeConn:Disconnect()
				getgenv().AutoEscapeConn = nil
			end
			if v then
				getgenv().AutoEscapeConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
					task.wait(0.2)
					local char = LocalPlayer.Character
					if not char then return end
					local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
					if not hum then return end
					if hum.Health <= getgenv()._EscapeHP then
						char:PivotTo(CFrame.new(1219, 77, -1045))
					end
				end)
			end
		end
})

						return false
					end
					return getmetatable(GrabHandler).__index.CheckValid(p28, p29, p30)
				end

					if p55 == LocalPlayer then

					end
					return getmetatable(GrabHandler).__index.Grab(p54, p55)
				end
			end)
			OhioNotify("已开启 不允许被抓取")
		end

	Title = T("绕过管理员命令", "Bypass Admin Commands"),
	Callback = function()
			pcall(function()
				local a = require(ReplicatedStorage.CmdrClient)
				local function buildText(...)
					local args = { ... }
					for i = 1, #args do
						args[i] = tostring(args[i])
					end
					return table.concat(args, " ")
				end
				a.RemoteEvent.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(p26, ...)
					local args = { ... }
					StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", { Title = "已拦截命令", Text = buildText(p26, unpack(args)) })
				end)
			end)
			OhioNotify("已开启 绕过管理员命令")
		end
})

MiscTab:Button({
	Title = T("绕过高级表情解锁", "Bypass Emote Unlock"),
	Callback = function()
			local emotesGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") and LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("Emotes")
			if emotesGui and emotesGui:FindFirstChild("Frame") and emotesGui.Frame:FindFirstChild("ScrollingFrame") then
				for _, v in pairs(emotesGui.Frame.ScrollingFrame:GetDescendants()) do
					if v.Name == "Locked" then
						v.Visible = false
					end
				end
			end
			OhioNotify("已开启 绕过高级表情解锁")
		end
})

MiscTab:Button({
	Title = T("绕过移动经销商解锁", "Bypass Mobile Dealer"),
	Callback = function()
			local function hookFreePurchase(remote)
				if not remote or not remote.InvokeServer then return end
				local oldInvoke = remote.InvokeServer
				remote.InvokeServer = function(self, ...)
					if self == remote then
						return oldInvoke(self, select(1, ...), false, select(3, ...))
					end
					return oldInvoke(self, ...)
				end
			end
			pcall(function()
				hookFreePurchase(ReplicatedStorage.devv.remoteStorage.attemptPurchase)
			end)
			pcall(function()
				hookFreePurchase(ReplicatedStorage.devv.remoteStorage.attemptPurchaseAmmo)
			end)
			LocalPlayer:SetAttribute("mobileDealer", true)
			pcall(function()
				local mobileDealer = require(ReplicatedStorage.devv.shared.Indicies.mobileDealer)
				for cat, items in pairs(mobileDealer) do
					for _, item in ipairs(items) do
						item.stock = 12e12
					end
				end
				table.insert(mobileDealer.Gun, { itemName = "Acid Gun", stock = 12e12 })
			end)
			OhioNotify("已开启 绕过移动经销商解锁")
		end
})

task.spawn(function()
		while true do
			if TrackConfig.userName and TrackConfig.userName ~= "" then
				pcall(function()
					statusLabel:SetTitle(getStatusText())
				end)
			else
				pcall(function()
					statusLabel:SetTitle("未设置目标")
				end)
			end
			task.wait(1)
		end
	end)

task.spawn(function()
		while true do
			local fps = math.floor(workspace:GetRealPhysicsFPS())
			local ping = 0
			pcall(function()
				local pingStr = game:GetService("Stats").Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValueString()
				ping = math.floor(tonumber(pingStr:match("%d+")) or 0)
			end)
			fpsL:SetTitle("帧率: " .. tostring(fps))
			pingL:SetTitle("延迟: " .. tostring(ping) .. "ms")
			if stateInfo then
				moneyL:SetTitle("当前钱数: " .. tostring(stateInfo.money or 0))
				totalMoneyL:SetTitle("历史总钱数: " .. tostring(stateInfo.totalMoneyEarned or 0))
				robuxL:SetTitle("当前Robux花费: " .. tostring(stateInfo.robuxSpent or 0))
				atmsL:SetTitle("ATM抢劫金钱: " .. tostring(stateInfo.atmsRobbed or 0))
				gemsL:SetTitle("宝石抢劫金钱: " .. tostring(stateInfo.gemsRobbed or 0))
				streakL:SetTitle("历史最高连续登陆: " .. tostring(stateInfo.topLoginStreak or 0))
				jobL:SetTitle("工作赚钱金钱: " .. tostring(stateInfo.jobEarnings or 0))
			end
			task.wait(1)
		end
	end)

SettingsTab:Section({ Title = T("配置管理", "Config Manager") })

function WOWConfigList()
	local list = {}
	if ConfigManager then
		list = ConfigManager:AllConfigs()
	end
	local found = false
	for _, v in ipairs(list) do
		if v == WOWConfigName then found = true break end
	end
	if not found then table.insert(list, 1, WOWConfigName) end
	return list
end

ConfigListDropdown = SettingsTab:Dropdown({
	Title = T("选择配置", "Select Config"),
	Values = WOWConfigList(),
	Value = WOWConfigName,
	Callback = function(v)
		WOWConfigName = v
	end
})

SettingsTab:Button({
	Title = T("刷新配置列表", "Refresh Config List"),
	Callback = function()
		ConfigListDropdown:Refresh(WOWConfigList())
		OhioNotify(T("配置列表已刷新", "Config list refreshed"))
	end
})

SettingsTab:Input({
	Title = T("配置名称", "Config Name"),
	Placeholder = "default",
	Callback = function(v)
		if v and v ~= "" then
			WOWConfigName = v
		end
	end
})

SettingsTab:Toggle({
	Title = T("下次自动使用配置", "Auto Load Next Time"),
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state)
		if not ConfigManager then return end
		if not WOWConfig then
			WOWConfig = ConfigManager:CreateConfig(WOWConfigName)
		end
		WOWConfig:SetAutoLoad(state)
		WOWConfig:Save()
		OhioNotify(state and T("已开启自动加载", "Auto load on") or T("已关闭自动加载", "Auto load off"))
	end
})

SettingsTab:Button({
	Title = T("保存当前配置", "Save Current Config"),
	Callback = function()
		if not ConfigManager then
			OhioNotify(T("配置系统不可用", "Config system unavailable"))
			return
		end
		WOWConfig = ConfigManager:CreateConfig(WOWConfigName)
		WOWConfig:Save()
		ConfigListDropdown:Refresh(WOWConfigList())
		OhioNotify(T("配置已保存: ", "Config saved: ") .. WOWConfigName)
	end
})

SettingsTab:Button({
	Title = T("使用配置", "Load Config"),
	Callback = function()
		if not ConfigManager then
			OhioNotify(T("配置系统不可用", "Config system unavailable"))
			return
		end
		WOWConfig = ConfigManager:CreateConfig(WOWConfigName)
		local ok, err = WOWConfig:Load()
		if ok then
			OhioNotify(T("配置已加载: ", "Config loaded: ") .. WOWConfigName)
		else
			OhioNotify(T("配置加载失败: ", "Config load failed: ") .. tostring(err))
		end
	end
})

Notify(T("WOWv3已经加载", "WOWv3 loaded"), 3)
